public class ConvertMSsqlTOoracle
{
	private ReaderLog myReaderLog;

	private static readonly string[] strSQLvartypes = new string[16]
	{
		"nvarchar", "\"nvarchar\"", "int", "\"int\"", "float", "\"float\"", "smalldatetime", "\"smalldatetime\"", "ntext", "\"ntext\"",
		"bit", "\"bit\"", "image", "\"image\"", "GO", null
	};

	private static readonly string[] strORACLEvartypes = new string[16]
	{
		"VARCHAR2", "VARCHAR2", "NUMBER (10, 0)", "NUMBER (10, 0)", "FLOAT", "FLOAT", "DATE", "DATE", "CLOB", "CLOB",
		"NUMBER (2, 0)", "NUMBER (2, 0)", "BLOB", "BLOB", "/", null
	};

	private const int strIgnoreNumRows = 9;

	private const int strIgnoreNumCols = 4;

	private static readonly string[,] strToIgnore = new string[9, 4]
	{
		{ "ON", "[PRIMARY]", null, null },
		{ "COLLATE", "SQL_Latin1_General_CP1_CI_AS", null, null },
		{ "TEXTIMAGE_ON", "[PRIMARY]", null, null },
		{ "SET", "QUOTED_IDENTIFIER", "ON", null },
		{ "SET", "QUOTED_IDENTIFIER", "OFF", null },
		{ "SET", "ANSI_NULLS", "ON", null },
		{ "CLUSTERED", null, null, null },
		{ "WITH", "NOCHECK", null, null },
		{ null, null, null, null }
	};

	public ConvertMSsqlTOoracle()
	{
		myReaderLog = new ReaderLog("", deleteOld: false, ReaderLog.LogType.NoLog);
	}

	public bool SetErrorLog(ReaderLog.LogType logType, bool ClearOldLog, string name)
	{
		myReaderLog.SetErrorLog(logType, ClearOldLog, name);
		return true;
	}

	public void CloseErrorLog()
	{
		if (myReaderLog != null)
		{
			myReaderLog.CloseLog();
			myReaderLog = null;
		}
	}

	private int GetTokensToIgnore(Tokens tokens, int index)
	{
		for (int i = 0; i < 9; i++)
		{
			int num = index;
			int num2 = 0;
			while (num2 < 4)
			{
				string text = "";
				if (num < tokens.Length)
				{
					text = tokens.GetAt(num);
					if (num2 != 0 && strToIgnore[i, num2] == null)
					{
						return num2;
					}
					if (text == "" || strToIgnore[i, num2] == null || string.Compare(text, strToIgnore[i, num2], ignoreCase: true) != 0)
					{
						break;
					}
					num2++;
					num++;
					continue;
				}
				if (num2 == 0 || strToIgnore[i, num2] != null)
				{
					break;
				}
				return num2;
			}
		}
		return 0;
	}

	public void ProcessFile(string filename)
	{
		InputFileReader inputFileReader = new InputFileReader(myReaderLog);
		bool flag = false;
		bool flag2 = false;
		inputFileReader.OpenFile(filename);
		while (inputFileReader.GetALine() != -1)
		{
			string text = "";
			Tokens tokens = new Tokens(inputFileReader.CurrentLineCopy, Tokens.delimiterNormal);
			int num = 0;
			while (num < tokens.Length)
			{
				if (flag2)
				{
					flag2 = false;
					num++;
					continue;
				}
				int num2 = 0;
				string at = tokens.GetAt(num);
				if ((num2 = GetTokensToIgnore(tokens, num)) > 0)
				{
					num += num2;
					continue;
				}
				num++;
				at = at.Replace("[", "\"");
				at = at.Replace("]", "\"");
				if (at.IndexOf(".") != -1)
				{
					bool flag3 = at.IndexOf("\"") == -1;
					Tokens tokens2 = new Tokens(at, Tokens.delimiterDot);
					at = ((!flag3) ? "" : "\"");
					for (int i = 0; i < tokens2.Length; i++)
					{
						if (string.Compare(tokens2.GetAt(i), "dbo", ignoreCase: true) != 0 && string.Compare(tokens2.GetAt(i), "\"dbo\"", ignoreCase: true) != 0)
						{
							at += tokens2.GetAt(i);
							if (flag3)
							{
								at += "\"";
							}
							if (i < tokens2.Length - 1)
							{
								at = ((!flag3) ? (at + ".") : (at + ".\""));
							}
						}
					}
				}
				int num3 = UtilityFuncs.FindStringInList(at, strSQLvartypes, ignoreCase: true, MatchOnlyBeginningOfString: false);
				if (num3 != -1)
				{
					at = strORACLEvartypes[num3];
				}
				text = text + " " + at;
			}
			if (text.Length != 0 && (!(text == "/") || !flag))
			{
				flag = text == "/";
				myReaderLog.AddMessageToLog(text);
			}
		}
		inputFileReader.CloseFile();
	}
}

	public DBwriter(IDatabase database, ReaderLog readerLog, bool ValidationMode)
	{
		//IL_0108: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
		//IL_0112: Expected O, but got Unknown
		//IL_0154: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
		//IL_015e: Expected O, but got Unknown
		//IL_0187: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
		//IL_0191: Expected O, but got Unknown
		//IL_0192: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
		//IL_019c: Expected O, but got Unknown
		//IL_01ba: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
		//IL_01c4: Expected O, but got Unknown
		//IL_01ff: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
		//IL_0209: Expected O, but got Unknown
		//IL_0334: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
		//IL_0346: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
		//IL_0350: Expected O, but got Unknown
		//IL_0371: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
		//IL_0378: Expected O, but got Unknown
		//IL_03a7: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
		//IL_03ae: Expected O, but got Unknown
		m_ReaderLog = readerLog;
		m_stringPassword = "";
		m_stringDatabankType = "";
		myNameIdentifierAdded = false;
		myAliasIdentifierAdded = false;
		bValidationMode = ValidationMode;
		if (myDatabase == null)
		{
			myDatabase = (Database)database;
		}
		IDatabankCollection databanks = default(IDatabankCollection);
		try
		{
			myDatabase.EnumerateDatabanks(ref databanks);
		}
		catch (Exception ex)
		{
			m_ReaderLog.AddMessageToLog("Failed to get databanks. Possible cause initializing database " + myDatabase.DataSource + " failed\n" + ex.Message);
			return;
		}
		myUOMDataGateway = (IUOMDataGateway)new UOMDataGateway();
		myUOMDataGateway.Databanks = databanks;
		myUOMDataManager = ((ISupportsUOMData)myDatabase).UOMDataManager;
		myUOMData = (IUOMData)((ISupportsUOMData)myDatabase).UOMDataManager;
		myMolDataGateway = (IMolDataGateway)new MolDataGateway();
		myMolDataGateway.Databanks = databanks;
		myMolDataManager = ((ISupportsMolData)myDatabase).MolDataManager;
		myCompGateway = (ICompoundPropertyGateway)new CompoundPropertyGateway();
		myCompGateway.Databanks = databanks;
		mySdm = ((ISupportsSundryData)myDatabase).SundryDataManager;
		myChangeControlManager = ((ISupportsChangeControl)myDatabase).ChangeControlManager;
		myChangeControlManager.ChangeSubmitted += new ChangeEventHandlerDelegate(ChangeSubmitted);
		m_RVEcurrentPropList = new Hashtable();
		m_EquationList = new SortedList();
		myCompoundManager = myDatabase.CompoundManager;
		myPropertyManager = myDatabase.PropertyManager;
		myCitationManager = myDatabase.CitationManager;
		if (myCitationManager != null)
		{
			myCitationManager.GetCitation("DipprCitation_-1", ref cosmoSolCitation);
		}
		cosmoSolProperties = new ArrayList();
		cosmoSolProperties.Add("CSACVL");
		cosmoSolProperties.Add("SGPRF1");
		cosmoSolProperties.Add("SGPRF2");
		cosmoSolProperties.Add("SGPRF3");
		cosmoSolProperties.Add("SGPRF4");
		cosmoSolProperties.Add("SGPRF5");
		ICollection collection = null;
		myPropertyManager.GetAllProperties((IDatabank)null, false, ref collection);
		m_RVEallPropsInDbase = new SortedList();
		if (collection != null)
		{
			Array array = Array.CreateInstance(typeof(PropertyObject), collection.Count);
			collection.CopyTo(array, 0);
			for (int i = 0; i < array.Length; i++)
			{
				m_RVEallPropsInDbase.Add(((PropertyObject)array.GetValue(i)).Name, (object)(PropertyObject)array.GetValue(i));
			}
			foreach (IProperty item in collection)
			{
				IProperty val = item;
				if (!m_EquationList.Contains(val.Name))
				{
					EquationObject value = new EquationObject(val.Name, val.ElementCount, val.Description, (byte[])null, "");
					m_EquationList.Add(val.Name, value);
				}
			}
		}
		myPropertyValueManager = myDatabase.PropertyValueManager;
		myReactionManager = myDatabase.ReactionManager;
	}

	public DBwriter(string DBalias, string DBpath, string initialCatalog, string username, string password, DatabaseProviderType dbpType, ReaderLog readerLog, bool ValidationMode)
	{
		//IL_0108: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
		//IL_0165: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
		//IL_016f: Expected O, but got Unknown
		//IL_0198: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
		//IL_01a2: Expected O, but got Unknown
		//IL_01a3: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
		//IL_01ad: Expected O, but got Unknown
		//IL_01cb: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
		//IL_01d5: Expected O, but got Unknown
		//IL_0210: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
		//IL_021a: Expected O, but got Unknown
		//IL_02a5: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
		//IL_02b7: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
		//IL_02c1: Expected O, but got Unknown
		//IL_02e2: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
		//IL_02e9: Expected O, but got Unknown
		//IL_0318: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
		//IL_031f: Expected O, but got Unknown
		m_ReaderLog = readerLog;

		m_stringDatabankType = "";

		myAliasIdentifierAdded = false;

			InitializeDatabase(DBalias, DBpath, initialCatalog, username, password, dbpType);
		}
		catch
		{
			m_ReaderLog.AddMessageToLog("Failed to initialize database " + DBpath);
			return;
		}
		IDatabankCollection databanks = default(IDatabankCollection);

		{
			myDatabase.EnumerateDatabanks(ref databanks);
		}
		catch (Exception ex)
		{
			m_ReaderLog.AddMessageToLog("Failed to get databanks. Possible cause initializing database " + DBpath + " failed\n" + ex.Message);

		ICollection collection = null;

		m_RVEallPropsInDbase = new SortedList();
		if (collection != null)
		{
			Array array = Array.CreateInstance(typeof(PropertyObject), collection.Count);
			collection.CopyTo(array, 0);
			for (int i = 0; i < array.Length; i++)
			{

		myCitationManager = myDatabase.CitationManager;
		if (myCitationManager != null)

			myCitationManager.GetCitation("DipprCitation_-1", ref cosmoSolCitation);

		cosmoSolProperties.Add("SGPRF1");

		cosmoSolProperties.Add("SGPRF3");
		cosmoSolProperties.Add("SGPRF4");
		cosmoSolProperties.Add("SGPRF5");
	}

	private void initializeSqlServer(string DBalias, string DBpath, string initialCatalog, string username, string password)
	{
		//IL_0008: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
		//IL_000e: Expected O, but got Unknown
		if (myDatabase == null)
		{
			SqlServerDatabase val = new SqlServerDatabase();
			val.Connect(DBalias, DBpath, username, password, initialCatalog);
			myDatabase = (Database)(object)val;
		}
	}

	private void InitializeDatabase(string dbAlias, string DBpath, string initialCatalog, string username, string password, DatabaseProviderType dbpType)
	{
		//IL_0000: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
		if ((int)dbpType == 0)
		{
			initializeSqlServer(dbAlias, DBpath, initialCatalog, username, password);
		}
	}

	protected void ChangeSubmitted(IChange change)
	{
		try
		{
			change.Approve();
		}
		catch
		{
			m_ReaderLog.AddMessageToLog("Change approval failed");
		}
	}

	public void ResetCurrentPropList()
	{
		if (bValidationMode)
		{
			m_RVEcurrentPropList.Clear();
		}
	}

	public void AddToTheCurrentPropList(string PropName)
	{
		if (bValidationMode)
		{
			m_RVEcurrentPropList.Add(PropName, "1");
		}
	}

	public void ReportAnyExtraPropertiesInDBase(string id, string idType)
	{
		if (!bValidationMode)
		{
			return;
		}
		bool flag = false;
		int count = m_RVEallPropsInDbase.Count;
		IPropertyValue val = default(IPropertyValue);
		for (int i = 0; i < count; i++)
		{
			string text = (string)m_RVEallPropsInDbase.GetKey(i);
			if (text != "NAME" && text != "ALIAS" && m_RVEcurrentPropList[text] == null)
			{
				myPropertyValueManager.GetUnaryPropertyValue(myDatabank, id, idType, text, ref val);
				if (val != null)
				{
					Console.WriteLine("ReportAnyExtraPropertiesInDBase !ERROR!: {0} found in {1} for {2} {3}", m_RVEallPropsInDbase[i], myDatabank.Name, idType, id);
					flag = true;
				}
			}
		}
		if (!flag)
		{
			Console.WriteLine("ReportAnyExtraPropertiesInDBase validated. No extra properties in DBase beyond ones in Input file PVAL for {0} {1} ", idType, id);
		}
	}

	public int SetDatabankAsDefault(IDatabank dbank)
	{
		if (dbank == null)
		{
			return -1;
		}
		myDatabank = dbank;
		return 0;
	}

	public IDatabankCollection GetDatabankList()
	{
		IDatabankCollection val = default(IDatabankCollection);
		myDatabase.EnumerateDatabanks(ref val);
		if (val != null && ((ICollection)val).Count > 0)
		{
			return val;
		}
		return null;
	}

	public void CreateNewDBank(string dbankType, string dbankPasswordOrName)
	{
		if (myDatabase == null)
		{
			return;
		}
		IDatabankCollection val = default(IDatabankCollection);
		myDatabase.EnumerateDatabanks(ref val);
		if (val != null && ((ICollection)val).Count > 0)
		{
			for (int i = 0; i < ((ICollection)val).Count; i++)
			{
				if (val[(object)i] != null && string.Compare(val[(object)i].Name, 0, dbankPasswordOrName, 0, 8, ignoreCase: true) == 0)
				{
					myDatabank = val[(object)i];
					return;
				}
			}
		}
		if (bValidationMode)
		{
			Console.WriteLine("CreateNewDBank !ERROR!: {0}, {1} does not exist in database", dbankType, dbankPasswordOrName);
			return;
		}
		IDatabank baseDatabank = GetBaseDatabank(dbankPasswordOrName);
		if (dbankPasswordOrName.ToUpperInvariant().CompareTo("INORGANI") == 0)
		{
			dbankPasswordOrName = "INORGANIC";
		}
		try
		{
			myDatabank = myDatabase.AddDatabank(dbankPasswordOrName, baseDatabank, "Aspen Plus databank " + dbankPasswordOrName);
		}
		catch (Exception ex)
		{
			m_ReaderLog.AddMessageToLog(string.Format("Fatal error has occurred while adding databank {0}. \n" + ex.Message, dbankPasswordOrName));
			throw new GatewayException(string.Format("Fatal error has occurred while adding databank {0}. \n" + ex.Message, dbankPasswordOrName));
		}
	}

	private IDatabank GetBaseDatabank(string dbankName)
	{
		if (dbankName == "")
		{
			return null;
		}
		if (DFMSconsts.BaseDatabankArray != null)
		{
			int num = UtilityFuncs.FindStringInList(dbankName, DFMSconsts.BaseDatabankArray, ignoreCase: true, MatchOnlyBeginningOfString: false);
			if (num < 1)
			{
				return null;
			}
			if (DFMSconsts.BaseDatabankArray[num - 1] == "")
			{
				return null;
			}
			return GetOldDBank(DFMSconsts.BaseDatabankArray[num - 1]);
		}
		return null;
	}

	public IDatabank GetOldDBank(string dbankPassword)
	{
		if (myDatabase != null)
		{
			IDatabankCollection val = default(IDatabankCollection);
			myDatabase.EnumerateDatabanks(ref val);
			if (val != null && ((ICollection)val).Count > 0)
			{
				if (dbankPassword.ToUpperInvariant().CompareTo("INORGANI") == 0)
				{
					for (int i = 0; i < ((ICollection)val).Count; i++)
					{
						if (val[(object)i] != null && string.Compare(val[(object)i].Name, 0, dbankPassword, 0, 8, ignoreCase: true) == 0)
						{
							return val[(object)i];
						}
					}
				}
				else
				{
					for (int j = 0; j < ((ICollection)val).Count; j++)
					{
						if (val[(object)j] != null && string.Compare(val[(object)j].Name, 0, dbankPassword, 0, 9, ignoreCase: true) == 0)
						{
							return val[(object)j];
						}
					}
				}
			}
		}
		return null;
	}

	public void AddIdentifier(string identifierName, string identifierDescription, bool allowMultiples)
	{
		if (identifierDescription == null)
		{
			identifierDescription = "Identifier " + identifierName;
		}
		if (!FindIdentifier(identifierName, out var _))
		{
			IIdentifier val = default(IIdentifier);
			myCompoundManager.AddIdentifier(identifierName, identifierDescription, allowMultiples, ref val);
		}
	}

	private void AddNameAndAliasProperties()
	{
		IdentifierObject identObj;
		if (!myNameIdentifierAdded)
		{
			if (!FindIdentifier("NAME", out identObj))
			{
				IIdentifier val = default(IIdentifier);
				myCompoundManager.AddIdentifier("NAME", "Compound Name", false, ref val);
				myNameIdentifierAdded = true;
			}
			else
			{
				myNameIdentifierAdded = true;
			}
		}
		if (!myAliasIdentifierAdded)
		{
			if (!FindIdentifier("ALIAS", out identObj))
			{
				IIdentifier val2 = default(IIdentifier);
				myCompoundManager.AddIdentifier("ALIAS", "Compound Formula or Alias", false, ref val2);
				myAliasIdentifierAdded = true;
			}
			else
			{
				myAliasIdentifierAdded = true;
			}
		}
	}

	public bool AddPureCompound(string CompName, string CompAlias)
	{
		if (CompAlias == "")
		{
			m_ReaderLog.AddMessageToLog($"ERROR: Compound Alias missing for {CompName}");
			return false;
		}
		if (CompName == "")
		{
			m_ReaderLog.AddMessageToLog($"ERROR: Compound Name missing for {CompAlias}");
			return false;
		}
		try
		{
			IIdentifier val = default(IIdentifier);
			myCompoundManager.GetIdentifier("NAME", ref val);
			IIdentifier val2 = default(IIdentifier);
			myCompoundManager.GetIdentifier("ALIAS", ref val2);
			ICompound val3 = default(ICompound);
			myCompoundManager.GetCompound(CompName, val, ref val3);
			ICompound val4 = default(ICompound);
			myCompoundManager.GetCompound(CompAlias, val2, ref val4);
			IIdentifierValue val6 = default(IIdentifierValue);
			if (val3 == null && val4 == null)
			{
				ICompound val5 = default(ICompound);
				myCompoundManager.AddCompound(CompName, val, ref val5);
				myCompoundManager.AddIdentifierValue(val5, val2, new string[1] { CompAlias }, ref val6);
				return true;
			}
			if (val3 != null && val4 != null)
			{
				if (val3.Id == val4.Id)
				{
					return true;
				}
				m_ReaderLog.AddMessageToLog($"Fatal error has occurred while adding Compound. NAME '{CompName}' and ALIAS '{CompAlias}' already exist but for different compounds");
				throw new GatewayException($"Fatal error has occurred while adding Compound. NAME '{CompName}' and ALIAS '{CompAlias}' already exist but for different compounds");
			}
			if (val3 != null && val4 == null)
			{
				myCompoundManager.AddIdentifierValue(val3, val2, new string[1] { CompAlias }, ref val6);
				return true;
			}
			if (val3 == null && val4 != null)
			{
				myCompoundManager.AddIdentifierValue(val4, val, new string[1] { CompName }, ref val6);
				return true;
			}
			return false;
		}
		catch (GatewayException ex)
		{
			m_ReaderLog.AddMessageToLog(string.Format("\nFatal error has occurred while trying to add pure compound with Name :{0} and Alias :{1}.\n" + ex.Message, CompName, CompAlias));
			throw ex;
		}
		catch (Exception ex2)
		{
			m_ReaderLog.AddMessageToLog(string.Format("\nFatal error has occurred while trying to add pure compound with Name :{0} and Alias :{1}.\n" + ex2.Message, CompName, CompAlias));
			throw ex2;
		}
	}

	public bool FindIdentifier(string identifierName, out IdentifierObject identObj)
	{
		//IL_0010: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
		//IL_0016: Expected O, but got Unknown
		IIdentifier val = default(IIdentifier);
		myCompoundManager.GetIdentifier(identifierName, ref val);
		identObj = (IdentifierObject)val;
		return identObj != null;
	}

	public bool FindProperty(string propName, out PropertyObject propObj)
	{
		//IL_0010: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
		//IL_0016: Expected O, but got Unknown
		IProperty val = default(IProperty);
		myPropertyManager.GetProperty(propName, ref val);
		propObj = (PropertyObject)val;
		return propObj != null;
	}

	private bool FindEquation(string PropName, out EquationObject equation)
	{
		//IL_001d: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
		//IL_0023: Expected O, but got Unknown
		//IL_0034: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
		//IL_003a: Expected O, but got Unknown
		equation = null;
		string text = PropName.ToUpperInvariant();
		string empty = string.Empty;
		try
		{
			equation = (EquationObject)m_EquationList[text];
			if (equation == null)
			{
				equation = (EquationObject)mySdm.GetItem(text);
				if (equation == null)
				{
					return false;
				}
				m_EquationList.Add(text, equation);
			}
			return true;
		}
		catch (GatewayException ex)
		{
			empty = ex.Message;
		}
		catch (Exception ex2)
		{
			empty = ex2.Message;
		}
		if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(empty))
		{
			empty = "An error occurs for processing data for " + text + ": " + empty;
			m_ReaderLog.AddMessageToLog(empty);
		}
		if (equation == null)
		{
			return false;
		}
		return true;
	}

	private bool FindEquationProperty(string PropName, out int NumElemsOfProp)
	{
		NumElemsOfProp = -1;
		EquationObject equation = null;
		if (!FindEquation(PropName, out equation))
		{
			return false;
		}
		NumElemsOfProp = equation.NumParameters;
		return true;
	}

	public void AddBinaryComponent(string CompName1, string CompAlias1, string CompName2, string CompAlias2)
	{
	}

	public void AddProperty(string propName, string propDescription, bool propIsGlobal, PropertyDataType propDataType, InteractionType propInteractionType, CompoundType propCompoundType, int propNumElems, ArrayList symmetryList, ArrayList labelList, ArrayList unitTypes)
	{
		//IL_0104: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
		//IL_0106: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
		//IL_0108: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
		//IL_0156: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
		//IL_0159: Invalid comparison between Unknown and I4
		//IL_016a: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
		//IL_0171: Expected O, but got Unknown
		//IL_00ea: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
		//IL_00f1: Expected O, but got Unknown
		if (!FindProperty(propName, out var propObj))
		{
			if (!bValidationMode)
			{
				try
				{
					if (symmetryList == null)
					{
						m_ReaderLog.AddMessageToLog(string.Format("Property : {0} doesn't exist in the database. Add {0} to the database before adding data for this property.", propName));
						throw new GatewayException(string.Format("Property : {0} doesn't exist in the database. Add {0} to the database before adding data for this property.", propName));
					}
					if (labelList == null)
					{
						labelList = new ArrayList();
						for (int i = 0; i < propNumElems; i++)
						{
							labelList.Add(propName + i);
						}
					}
					if (unitTypes == null)
					{
						m_ReaderLog.AddMessageToLog($"Warning : No Unit Type information was found for property {propName}. Assuming it to be dimensionless.");
						unitTypes = new ArrayList();
						int rowId = myUOMData.GetRowId("DIMENSIONLES");
						string text = default(string);
						string text2 = default(string);
						myUOMData.GetRowNameAndUOM(rowId, ref text, ref text2);
						if (text == "" || text2 == "")
						{
							throw new GatewayException("Unit Row with specified Row Id is not present in the database.");
						}
						UnitRowObject value = new UnitRowObject(rowId, text, text2);
						unitTypes.Add(value);
					}
					IProperty val = default(IProperty);
					myPropertyManager.AddProperty(propName, propDescription, propIsGlobal, propDataType, propInteractionType, propCompoundType, propNumElems, (int[])symmetryList.ToArray(typeof(int)), (string[])labelList.ToArray(typeof(string)), (IUnitRow[])unitTypes.ToArray(typeof(IUnitRow)), false, ref val);
					if ((int)propDataType == 1 && propNumElems > 1)
					{
						EquationObject val2 = new EquationObject(propName, propNumElems, propDescription, (byte[])null, "");
						mySdm.UpdateItem((IDatabaseObject)(object)val2);
					}
					return;
				}
				catch (Exception ex)
				{
					m_ReaderLog.AddMessageToLog(string.Format("Fatal error has occurred while trying to add missing property {0} to the database. " + ex.Message, propName));
					throw new GatewayException(string.Format("Fatal error has occurred while trying to add missing property {0} to the database. " + ex.Message, propName));
				}
			}
			Console.WriteLine("AddProperty: !ERROR! property not found: {0}\n", propName);
		}
		else if (propObj.ElementCount != propNumElems)
		{
			m_ReaderLog.AddMessageToLog(string.Format("Fatal error has occurred. Specified number of elements ({1}) for  property : {0} does not match with existing number of elements ({2}) for this property in the database.\n Data Conversion will terminate now.", propObj.Name, propNumElems, propObj.ElementCount));
			throw new GatewayException(string.Format("Fatal error has occurred. Specified number of elements ({1}) for  property : {0} does not match with existing number of elements ({2}) for this property in the database.\n Data Conversion will terminate now.", propObj.Name, propNumElems, propObj.ElementCount));
		}
	}

	public int GetNumElemsOfParam(string Parameter)
	{
		//IL_0012: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
		//IL_0017: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
		//IL_0019: Expected O, but got Unknown
		//IL_001c: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
		int result = -1;
		object obj = null;
		PropertyObject propObj = null;
		PropertyObject val = (PropertyObject)m_RVEallPropsInDbase[Parameter];
		obj = (object)val;
		if ((int)val != 0)
		{
			result = ((PropertyObject)obj).ElementCount;
		}
		else if (FindProperty(Parameter, out propObj))
		{
			result = propObj.ElementCount;
			m_RVEallPropsInDbase.Add(Parameter, propObj);
		}
		return result;
	}

	public bool CheckCompAliasExists(string CompAlias)

		return CheckCompIdentifierExists(CompAlias, "ALIAS");
	}

		return CheckCompIdentifierExists(CompName, "NAME");
	}

	public bool CheckCompIdentifierExists(string IdValue, string IdentifierType)
	{
		IIdentifierValue val = default(IIdentifierValue);
		myCompoundManager.GetCompoundIdentifier(IdValue, IdentifierType, IdentifierType, ref val);
		if (val == null)
		{
			return false;
		}
		return true;
	}

	public void AddIdentifierValues(string CompID, string IdType, string identifierName, string[] identifierList)
	{
		IdentifierObject identObj = null;
		if (!FindIdentifier(identifierName, out identObj))
		{
			m_ReaderLog.AddMessageToLog($"Identifier '{identifierName}' does not exist: {CompID}, {IdType}\n");
			return;
		}
		if (!identObj.AllowMultiples && identifierList.Length > 1)
		{
			m_ReaderLog.AddMessageToLog($"Identifier '{identifierName}' does not support multiple values: {CompID}, {IdType}\n");
			return;
		}
		IIdentifier val = default(IIdentifier);
		myCompoundManager.GetIdentifier(IdType, ref val);
		ICompound val2 = default(ICompound);
		myCompoundManager.GetCompound(CompID, val, ref val2);
		myCompoundManager.GetIdentifier(identifierName, ref val);
		IIdentifierValue val3 = default(IIdentifierValue);
		myCompoundManager.GetIdentifierValue(val2, val, ref val3);
		Hashtable hashtable = new Hashtable();
		ArrayList arrayList = new ArrayList();
		if (val3 == null)
		{
			for (int i = 0; i < identifierList.Length; i++)
			{
				if (identifierList[i] != "" && !hashtable.ContainsKey(identifierList[i]))
				{
					hashtable.Add(identifierList[i], identifierList[i]);
					arrayList.Add(identifierList[i].Trim());
				}
			}
			myCompoundManager.AddIdentifierValue(val2, val, (string[])arrayList.ToArray(typeof(string)), ref val3);
		}
		else
		{
			if (!val.AllowMultiples)
			{
				return;
			}
			hashtable = new Hashtable();
			foreach (string value in val3.ValueList)
			{
				hashtable.Add(value.ToLowerInvariant(), value);
			}
			arrayList = new ArrayList();
			for (int j = 0; j < identifierList.Length; j++)
			{
				if (identifierList[j] != "" && !hashtable.ContainsKey(identifierList[j].ToLowerInvariant()))
				{
					hashtable.Add(identifierList[j].ToLowerInvariant(), identifierList[j]);
					arrayList.Add(identifierList[j]);
				}
			}
			if (arrayList.Count > 0)
			{
				myCompoundManager.AddIdentifierValue(val2, val, (string[])arrayList.ToArray(typeof(string)), ref val3);
			}
		}
	}

	public void AddEnumerationValues(string CompID, string IdType, string propName, string[] valueList)
	{
		PropertyObject propObj = null;
		if (!FindProperty(propName, out propObj))
		{
			m_ReaderLog.AddMessageToLog($"Property '{propName}' does not exist: {CompID}, {IdType}\n");
			return;
		}
		int elementCount = propObj.ElementCount;
		if (elementCount == 0)
		{
			IPropertyValue val = default(IPropertyValue);
			myPropertyValueManager.AddPropertyValue(myDatabank, CompID, IdType, propName, (object)valueList, (string)null, (string)null, (string)null, (ICitation)null, (string)null, ref val);
		}
		else if (valueList.Length > elementCount)
		{
			m_ReaderLog.AddMessageToLog($"Exceeded maximum number of elements ({elementCount}) for Property '{propName}': {CompID}, {IdType}\n");
		}
		else
		{
			IPropertyValue val2 = default(IPropertyValue);
			myPropertyValueManager.AddPropertyValue(myDatabank, CompID, IdType, propName, (object)valueList, (string)null, (string)null, (string)null, (ICitation)null, (string)null, ref val2);
		}
	}

	public bool UpdateTHRSWTorTRNSWT(string CompAlias, string IdType, string CurrentProp, int iNumElems, ArrayList dblArray, out double[] values, out IPropertyValue propertyValue)
	{
		values = null;
		propertyValue = null;
		bool result = false;
		IIdentifier val = default(IIdentifier);
		myCompoundManager.GetIdentifier(IdType, ref val);
		ICompound val2 = default(ICompound);
		myCompoundManager.GetCompound(CompAlias, val, ref val2);
		IProperty val3 = default(IProperty);
		myPropertyManager.GetProperty(CurrentProp, ref val3);
		myPropertyValueManager.GetPropertyValue(myDatabank, val2, val3, true, ref propertyValue);
		if (propertyValue != null && propertyValue.Value != null)
		{
			values = (double[])propertyValue.Value;
			int num = 0;
			if (CurrentProp == "TRNSWT" || CurrentProp == "THRSWT")
			{
				for (int i = 0; i < iNumElems; i++)
				{
					string text = Convert.ToString(values[i], CultureInfo.InvariantCulture);
					if (dblArray[i] != null && text.CompareTo("1E+35") == 0)
					{
						values[i] = (double)dblArray[i];
						num++;
					}
					if (num > 0)
					{
						result = true;
					}
				}
			}
		}
		return result;
	}

	public void AddParamElemValue(string CompAlias, string IdType, string CurrentProp, int ElemIndex, double dblValue, ArrayList dblArray, int HighestNonNullElementInVector)
	{
		//IL_0291: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
		//IL_029b: Expected O, but got Unknown
		//IL_0197: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
		//IL_020e: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
		//IL_0640: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
		//IL_0467: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
		//IL_0471: Expected O, but got Unknown
		//IL_06f3: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
		//IL_06fd: Expected O, but got Unknown
		//IL_0545: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
		//IL_054f: Expected O, but got Unknown
		int numElemsOfParam = GetNumElemsOfParam(CurrentProp);
		bool flag = false;
		object obj = null;
		double[] values = null;
		IPropertyValue propertyValue = null;
		if ((CurrentProp == "THRSWT" || CurrentProp == "TRNSWT") && myDatabank.Name == "NIST-TRC")
		{
			flag = UpdateTHRSWTorTRNSWT(CompAlias, IdType, CurrentProp, numElemsOfParam, dblArray, out values, out propertyValue);
			if (flag)
			{
				obj = values;
			}
		}
		if (CurrentProp == "THRSWT" && myDatabank.Name.StartsWith("PURE") && myDatabank.Database.Alias.StartsWith("APV") && dblArray[4] != null && dblArray[4].ToString().CompareTo("102") == 0)
		{
			string value = "100.0";
			double num = UtilityFuncs.ConvertToDouble(value);
			dblArray[4] = num;
		}
		if (bValidationMode && numElemsOfParam < 0)
		{
			Console.WriteLine("AddParamElemValue !ERROR! Parameter does not exist: {0}, {1}, {2}, {3} \n", CompAlias, CurrentProp, ElemIndex, dblValue);
			return;
		}
		if (numElemsOfParam == 1)
		{
			if (bValidationMode)
			{
				IPropertyValue val = default(IPropertyValue);
				myPropertyValueManager.GetUnaryPropertyValue(myDatabank, CompAlias, "ALIAS", CurrentProp, ref val);
				if (val == null)
				{
					if (dblValue == 1E+35)
					{
						Console.WriteLine("AddParamElemValue validated missing value: {0}, {1}, {2} \n", CompAlias, CurrentProp, ElemIndex);
						return;
					}
					Console.WriteLine("AddParamElemValue !MISSING IN DB! value: {0}, {1}, {2}, {3}.", CompAlias, CurrentProp, ElemIndex, dblValue);
					return;
				}
				if (val != null && (double)((PropertyValueObject)val).Value == dblValue)
				{
					Console.WriteLine("AddParamElemValue validated value: {0}, {1}, {2}, {3} \n", CompAlias, CurrentProp, ElemIndex, dblValue);
					return;
				}
				Console.WriteLine("AddParamElemValue !DIFFERENT! value: {0}, {1}, {2}, {3}.", CompAlias, CurrentProp, ElemIndex, dblValue);
				if (val != null)
				{
					Console.WriteLine("!DIFFERENT! DBase value: {0}, Input file value: {1}  \n", ((PropertyValueObject)val).Value, dblValue);
				}
				else
				{
					Console.WriteLine("!DIFFERENT! DBase value: !MISSING!, Input file value: {1}  \n", dblValue);
				}
			}
			else
			{
				if (dblValue == 1E+35)
				{
					return;
				}
				try
				{
					IPropertyValue val2 = default(IPropertyValue);
					myPropertyValueManager.AddPropertyValue(myDatabank, CompAlias, IdType, CurrentProp, (object)dblValue, (string)null, (string)null, (string)null, (ICitation)null, (string)null, ref val2);
					if (cosmoSolProperties.Contains(CurrentProp.ToUpperInvariant()))
					{
						myCitationManager.AddCitation(myDatabank, CompAlias, IdType, CurrentProp, (PropertyCitationsObject)cosmoSolCitation);
					}
					return;
				}
				catch (GatewayException ex)
				{
					if (ex.Message.StartsWith("Data Conversion Warning:"))
					{
						m_ReaderLog.AddMessageToLog(ex.Message);
						return;
					}
					throw ex;
				}
			}
			return;
		}
		EquationObject equation = null;
		if (!FindEquation(CurrentProp, out equation) || equation == null)
		{
			throw new GatewayException($"Fatal error has occurred. Property {CurrentProp} does not exit in database. Please make sure this property has been added to database before you add data for it.\n  Data Conversion will terminate now.");
		}
		object[] array = new object[equation.NumParameters];
		for (int i = 0; i < equation.NumParameters; i++)
		{
			array[i] = null;
		}
		if (dblArray == null)
		{
			IPropertyValue val3 = default(IPropertyValue);
			myPropertyValueManager.GetUnaryPropertyValue(myDatabank, CompAlias, "ALIAS", CurrentProp, ref val3);
			if (bValidationMode)
			{
				if (val3 == null && dblValue != 1E+35)
				{
					Console.WriteLine("AddParamElemValue !DIFFERENT! values, missing in DBase: {0}, {1}, {2}, {3} \n", CompAlias, CurrentProp, ElemIndex, dblValue);
					return;
				}
				array = (object[])val3.Value;
				if (array.Length >= ElemIndex && UtilityFuncs.ConvertToDouble(array[ElemIndex - 1].ToString()) == dblValue)
				{
					Console.WriteLine("AddParamElemValue validated: {0}, {1}, {2}, {3} \n", CompAlias, CurrentProp, ElemIndex, dblValue);
				}
				else
				{
					Console.WriteLine("AddParamElemValue !DIFFERENT! values, missing in DBase: {0}, {1}, {2}, {3} \n", CompAlias, CurrentProp, ElemIndex, dblValue);
				}
				return;
			}
			if (val3 == null)
			{
				array[ElemIndex - 1] = dblValue;
				try
				{
					IPropertyValue val4 = default(IPropertyValue);
					myPropertyValueManager.AddPropertyValue(myDatabank, CompAlias, IdType, CurrentProp, (object)array, (string)null, (string)null, (string)null, (ICitation)null, (string)null, ref val4);
					if (cosmoSolProperties.Contains(CurrentProp.ToUpperInvariant()))
					{
						myCitationManager.AddCitation(myDatabank, CompAlias, IdType, CurrentProp, (PropertyCitationsObject)cosmoSolCitation);
					}
					return;
				}
				catch (GatewayException ex2)
				{
					if (ex2.Message.StartsWith("Data Conversion Warning:"))
					{
						m_ReaderLog.AddMessageToLog(ex2.Message);
						return;
					}
					throw ex2;
				}
			}
			object[] array2 = (object[])val3.Value;
			if (array2.Length >= ElemIndex && UtilityFuncs.ConvertToDouble(array2[ElemIndex - 1].ToString()) == dblValue)
			{
				return;
			}
			for (int j = 0; j < array2.Length; j++)
			{
				array[j] = array2[j];
			}
			array[ElemIndex - 1] = dblValue;
			try
			{
				IPropertyValue val5 = default(IPropertyValue);
				myPropertyValueManager.AddPropertyValue(myDatabank, CompAlias, IdType, CurrentProp, (object)array, (string)null, (string)null, (string)null, (ICitation)null, (string)null, ref val5);
				if (cosmoSolProperties.Contains(CurrentProp.ToUpperInvariant()))
				{
					myCitationManager.AddCitation(myDatabank, CompAlias, IdType, CurrentProp, (PropertyCitationsObject)cosmoSolCitation);
				}
				return;
			}
			catch (GatewayException ex3)
			{
				if (ex3.Message.StartsWith("Data Conversion Warning:"))
				{
					m_ReaderLog.AddMessageToLog(ex3.Message);
					return;
				}
				throw ex3;
			}
		}
		if (HighestNonNullElementInVector == 0)
		{
			if (bValidationMode)
			{
				IPropertyValue val6 = default(IPropertyValue);
				myPropertyValueManager.GetUnaryPropertyValue(myDatabank, CompAlias, "ALIAS", CurrentProp, ref val6);
				if (val6 == null)
				{
					Console.WriteLine("AddParamElemValue - validated BLANK: {0}, {1}, {2}, {3} ", CompAlias, CurrentProp, ElemIndex, dblValue);
				}
				else
				{
					Console.WriteLine("AddParamElemValue - !ERROR! failed to validate BLANK: {0}, {1}, {2}, {3} \n", CompAlias, CurrentProp, ElemIndex, dblValue);
				}
			}
			return;
		}
		array = dblArray.ToArray();
		if (bValidationMode)
		{
			IPropertyValue val7 = default(IPropertyValue);
			myPropertyValueManager.GetUnaryPropertyValue(myDatabank, CompAlias, "ALIAS", CurrentProp, ref val7);
			if (val7 != null && EquationValuesAreSame(dblArray, (object[])((PropertyValueObject)val7).Value))
			{
				Console.Write("AddParamElemValue validated: {0}, {1}, {2} elements ", CompAlias, CurrentProp, ElemIndex);
				PrintArrayList(dblArray, ',', terminate: true);
			}
			else
			{
				Console.Write("AddParamElemValue !ERROR! different values: {0}, {1}, {2} elements ", CompAlias, CurrentProp, ElemIndex);
				PrintArrayList(dblArray, ',', terminate: true);
			}
			return;
		}
		try
		{
			if (flag)
			{
				myPropertyValueManager.UpdatePropertyValue(ref propertyValue, true, obj, (string)null, (string)null, (string)null, (ICitation)null, (string)null);
				return;
			}
			IPropertyValue val8 = default(IPropertyValue);
			myPropertyValueManager.AddPropertyValue(myDatabank, CompAlias, IdType, CurrentProp, (object)array, (string)null, (string)null, (string)null, (ICitation)null, (string)null, ref val8);
			if (cosmoSolProperties.Contains(CurrentProp.ToUpperInvariant()))
			{
				myCitationManager.AddCitation(myDatabank, CompAlias, IdType, CurrentProp, (PropertyCitationsObject)cosmoSolCitation);
			}
		}
		catch (GatewayException ex4)
		{
			if (ex4.Message.StartsWith("Data Conversion Warning:"))
			{
				m_ReaderLog.AddMessageToLog(ex4.Message);
				return;
			}
			throw ex4;
		}
	}

	private bool EquationValuesAreSame(ArrayList dblArray, object[] oldValue)
	{
		if (dblArray.Count != oldValue.Length)
		{
			return false;
		}
		for (int i = 0; i < dblArray.Count; i++)
		{
			if (dblArray[i] == null || oldValue[i] == null)
			{
				if (dblArray[i] != oldValue[i])
				{
					return false;
				}
			}
			else if (dblArray.ToString() != oldValue[i].ToString())
			{
				return false;
			}
		}
		return true;
	}

	private int ParInfoNeedsToBeAddedToDBank(InteractionParameterDatabankInformationObject.GammaModelParameterInformation parInfo, ArrayList pInfoList, int indexInArray)
	{
		indexInArray = -1;
		int result = 1;
		for (int i = 0; i < pInfoList.Count; i++)
		{
			InteractionParameterDatabankInformationObject.GammaModelParameterInformation gammaModelParameterInformation = (InteractionParameterDatabankInformationObject.GammaModelParameterInformation)pInfoList[i];
			if (gammaModelParameterInformation.ParameterName == parInfo.ParameterName)
			{
				result = ((!(gammaModelParameterInformation.GammaModelName == parInfo.GammaModelName) || !(gammaModelParameterInformation.VaporModelName == parInfo.VaporModelName)) ? (-1) : 0);
				break;
			}
		}
		return result;
	}

	public void AddBinaryDatabankForParams(ArrayList ParamNames, string DbName, string EosModel, string GammaModel)
	{
		CreateNewDBank("", DbName);
		switch (DbName.ToUpperInvariant())
		{
		case "NIST-RK":
			EosModel = "RK";
			GammaModel = "ActivityCoefficient";
			break;
		case "NIST-IG":
			EosModel = "IG";
			GammaModel = "ActivityCoefficient";
			break;
		case "NIST-EOS":
			EosModel = "EOS";
			GammaModel = "ActivityCoefficient";
			break;
		}
		ISundryDataManager sundryDataManager = myDatabase.SundryDataManager;
		IDatabaseObject item = sundryDataManager.GetItem(DbName);
		InteractionParameterDatabankInformationObject interactionParameterDatabankInformationObject;
		ArrayList parameterInfoList;
		if (item != null)
		{
			interactionParameterDatabankInformationObject = (InteractionParameterDatabankInformationObject)(object)item;
			parameterInfoList = interactionParameterDatabankInformationObject.ParameterInfoList;
		}
		else
		{
			interactionParameterDatabankInformationObject = new InteractionParameterDatabankInformationObject(DbName, null);
			parameterInfoList = interactionParameterDatabankInformationObject.ParameterInfoList;
		}
		bool flag = false;
		for (int i = 0; i < ParamNames.Count; i++)
		{
			InteractionParameterDatabankInformationObject.GammaModelParameterInformation gammaModelParameterInformation = new InteractionParameterDatabankInformationObject.GammaModelParameterInformation
			{
				ParameterName = ParamNames[i].ToString(),
				VaporModelName = EosModel,
				GammaModelName = GammaModel
			};
			int num = -1;
			switch (ParInfoNeedsToBeAddedToDBank(gammaModelParameterInformation, parameterInfoList, num))
			{
			case 1:
				parameterInfoList.Add(gammaModelParameterInformation);
				flag = true;
				continue;
			case 0:
				continue;
			}
			InteractionParameterDatabankInformationObject.GammaModelParameterInformation gammaModelParameterInformation2 = (InteractionParameterDatabankInformationObject.GammaModelParameterInformation)parameterInfoList[num];
			m_ReaderLog.AddMessageToLog(string.Format("Warning : AddBinaryDatabankForParams - redefinition of Gamma and EOS models for parameter %s: New %s %s ", gammaModelParameterInformation.ParameterName, gammaModelParameterInformation.GammaModelName, gammaModelParameterInformation.VaporModelName));
			m_ReaderLog.AddMessageToLog(string.Format(" Old %s, %s ", gammaModelParameterInformation2.GammaModelName, gammaModelParameterInformation2.VaporModelName));
			parameterInfoList[num] = gammaModelParameterInformation;
			flag = true;
		}
		if (flag)
		{
			sundryDataManager.UpdateItem((IDatabaseObject)(object)interactionParameterDatabankInformationObject);
		}
	}

	public void AddBinaryParamVector(string CompI, string CompJ, string ParamName, bool bIsPairParam, ArrayList StoreValues, ALL_Regparams myRegparams, int RegType, bool isDFMS)
	{
		double[] array = new double[StoreValues.Count];
		bool flag = true;
		for (int i = 0; i < StoreValues.Count; i++)
		{
			if (StoreValues[i] != null && Convert.ToDouble(StoreValues[i], CultureInfo.InvariantCulture) != 1E+35)
			{
				array[i] = Convert.ToDouble(StoreValues[i], CultureInfo.InvariantCulture);
				flag = false;
			}
			else
			{
				array[i] = 1E+35;
			}
		}
		if (flag)
		{
			return;
		}
		IIdentifier val = default(IIdentifier);
		myCompoundManager.GetIdentifier("ALIAS", ref val);
		Regex regex = new Regex(".*-B[1-15]$");
		if (bIsPairParam)
		{
			char[] separator = new char[1] { '$' };
			string[] array2 = CompI.Split(separator);
			if (array2.Length > 2)
			{
				m_ReaderLog.AddMessageToLog($"Invalid pair name {CompI} ");
			}
			string[] array3 = array2;
			ICompound val2 = default(ICompound);
			foreach (string text in array3)
			{
				if (!CheckCompAliasExists(text))
				{
					myCompoundManager.AddCompound(text, val, ref val2);
				}
			}
			string text2 = null;
			if (array2.Length > 1)
			{
				text2 = array2[1];
			}
			string[] array4 = CompJ.Split(separator);
			if (array4.Length > 2)
			{
				m_ReaderLog.AddMessageToLog($"Invalid pair name {CompJ} ");
			}
			string[] array5 = array4;
			ICompound val3 = default(ICompound);
			foreach (string text3 in array5)
			{
				if (!CheckCompAliasExists(text3))
				{
					myCompoundManager.AddCompound(text3, val, ref val3);
				}
			}
			string text4 = null;
			if (array4.Length > 1)
			{
				text4 = array4[1];
			}
			if (text2 == null && text4 == null)
			{
				m_ReaderLog.AddMessageToLog($"At least one species must be a pair.  Skipping {CompI}, {CompJ} ");
				return;
			}
			try
			{
				IPropertyValue val4 = default(IPropertyValue);
				myPropertyValueManager.AddPairPropertyValue(myDatabank, array2[0], text2, array4[0], text4, "ALIAS", ParamName, (object)array, (string)null, (string)null, (string)null, (ICitation)null, (string)null, ref val4);
			}
			catch (GatewayException ex)
			{
				if (!ex.Message.StartsWith("Data Conversion Warning:"))
				{
					throw ex;
				}
				m_ReaderLog.AddMessageToLog(ex.Message);
			}
		}
		else if (isDFMS)
		{
			bool flag2 = CheckCompNameExists(CompI);
			bool flag3 = CheckCompAliasExists(CompI);
			bool flag4 = CheckCompNameExists(CompJ);
			bool flag5 = CheckCompAliasExists(CompJ);
			if (!flag2 && !flag3)
			{
				m_ReaderLog.AddMessageToLog($"Fatal error has occurred. Please make sure to add compound {CompI} in NEW-COMP section before you specify property values for it.\nData Conversion will terminate now.");
				throw new GatewayException($"Fatal error has occurred. Please make sure to add compound {CompI} in NEW-COMP section before you specify property values for it.\nData Conversion will terminate now.");
			}
			if (!flag4 && !flag5)
			{
				m_ReaderLog.AddMessageToLog($"Fatal error has occurred. Please make sure to add compound {CompJ} in NEW-COMP section before you specify property values for it.\nData Conversion will terminate now.");
				throw new GatewayException($"Fatal error has occurred. Please make sure to add compound {CompJ} in NEW-COMP section before you specify property values for it.\nData Conversion will terminate now.");
			}
			try
			{
				IPropertyValue val5 = default(IPropertyValue);
				if (flag2 && flag4)
				{
					myPropertyValueManager.AddBinaryPropertyValue(myDatabank, CompI, CompJ, "NAME", ParamName, (object)array, (string)null, (string)null, (string)null, (ICitation)null, (string)null, ref val5);
				}
				else if (flag3 && flag5)
				{
					myPropertyValueManager.AddBinaryPropertyValue(myDatabank, CompI, CompJ, "ALIAS", ParamName, (object)array, (string)null, (string)null, (string)null, (ICitation)null, (string)null, ref val5);
				}
			}
			catch (GatewayException ex2)
			{
				if (!ex2.Message.StartsWith("Data Conversion Warning:"))
				{
					throw ex2;
				}
				m_ReaderLog.AddMessageToLog(ex2.Message);
			}
		}
		else
		{
			if (!CheckCompAliasExists(CompI))
			{
				ICompound val6 = default(ICompound);
				myCompoundManager.AddCompound(CompI, val, ref val6);
				if (regex.IsMatch(CompI))
				{
					IIdentifier val7 = default(IIdentifier);
					myCompoundManager.GetIdentifier("NAME", ref val7);
					string[] array6 = new string[1] { CompI };
					IIdentifierValue val8 = default(IIdentifierValue);
					myCompoundManager.AddIdentifierValue(val6, val7, array6, ref val8);
				}
			}
			if (!CheckCompAliasExists(CompJ))
			{
				ICompound val9 = default(ICompound);
				myCompoundManager.AddCompound(CompJ, val, ref val9);
				if (regex.IsMatch(CompJ))
				{
					IIdentifier val10 = default(IIdentifier);
					myCompoundManager.GetIdentifier("NAME", ref val10);
					string[] array7 = new string[1] { CompJ };
					IIdentifierValue val11 = default(IIdentifierValue);
					myCompoundManager.AddIdentifierValue(val9, val10, array7, ref val11);
				}
			}
			try
			{
				IPropertyValue val12 = default(IPropertyValue);
				myPropertyValueManager.AddBinaryPropertyValue(myDatabank, CompI, CompJ, "ALIAS", ParamName, (object)array, (string)null, (string)null, (string)null, (ICitation)null, (string)null, ref val12);
			}
			catch (GatewayException ex3)
			{
				if (!ex3.Message.StartsWith("Data Conversion Warning:"))
				{
					throw ex3;
				}
				m_ReaderLog.AddMessageToLog(ex3.Message);
			}
		}
		if (RegType == 0)
		{
			return;
		}
		IpHelpInfoObject ipHelpInfoObject = new IpHelpInfoObject();
		((DatabaseObject)ipHelpInfoObject).Database = (IDatabase)(object)myDatabase;
		bool flag6 = false;
		if (!ipHelpInfoObject.FetchExistingHelpInfo(myDatabank.Name, ParamName, CompI, "ALIAS", CompJ, "ALIAS", out var _))
		{
			ipHelpInfoObject = new IpHelpInfoObject((IDatabase)(object)myDatabase, (IpHelpInfoType)RegType, myDatabank.Name, ParamName, CompI, "ALIAS", CompJ, "ALIAS");
			flag6 = true;
		}
		if (ipHelpInfoObject.ParamType != (IpHelpInfoType)RegType)
		{
			ipHelpInfoObject.ParamType = (IpHelpInfoType)RegType;
			flag6 = true;
		}
		switch (RegType)
		{
		case 1:
			if (!flag6 && !ipHelpInfoObject.VleInfo.IsEqual((VLE_Regparams)myRegparams))
			{
				flag6 = true;
			}
			ipHelpInfoObject.VleInfo = (VLE_Regparams)myRegparams;
			break;
		case 2:
			if (!flag6 && !ipHelpInfoObject.LleInfo.IsEqual((LLE_Regparams)myRegparams))
			{
				flag6 = true;
			}
			ipHelpInfoObject.LleInfo = (LLE_Regparams)myRegparams;
			break;
		case 3:
			if (!flag6 && !ipHelpInfoObject.HenryInfo.IsEqual((HENRY_Regparams)myRegparams))
			{
				flag6 = true;
			}
			ipHelpInfoObject.HenryInfo = (HENRY_Regparams)myRegparams;
			break;
		case 4:
			if (!flag6 && !ipHelpInfoObject.NistInfo.IsEqual((NIST_Regparams)myRegparams))
			{
				flag6 = true;
			}
			ipHelpInfoObject.NistInfo = (NIST_Regparams)myRegparams;
			break;
		default:
			m_ReaderLog.AddMessageToLog($"Unknown regression information type: {RegType} ");
			flag6 = false;
			break;
		}
		if (flag6)
		{
			ISundryDataManager sundryDataManager = myDatabase.SundryDataManager;
			sundryDataManager.UpdateItem((IDatabaseObject)(object)ipHelpInfoObject);
		}
	}

	public void AddBinaryParameters(ArrayList ParamNames, ArrayList NumSymElems, ArrayList SymmetryCodes, ArrayList LabelList)
	{
		//IL_00eb: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
		//IL_00f2: Expected O, but got Unknown
		int count = ParamNames.Count;
		int i = 0;
		int j = 0;
		IProperty val = default(IProperty);
		for (int k = 0; k < count; k++)
		{
			string text = (string)ParamNames[k];
			string text2 = text;
			int num = (int)NumSymElems[k];
			ArrayList arrayList = new ArrayList();
			ArrayList arrayList2 = new ArrayList();
			int num2 = 0;
			int num3 = 0;
			for (; i < SymmetryCodes.Count; i++)
			{
				arrayList.Add(SymmetryCodes[i]);
				num2 = (((int)SymmetryCodes[i] != 0 && (int)SymmetryCodes[i] != 3) ? (num2 + 1) : (num2 + 2));
				if (num3 == num - 1)
				{
					i++;
					break;
				}
				num3++;
			}
			int num4 = 0;
			for (; j < LabelList.Count; j++)
			{
				arrayList2.Add(LabelList[j]);
				if (num4 == num2 - 1)
				{
					j++;
					break;
				}
				num4++;
			}
			myPropertyManager.GetProperty(text, ref val);
			PropertyObject val2 = (PropertyObject)val;
			if (val2 == null)
			{
				m_ReaderLog.AddMessageToLog("Fatal error has occurred Name: " + text + "does not exist in the database.  Make sure it's PPLCDEFS definition has been processed before you add data for it. Data Conversion will terminate now.");
				throw new GatewayException("Fatal error has occurred Name: " + text + "does not exist in the database.  Make sure it's PPLCDEFS definition has been processed before you add data for it. Data Conversion will terminate now.");
			}
			if (val2.ElementCount != arrayList.Count)
			{
				m_ReaderLog.AddMessageToLog("Fatal error has occurred. Specified number of elements for Property : " + text + " does not match with what exists in the database. Data Conversion will terminate now.");
				throw new GatewayException("Fatal error has occurred. Specified number of elements for Property : " + text + " does not match with what exists in the database. Data Conversion will terminate now.");
			}
			if (val2.SymmetryList.Length != arrayList.Count)
			{
				m_ReaderLog.AddMessageToLog("Fatal error has occurred. Specified symmetry codes for Property : " + text + " does not match with what exists in the database. Data Conversion will terminate now.");
				throw new GatewayException("Fatal error has occurred. Specified symmetry codes for Property : " + text + " does not match with what exists in the database. Data Conversion will terminate now.");
			}
			for (int l = 0; l < val2.SymmetryList.Length; l++)
			{
				if ((int)val2.SymmetryList.GetValue(l) != (int)arrayList[l])
				{
					m_ReaderLog.AddMessageToLog("Fatal error has occurred. Specified symmetry code at element : " + l + " for Property : " + text + " does not match with what exists in the database. Data Conversion will terminate now.");
					throw new GatewayException("Fatal error has occurred. Specified symmetry code at element : " + l + " for Property : " + text + " does not match with what exists in the database. Data Conversion will terminate now.");
				}
			}
		}
	}

	private void PrintArrayList(ArrayList myList, char mySeparator, bool terminate)
	{
		IEnumerator enumerator = myList.GetEnumerator();
		enumerator.MoveNext();
		Console.Write("{0}", enumerator.Current);
		while (enumerator.MoveNext())
		{
			Console.Write("{0} {1}", mySeparator, enumerator.Current);
		}
		if (terminate)
		{
			Console.WriteLine();
		}
	}

	public void AddPureDatabankInfo(string DbName, string DisplayNameMM, string Password, string DatabankType, string PromptMM, string Product, bool UpdateDisplayNameMM, bool UpdatePassword, bool UpdateDatabankType, bool UpdatePromptMM, bool UpdateProduct)
	{
		if (!(DbName.Trim() == ""))
		{
			string text = "";
			ISundryDataManager sundryDataManager = myDatabase.SundryDataManager;
			string sundryDataTableEntryName = PureDatabankInfoObject.GetSundryDataTableEntryName(DbName);
			IDatabaseObject item = sundryDataManager.GetItem(sundryDataTableEntryName);
			bool flag = false;
			PureDatabankInfoObject pureDatabankInfoObject;
			if (item != null)
			{
				pureDatabankInfoObject = (PureDatabankInfoObject)(object)item;
			}
			else
			{
				pureDatabankInfoObject = new PureDatabankInfoObject(DbName);
				flag = true;
			}
			if (UpdateDisplayNameMM && pureDatabankInfoObject.DisplayNameMM != DisplayNameMM)
			{
				pureDatabankInfoObject.DisplayNameMM = DisplayNameMM;
				flag = true;
				text = text + " DisplayNameMM = " + DisplayNameMM;
			}
			if (UpdatePassword && pureDatabankInfoObject.Password != Password)
			{
				pureDatabankInfoObject.Password = Password;
				flag = true;
				text = text + " Password = " + Password;
			}
			if (UpdateDatabankType && pureDatabankInfoObject.DatabankType != DatabankType)
			{
				pureDatabankInfoObject.DatabankType = DatabankType;
				flag = true;
				text = text + " DatabankType = " + DatabankType;
			}
			if (UpdatePromptMM && pureDatabankInfoObject.PromptMM != PromptMM)
			{
				pureDatabankInfoObject.PromptMM = PromptMM;
				flag = true;
				text = text + " PromptMM = " + PromptMM;
			}
			if (UpdateProduct && pureDatabankInfoObject.Product != Product)
			{
				pureDatabankInfoObject.Product = Product;
				flag = true;
				text = text + " Product = " + Product;
			}
			if (flag)
			{
				sundryDataManager.UpdateItem((IDatabaseObject)(object)pureDatabankInfoObject);
			}
		}
	}

	public void AddParameter(string ParameterName, string ParameterDescription, int dimcode, int nElem, ArrayList symmetryList, ArrayList labelList, ArrayList conversionCodes)
	{
		//IL_00df: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
		//IL_00e2: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
		//IL_00e7: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
		//IL_00ea: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
		//IL_00ef: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
		//IL_00f2: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
		//IL_00f7: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
		//IL_00fa: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
		//IL_00ff: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
		//IL_0102: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
		//IL_011c: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
		//IL_011e: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
		//IL_0064: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
		//IL_006b: Expected O, but got Unknown
		ArrayList arrayList = new ArrayList();
		int num = -1;
		string text = "";
		string text2 = "";
		if (conversionCodes != null)
		{
			for (int i = 0; i < conversionCodes.Count; i++)
			{
				num = (int)conversionCodes[i];
				myUOMData.GetRowNameAndUOM(num, ref text, ref text2);
				if (text == "" || text2 == "")
				{
					throw new GatewayException("Unit Row with specified Row Id is not present in the database.");
				}
				UnitRowObject value = new UnitRowObject(num, text, text2);
				arrayList.Add(value);
			}
		}
		if (labelList.Count == 0)
		{
			for (int j = 0; j < nElem; j++)
			{
				labelList.Add(ParameterName + j);
			}
		}
		InteractionType propInteractionType;
		CompoundType propCompoundType;
		switch (dimcode)
		{
		case 1:
			propInteractionType = (InteractionType)0;
			propCompoundType = (CompoundType)0;
			break;
		case 2:
			propInteractionType = (InteractionType)1;
			propCompoundType = (CompoundType)0;
			break;
		case 5:
			propInteractionType = (InteractionType)0;
			propCompoundType = (CompoundType)1;
			break;
		case 6:
			propInteractionType = (InteractionType)1;
			propCompoundType = (CompoundType)1;
			break;
		case 8:
			propInteractionType = (InteractionType)3;
			propCompoundType = (CompoundType)2;
			break;
		default:
			Console.WriteLine("AddParameter !ERROR!: Unrecognized dimcode {0}", dimcode);
			return;
		}
		AddProperty(ParameterName, ParameterDescription, propIsGlobal: false, (PropertyDataType)1, propInteractionType, propCompoundType, nElem, symmetryList, labelList, arrayList);
	}

	public void AddReaction(string reactnName, string reactnType, ArrayList synArrList, ArrayList coeffsArrList, ArrayList eqConstsArrList, string reactnBasis, ArrayList SymmetricEqConsts, string symmetricEqConstBasis)
	{
		IReaction val = null;
		try
		{
			IIdentifier val2 = default(IIdentifier);
			myCompoundManager.GetIdentifier("ALIAS", ref val2);
			ICompound val3 = default(ICompound);
			for (int i = 0; i < synArrList.Count; i++)
			{
				myCompoundManager.GetCompound(synArrList[i].ToString(), val2, ref val3);
				if (val3 == null)
				{
					myCompoundManager.AddCompound(synArrList[i].ToString(), val2, ref val3);
				}
			}
			myReactionManager.AddReaction(myDatabank, reactnName, reactnType, (ICollection)synArrList, (ICollection)coeffsArrList, (ICollection)null, (ICollection)eqConstsArrList, reactnBasis, (string)null, (string)null, SymmetricEqConsts, symmetricEqConstBasis, ref val);
		}
		catch (GatewayException ex)
		{
			m_ReaderLog.AddMessageToLog(ex.Message);
			if (!ex.Message.StartsWith("Specified reaction"))
			{
				throw ex;
			}
		}
	}

	public void DealWithProperties(ArrayList IDatabaseLst, ArrayList propLst, Hashtable optionTbl)
	{
		//IL_0017: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
		//IL_001e: Expected O, but got Unknown
		//IL_002d: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
		//IL_0034: Expected O, but got Unknown
		//IL_009c: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
		//IL_00a3: Expected O, but got Unknown
		ArrayList conflictingPropLst = new ArrayList();
		ArrayList arrayList = new ArrayList();
		for (int i = 0; i < IDatabaseLst.Count; i++)
		{
			IDatabase source = (IDatabase)IDatabaseLst[i];
			for (int j = i + 1; j < IDatabaseLst.Count; j++)
			{
				IDatabase destination = (IDatabase)IDatabaseLst[j];
				ResolvePropertyConflicts(source, destination, propLst, optionTbl, ref conflictingPropLst);
				for (int k = 0; k < conflictingPropLst.Count; k++)
				{
					propLst.Remove(conflictingPropLst[k]);
				}
			}
		}
		conflictingPropLst.Clear();
		IDatabase source2 = (IDatabase)(object)myDatabase;
		for (int l = 0; l < IDatabaseLst.Count; l++)
		{
			IDatabase destination2 = (IDatabase)IDatabaseLst[l];
			ResolvePropertyConflicts(source2, destination2, propLst, optionTbl, ref conflictingPropLst);
			for (int m = 0; m < conflictingPropLst.Count; m++)
			{
				propLst.Remove(conflictingPropLst[m]);
			}
		}
	}

	private void ResolvePropertyConflicts(IDatabase source, IDatabase destination, ArrayList propLst, Hashtable optionTbl, ref ArrayList conflictingPropLst)
	{
		//IL_0001: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
		//IL_000d: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
		//IL_0070: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
		//IL_0076: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
		//IL_0240: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
		//IL_0246: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
		//IL_024c: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
		//IL_0083: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
		//IL_0089: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
		//IL_00a9: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
		//IL_00af: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
		IPropertyManager propertyManager = ((Database)source).PropertyManager;
		IPropertyManager propertyManager2 = ((Database)destination).PropertyManager;
		IProperty val = null;
		IProperty val2 = null;
		string text = "";
		for (int i = 0; i < propLst.Count; i++)
		{
			propertyManager.GetProperty(propLst[i].ToString(), ref val);
			propertyManager2.GetProperty(propLst[i].ToString(), ref val2);
			if (val != null && val2 != null)
			{
				if (!val.Status)
				{
					continue;
				}
				bool flag = true;
				if (val.CompoundType != val2.CompoundType)
				{
					flag = false;
				}
				else if (val.DataType != val2.DataType)
				{
					flag = false;
				}
				else if (val.ElementCount != val2.ElementCount)
				{
					flag = false;
				}
				else if (val.InteractionType != val2.InteractionType)
				{
					flag = false;
				}
				else if (val.Name != val2.Name)
				{
					flag = false;
				}
				else if (val.SymmetryList.Length != val2.SymmetryList.Length)
				{
					flag = false;
				}
				else if (val.UnitsTypeList.Length != val2.UnitsTypeList.Length)
				{
					flag = false;
				}
				if (flag)
				{
					for (int j = 0; j < val.SymmetryList.Length; j++)
					{
						int num = val.SymmetryList[j];
						int num2 = val2.SymmetryList[j];
						if (num != num2)
						{
							flag = false;
							break;
						}
					}
				}
				if (flag)
				{
					for (int k = 0; k < val.UnitsTypeList.Length; k++)
					{
						IUnitRow val3 = val.UnitsTypeList[k];
						IUnitRow val4 = val2.UnitsTypeList[k];
						if (val3.DefaultUOM != val4.DefaultUOM)
						{
							flag = false;
							break;
						}
						if (val3.RowId != val4.RowId)
						{
							flag = false;
							break;
						}
					}
				}
				if (!flag)
				{
					conflictingPropLst.Add(propLst[i].ToString());
					text = "Warning : property definition conflict was detected. Property '{0}'  from Database '{1}' does not match with Property '{2}' from Database '{3}'";
					m_ReaderLog.AddMessageToLog(string.Format(text, val.Name, source.Alias, val2.Name, destination.Alias));
				}
			}
			else if (val == null && val2 != null && source.Alias.CompareTo(((IDatabase)myDatabase).Alias) == 0)
			{
				IProperty val5 = null;
				myPropertyManager.AddProperty(val2.Name, val2.Description, val2.IsGlobal, val2.DataType, val2.InteractionType, val2.CompoundType, val2.ElementCount, val2.SymmetryList, val2.LabelList, val2.UnitsTypeList, false, ref val5);
				if (val5 != null)
				{
					text = "Successfully added new property {0} to destination database {1}.";
					m_ReaderLog.AddMessageToLog(string.Format(text, val5.Name, ((IDatabase)myDatabase).Alias));
				}
			}
		}
	}

	public ArrayList DealWithCompounds(ArrayList IDatabaseLst, ArrayList compLst, string compIdentifier, Hashtable optionTbl)
	{
		//IL_0231: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
		//IL_0238: Expected O, but got Unknown
		//IL_03ec: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
		//IL_03f3: Expected O, but got Unknown
		//IL_0066: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
		//IL_006d: Expected O, but got Unknown
		//IL_007d: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
		//IL_0084: Expected O, but got Unknown
		//IL_010a: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
		//IL_0111: Expected O, but got Unknown
		string text = "";
		Hashtable AddCompsInDestination = new Hashtable();
		Hashtable UpdateCompsInDestination = new Hashtable();
		Hashtable molStrTbl = new Hashtable();
		ArrayList conflictingCompLst = new ArrayList();
		ArrayList arrayList = new ArrayList();
		compIdentifier = compIdentifier.ToUpperInvariant();
		bool flag = false;
		object obj = optionTbl["OVERWRITE-DATA"];
		if (obj != null && obj.ToString().CompareTo("YES") == 0)
		{
			flag = true;
		}
		for (int i = 0; i < IDatabaseLst.Count; i++)
		{
			IDatabase source = (IDatabase)IDatabaseLst[i];
			for (int j = i + 1; j < IDatabaseLst.Count; j++)
			{
				IDatabase destination = (IDatabase)IDatabaseLst[j];
				ResolveCompoundConflicts(source, destination, compIdentifier, compLst, ref conflictingCompLst, ref AddCompsInDestination, ref UpdateCompsInDestination, ref molStrTbl);
			}
		}
		for (int k = 0; k < conflictingCompLst.Count; k++)
		{
			arrayList.Add(conflictingCompLst[k]);
			compLst.Remove(conflictingCompLst[k]);
		}
		conflictingCompLst.Clear();
		IDatabase source2 = (IDatabase)(object)myDatabase;
		for (int l = 0; l < IDatabaseLst.Count; l++)
		{
			IDatabase destination2 = (IDatabase)IDatabaseLst[l];
			ResolveCompoundConflicts(source2, destination2, compIdentifier, compLst, ref conflictingCompLst, ref AddCompsInDestination, ref UpdateCompsInDestination, ref molStrTbl);
		}
		for (int m = 0; m < conflictingCompLst.Count; m++)
		{
			arrayList.Add(conflictingCompLst[m]);
			compLst.Remove(conflictingCompLst[m]);
		}
		if (AddCompsInDestination != null && AddCompsInDestination.Count > 0)
		{
			foreach (string key5 in AddCompsInDestination.Keys)
			{
				KeyValuePair<ICompound, Hashtable> keyValuePair = (KeyValuePair<ICompound, Hashtable>)AddCompsInDestination[key5];
				ICompound key2 = keyValuePair.Key;
				Hashtable value = keyValuePair.Value;
				IIdentifier val = null;
				ICompound val2 = null;
				myCompoundManager.GetIdentifier(compIdentifier.ToUpperInvariant(), ref val);
				myCompoundManager.AddCompound(key2.Compound1Name, val, ref val2);
				if (val2 != null)
				{
					text = "New Compound {0} Added.";
					m_ReaderLog.AddMessageToLog(string.Format(text, val2.Compound1Name.ToUpperInvariant()));
					foreach (IIdentifierValue value3 in value.Values)
					{
						IIdentifierValue val3 = value3;
						if (val3.Identifier.Id == 1)
						{
							continue;
						}
						IIdentifier val4 = null;
						IIdentifierValue val5 = null;
						myCompoundManager.GetIdentifier(val3.Identifier.Name, ref val4);
						if (val4 == null)
						{
							myCompoundManager.AddIdentifier(val3.Identifier.Name, val3.Identifier.Description, val3.Identifier.AllowMultiples, ref val4);
						}
						myCompoundManager.GetIdentifierValue(val2, val4, ref val5);
						if (val5 == null)
						{
							try
							{
								myCompoundManager.AddIdentifierValue(val2, val4, (string[])val3.ValueList, ref val5);
							}
							catch (Exception ex)
							{
								m_ReaderLog.AddMessageToLog(ex.Message);
							}
						}
					}
					object obj2 = molStrTbl[key5];
					if (obj2 != null)
					{
						string text2 = obj2.ToString();
						myMolDataManager.AddStructure(val2.Id, text2, 1);
					}
				}
				else
				{
					text = "Error adding new compound {0}.";
					m_ReaderLog.AddMessageToLog(string.Format(text, key2.Compound1Name.ToUpperInvariant()));
				}
			}
		}
		if (UpdateCompsInDestination != null && UpdateCompsInDestination.Count > 0)
		{
			foreach (string key6 in UpdateCompsInDestination.Keys)
			{
				KeyValuePair<ICompound, Hashtable> keyValuePair2 = (KeyValuePair<ICompound, Hashtable>)UpdateCompsInDestination[key6];
				ICompound key4 = keyValuePair2.Key;
				Hashtable value2 = keyValuePair2.Value;
				foreach (IIdentifierValue value4 in value2.Values)
				{
					IIdentifierValue val6 = value4;
					if (val6.Identifier.Id == 1)
					{
						continue;
					}
					IIdentifier val7 = null;
					IIdentifierValue val8 = null;
					myCompoundManager.GetIdentifier(val6.Identifier.Name, ref val7);
					if (val7 == null)
					{
						myCompoundManager.AddIdentifier(val6.Identifier.Name, val6.Identifier.Description, val6.Identifier.AllowMultiples, ref val7);
					}
					else if (flag)
					{
						myCompoundManager.UpdateIdentifier(ref val7, val6.Identifier.Name, val6.Identifier.Description, val6.Identifier.AllowMultiples);
					}
					myCompoundManager.GetIdentifierValue(key4, val7, ref val8);
					try
					{
						if (val8 == null)
						{
							myCompoundManager.AddIdentifierValue(key4, val7, (string[])val6.ValueList, ref val8);
						}
						else if (flag)
						{
							myCompoundManager.UpdateIdentifierValue(ref val8, (string[])val6.ValueList);
						}
					}
					catch (Exception ex2)
					{
						m_ReaderLog.AddMessageToLog(ex2.Message);
					}
				}
				string text3 = "";
				if (myMolDataGateway.Databanks != null && ((ICollection)myMolDataGateway.Databanks).Count > 0)
				{
					myMolDataGateway.GetStructure(key4.Id, ref text3, 1);
				}
				if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(text3))
				{
					object obj3 = molStrTbl[key6];
					if (obj3 != null)
					{
						text3 = obj3.ToString();
						myMolDataManager.AddStructure(key4.Id, text3, 1);
					}
				}
				else if (flag)
				{
					myMolDataManager.RemoveStructure(key4.Id, 1);
					myMolDataManager.AddStructure(key4.Id, text3, 1);
				}
			}
		}
		return arrayList;
	}

	private void ResolveCompoundConflicts(IDatabase source, IDatabase destination, string compIdentifier, ArrayList compLst, ref ArrayList conflictingCompLst, ref Hashtable AddCompsInDestination, ref Hashtable UpdateCompsInDestination, ref Hashtable molStrTbl)

		//IL_048b: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
		//IL_0492: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)

		//IL_01cb: Expected O, but got Unknown

		//IL_023e: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
		//IL_0245: Expected O, but got Unknown

		//IL_056d: Expected O, but got Unknown
		//IL_09b6: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
		//IL_09bd: Expected O, but got Unknown
		//IL_0851: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
		//IL_0858: Expected O, but got Unknown
		//IL_0a2a: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)

		{
			ICompoundManager compoundManager = ((Database)source).CompoundManager;
			ICompoundManager compoundManager2 = ((Database)destination).CompoundManager;
			IMolDataManager molDataManager = ((ISupportsMolData)destination).MolDataManager;
			IMolData val = (IMolData)(object)((molDataManager is IMolData) ? molDataManager : null);
			IIdentifier val2 = null;
			IIdentifier val3 = null;
			ICompound val4 = null;
			ICompound val5 = null;
			ICollection collection = null;
			ICollection collection2 = null;
			string text = "";
			string text2 = "";
			string text3 = "";
			bool flag = true;
			bool flag2 = source.Alias.CompareTo(((IDatabase)myDatabase).Alias) == 0;
			compoundManager.GetIdentifier(compIdentifier, ref val2);
			compoundManager2.GetIdentifier(compIdentifier, ref val3);
			if (val2 != null && val3 != null)
			{
				if (val2.AllowMultiples != val3.AllowMultiples)
				{
					flag = false;
				}
				else if (val2.Name.ToLowerInvariant().CompareTo(val3.Name.ToLowerInvariant()) != 0)
				{
					flag = false;
				}
				if (!flag)
				{
					text3 = "Warning : compound identifier definition conflict was detected. Identifier '{0}'  from Database '{1}' does not match with Identifier from Database '{2}'";
					m_ReaderLog.AddMessageToLog(string.Format(text3, compIdentifier, source.Alias, destination.Alias));
				}
			}
			SortedList sortedList = new SortedList();
			SortedList sortedList2 = new SortedList();
			for (int i = 0; i < compLst.Count; i++)
			{
				compoundManager.GetCompound(compLst[i].ToString(), val2, ref val4);
				compoundManager2.GetCompound(compLst[i].ToString(), val3, ref val5);
				if (val4 == null && val5 != null)
				{
					if (!flag2)
					{
						continue;
					}
					string value = "";
					val.GetStructure(val5.Id, ref value, 1);
					if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(value) && !molStrTbl.ContainsKey(compLst[i]))
					{
						molStrTbl.Add(compLst[i], value);
					}
					compoundManager2.GetAllIdentifierValues(val5, ref collection2);
					object obj = AddCompsInDestination[val5.Compound1Name];
					if (obj == null)
					{
						Hashtable hashtable = new Hashtable();
						foreach (IIdentifierValue item in collection2)
						{
							IIdentifierValue val6 = item;
							if (val6.Identifier.Id == 0)
							{
								continue;
							}
							if (val6.Identifier.AllowMultiples)
							{
								if (!hashtable.Contains(val6.Identifier.Id))
								{
									hashtable.Add(val6.Identifier.Id, val6);
									continue;
								}
								IIdentifierValue addTo = (IIdentifierValue)hashtable[val6.Identifier.Id];
								IIdentifierValue value2 = AddAdditionalSynonyms(addTo, val6);
								hashtable.Remove(val6.Identifier.Id);
								hashtable.Add(val6.Identifier.Id, value2);
							}
							else if (!hashtable.Contains(val6.Identifier.Id))
							{
								hashtable.Add(val6.Identifier.Id, val6);
							}
						}
						KeyValuePair<ICompound, Hashtable> keyValuePair = new KeyValuePair<ICompound, Hashtable>(val5, hashtable);
						AddCompsInDestination.Add(val5.Compound1Name, keyValuePair);
						continue;
					}
					Hashtable value3 = ((KeyValuePair<ICompound, Hashtable>)obj).Value;
					foreach (IIdentifierValue item2 in collection2)
					{
						IIdentifierValue val7 = item2;
						if (val7.Identifier.Id == 0)
						{
							continue;
						}
						if (val7.Identifier.AllowMultiples)
						{
							if (!value3.Contains(val7.Identifier.Id))
							{

								continue;
							}
