							IIdentifierValue value4 = AddAdditionalSynonyms(addTo2, val7);
							value3.Remove(val7.Identifier.Id);
							value3.Add(val7.Identifier.Id, value4);
						}
						else if (!value3.Contains(val7.Identifier.Id))
						{
							value3.Add(val7.Identifier.Id, val7);
						}
					}
				}
				else
				{
					if (val4 == null || val5 == null)
					{
						continue;
					}
					flag = true;
					sortedList.Clear();
					sortedList2.Clear();
					text = "";
					text2 = "";
					if (val4.CompoundType != val5.CompoundType)
					{
						flag = false;
					}
					else if (val4.InteractionType != val5.InteractionType)
					{
						flag = false;
					}
					else
					{
						compoundManager.GetAllIdentifierValues(val4, ref collection);
						compoundManager2.GetAllIdentifierValues(val5, ref collection2);
						foreach (IIdentifierValue item3 in collection)
						{
							IIdentifierValue val8 = item3;
							if (val8.Identifier.AllowMultiples)
							{
								continue;
							}
							foreach (string value11 in val8.ValueList)
							{
								sortedList.Add(val8.Identifier.Id, value11);
							}
						}
						foreach (IIdentifierValue item4 in collection2)
						{
							IIdentifierValue val9 = item4;
							if (val9.Identifier.AllowMultiples)
							{
								continue;
							}
							foreach (string value12 in val9.ValueList)
							{
								sortedList2.Add(val9.Identifier.Id, value12);
							}
						}
						if (sortedList[2] != null && sortedList2[2] != null && sortedList[2].ToString().ToLowerInvariant().CompareTo(sortedList2[2].ToString().ToLowerInvariant()) != 0)
						{
							flag = false;
							text = sortedList[2].ToString();
							text2 = sortedList2[2].ToString();
						}
						else if (sortedList[3] != null && sortedList2[3] != null && sortedList[3].ToString().ToLowerInvariant().CompareTo(sortedList2[3].ToString().ToLowerInvariant()) != 0)
						{
							flag = false;
							text = sortedList[3].ToString();
							text2 = sortedList2[3].ToString();
						}
						else if (sortedList[4] != null && sortedList2[4] != null && sortedList[4].ToString().ToLowerInvariant().CompareTo(sortedList2[4].ToString().ToLowerInvariant()) != 0)
						{
							flag = false;
							text = sortedList[4].ToString();
							text2 = sortedList2[4].ToString();
						}
					}
					if (!flag)
					{
						conflictingCompLst.Add(compLst[i].ToString());
						text3 = "Error: Compound identifier '{0}' in source database '{1}' was found to have been used for different compound : '{2}' in destination database '{3}'.";
						m_ReaderLog.AddMessageToLog(string.Format(text3, text, source.Alias, text2, destination.Alias));
					}
					else
					{
						if (!flag2)
						{
							continue;
						}
						string value7 = "";
						val.GetStructure(val5.Id, ref value7, 1);
						if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(value7) && !molStrTbl.ContainsKey(compLst[i]))
						{
							molStrTbl.Add(compLst[i], value7);
						}
						object obj2 = UpdateCompsInDestination[val4.Compound1Name];
						if (obj2 == null)
						{
							Hashtable hashtable2 = new Hashtable();
							foreach (IIdentifierValue item5 in collection2)
							{
								IIdentifierValue val10 = item5;
								if (val10.Identifier.Id == 0)
								{
									continue;
								}
								if (val10.Identifier.AllowMultiples)
								{
									if (!hashtable2.Contains(val10.Identifier.Id))
									{
										hashtable2.Add(val10.Identifier.Id, val10);
										continue;
									}
									IIdentifierValue addTo3 = (IIdentifierValue)hashtable2[val10.Identifier.Id];
									IIdentifierValue value8 = AddAdditionalSynonyms(addTo3, val10);
									hashtable2.Remove(val10.Identifier.Id);
									hashtable2.Add(val10.Identifier.Id, value8);
								}
								else if (!hashtable2.Contains(val10.Identifier.Id))
								{
									hashtable2.Add(val10.Identifier.Id, val10);
								}
							}
							KeyValuePair<ICompound, Hashtable> keyValuePair2 = new KeyValuePair<ICompound, Hashtable>(val4, hashtable2);
							UpdateCompsInDestination.Add(val4.Compound1Name, keyValuePair2);

						{
							IIdentifierValue val11 = item6;

								}

									IIdentifierValue value10 = AddAdditionalSynonyms(addTo4, val11);
									value9.Remove(val11.Identifier.Id);
									value9.Add(val11.Identifier.Id, value10);
								}
							}
						}
					}
				}
			}
		}
		catch (Exception ex)
		{
			throw new GatewayException(ex.Message);
		}
	}

	private IIdentifierValue AddAdditionalSynonyms(IIdentifierValue addTo, IIdentifierValue addFrom)
	{
		//IL_00c2: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
		//IL_00c8: Expected O, but got Unknown
		IdentifierValueObject val = null;
		ArrayList arrayList = new ArrayList();
		foreach (string value in addTo.ValueList)
		{
			if (!arrayList.Contains(value))
			{
				arrayList.Add(value);
			}
		}
		foreach (string value2 in addFrom.ValueList)
		{
			if (!arrayList.Contains(value2))
			{
				arrayList.Add(value2);
			}
		}
		return (IIdentifierValue)new IdentifierValueObject(addTo.Compound, addTo.Identifier, (string[])arrayList.ToArray(typeof(string)));
	}

	private void AddDIPPRData(Hashtable IDatabaseHT, Hashtable dbankTbl, IDatabank destinationDbank, int dbankPos, string compIdentifier, string dbasedbank, string compound, string property)
	{
		//IL_0015: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
		//IL_001b: Expected O, but got Unknown
		//IL_0064: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
		//IL_006b: Expected O, but got Unknown
		//IL_00ea: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
		//IL_00f4: Expected O, but got Unknown
		object obj = IDatabaseHT[dbankPos];
		if (obj == null)
		{
			return;
		}
		Database val = (Database)obj;
		ICitationManager citationManager = val.CitationManager;
		Hashtable hashtable = (Hashtable)dbankTbl[val];
		if (hashtable == null)
		{
			return;
		}
		string[] array = dbasedbank.Split('.');
		if (array == null || array.Length != 2)
		{
			return;
		}
		IDatabank val2 = (IDatabank)hashtable[array[1]];
		PropertyDataInfoObject val3 = default(PropertyDataInfoObject);
		citationManager.GetDataInfo(val2, compound, compIdentifier, property, ref val3);
		if (val3 != null)
		{
			myCitationManager.AddDataInfo(destinationDbank, compound, compIdentifier, property, val3);
		}
		NotesObject val4 = default(NotesObject);
		citationManager.GetNote(val2, compound, compIdentifier, property, ref val4);
		if (val4 != null)
		{
			myCitationManager.AddNote(destinationDbank, compound, compIdentifier, property, val4);
		}
		ArrayList arrayList = default(ArrayList);
		citationManager.GetCitation(val2, compound, compIdentifier, property, ref arrayList);
		if (arrayList != null)
		{
			for (int i = 0; i < arrayList.Count; i++)
			{
				myCitationManager.AddCitation(destinationDbank, compound, compIdentifier, property, (PropertyCitationsObject)arrayList[i]);
			}
		}
	}

	public void DealWithPropertyValues(Hashtable IDatabaseHT, ArrayList dbaseDbankLst, Hashtable dbankTbl, ArrayList compLst, ArrayList propLst, string compIdentifier, Hashtable optionTbl, bool IsDataBeingMerged)

			{
				object obj = optionTbl["OVERWRITE-DATA"];
				if (obj != null && obj.ToString().CompareTo("YES") == 0)
				{
					flag = true;
				}
			}
			string text = "";
			dbaseDbankLst.Add("");
			compLst.Add("");

			int objectHandle = -1;

			int numOfElements = -1;

			}
			Optimized_ReleaseHandleFn(objectHandle);

			int compoundIndex = -1;
			StringBuilder databaseName = new StringBuilder();
			StringBuilder name = new StringBuilder();
			StringBuilder alias = new StringBuilder();

				bool flag2 = false;
				if (IsDataBeingMerged)
				{
					myPropertyValueManager.GetUnaryPropertyValue(myDatabank, array[compoundPosition], compIdentifier, array2[propertyPosition], ref val);
					if (val != null)
					{
						flag2 = true;

						{
							myPropertyValueManager.UpdatePropertyValue(ref val, true, (object)value, "", "", "", (ICitation)null, "");

						}
					}
				}
				if (!IsDataBeingMerged || !flag2)
				{
					myPropertyValueManager.AddPropertyValue(myDatabank, array[compoundPosition], compIdentifier, array2[propertyPosition], (object)value, "", "", "", (ICitation)null, "", ref val);
					if (val != null)
					{
						text = "Added new {0} property value for compound {1}.";
						m_ReaderLog.AddMessageToLog(string.Format(text, val.Property.Name, val.Compound.Compound1Name));
					}
					AddDIPPRData(IDatabaseHT, dbankTbl, myDatabank, databankPosition, compIdentifier, array3[databankPosition], array[compoundPosition], array2[propertyPosition]);
				}
				databankPosition = -1;
				compoundPosition = -1;

			}

				bool flag2 = false;
				double[] array5 = new double[valueSize];
				for (int m = 0; m < valueSize; m++)
				{
					array5[m] = array4[m];
				}
				if (IsDataBeingMerged)
				{
					myPropertyValueManager.GetUnaryPropertyValue(myDatabank, array[compoundPosition], compIdentifier, array2[propertyPosition], ref val2);
					if (val2 != null)
					{
						flag2 = true;
						if (flag)
						{
							myPropertyValueManager.UpdatePropertyValue(ref val2, true, (object)array5, "", "", "", (ICitation)null, "");
							text = "Updated {0} property value for compound {1}.";
							m_ReaderLog.AddMessageToLog(string.Format(text, val2.Property.Name, val2.Compound.Compound1Name));
							AddDIPPRData(IDatabaseHT, dbankTbl, myDatabank, databankPosition, compIdentifier, array3[databankPosition], array[compoundPosition], array2[propertyPosition]);
						}
						else
						{
							text = "Skipped already existing {0} property value for compound {1}.";
							m_ReaderLog.AddMessageToLog(string.Format(text, array2[propertyPosition], array[compoundPosition]));
						}
					}
				}
				if (!IsDataBeingMerged || !flag2)
				{
					myPropertyValueManager.AddPropertyValue(myDatabank, array[compoundPosition], compIdentifier, array2[propertyPosition], (object)array5, "", "", "", (ICitation)null, "", ref val2);
					if (val2 != null)
					{
						text = "Added new {0} property value for compound {1}.";
						m_ReaderLog.AddMessageToLog(string.Format(text, val2.Property.Name, val2.Compound.Compound1Name));
					}
					AddDIPPRData(IDatabaseHT, dbankTbl, myDatabank, databankPosition, compIdentifier, array3[databankPosition], array[compoundPosition], array2[propertyPosition]);
				}
				valueSize = -1;
				databankPosition = -1;
				compoundPosition = -1;
				propertyPosition = -1;
			}
			Optimized_ReleaseHandleFn(objectHandle);
			IpHelpInfoObject ipHelpInfoObject = new IpHelpInfoObject();
			ArrayList arrayList = new ArrayList();
			IPropertyValue val3 = null;
			int bCondensedForViewing = 1;
			num = Optimized_GetAllBinaryPropertyValuesFn(out objectHandle);
			int compound1Position;
			int compound2Position;
			for (int n = 0; n < num; n++)
			{
				Optimized_GetNextBinaryPropertyValueWithCondensedFlagFn(objectHandle, n, out valueSize, out databankPosition, out compound1Position, out compound2Position, out propertyPosition, array4, out bCondensedForViewing);
				bool flag2 = false;
				bool flag3 = false;
				double[] array6 = new double[valueSize];
				for (int num2 = 0; num2 < valueSize; num2++)
				{
					array6[num2] = array4[num2];
				}
				if (IsDataBeingMerged)
				{
					myPropertyValueManager.GetBinaryPropertyValue(myDatabank, array[compound1Position], array[compound2Position], compIdentifier, array2[propertyPosition], true, ref val3);
					if (val3 != null)
					{
						flag2 = true;
						if (flag)
						{
							myPropertyValueManager.UpdatePropertyValue(ref val3, true, (object)array6, "", "", "", (ICitation)null, "");
							text = "Updated {0} property value for compound {1} and compound {2}.";
							m_ReaderLog.AddMessageToLog(string.Format(text, val3.Property.Name, val3.Compound.Compound1Name, val3.Compound.Compound2Name));
							flag3 = true;
						}
						else
						{
							text = "Skipped already existing {0} property value for compound {1} and compound {2}.";
							m_ReaderLog.AddMessageToLog(string.Format(text, array2[propertyPosition], array[compound1Position], array[compound2Position]));
						}
					}
				}
				if (!IsDataBeingMerged || !flag2)
				{
					myPropertyValueManager.AddBinaryPropertyValue(myDatabank, array[compound1Position], array[compound2Position], compIdentifier, array2[propertyPosition], (object)array6, "", "", "", (ICitation)null, "", ref val3);
					if (val3 != null)
					{
						text = "Added new {0} binary property value for compound {1} and compound {2}.";
						m_ReaderLog.AddMessageToLog(string.Format(text, val3.Property.Name, val3.Compound.Compound1Name, val3.Compound.Compound2Name));
						flag3 = true;
						arrayList.Add(val3.Property);
					}
				}
				if (flag3 && val3 != null)
				{
					IDatabase database = (IDatabase)IDatabaseHT[databankPosition];
					((DatabaseObject)ipHelpInfoObject).Database = database;
					string text2 = array3[databankPosition];
					if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(text2))
					{
						string[] array7 = text2.Split('.');
						if (array7 != null && array7.Length == 2 && ipHelpInfoObject.FetchExistingHelpInfo(array7[1], val3.Property.Name, val3.Compound.Compound1Name, val3.Compound.Identifier.Name, val3.Compound.Compound2Name, val3.Compound.Identifier.Name, out var _))
						{
							IpHelpInfoObject ipHelpInfoObject2 = new IpHelpInfoObject((IDatabase)(object)myDatabase, ipHelpInfoObject.ParamType, myDatabank.Name, val3.Property.Name, val3.Compound.Compound1Name, val3.Compound.Identifier.Name, val3.Compound.Compound2Name, val3.Compound.Identifier.Name);
							ipHelpInfoObject2.HenryInfo = ipHelpInfoObject.HenryInfo;
							ipHelpInfoObject2.LleInfo = ipHelpInfoObject.LleInfo;
							ipHelpInfoObject2.VleInfo = ipHelpInfoObject.VleInfo;
							mySdm.UpdateItem((IDatabaseObject)(object)ipHelpInfoObject2);
						}
					}
				}
				valueSize = -1;
				databankPosition = -1;
				compound1Position = -1;
				compound2Position = -1;
				propertyPosition = -1;
			}
			Optimized_ReleaseHandleFn(objectHandle);
			Optimized_UnInitializeAllListsFn();
			int interactionType = 3;
			bCondensedForViewing = 2;
			int num3 = BinaryPropertyValueObjects_GetAllBinaryPropertyValueObjectsFn(array, compIdentifier, array3, array2, out interactionType, out bCondensedForViewing, out objectHandle);
			if (num3 > 0)
			{
				num = BinaryPropertyValueObjects_NumberOfElementsFn(objectHandle);
				IPropertyValue val4 = default(IPropertyValue);
				for (int num4 = 0; num4 < num; num4++)
				{
					BinaryPropertyValueObjects_GetNextPropertyValueObject_APlusFn(objectHandle, out compound1Position, out compound2Position, out var comp3Index, out var comp4Index, out databankPosition, out propertyPosition, out valueSize, array4);
					bool flag2 = false;
					double[] array8 = new double[valueSize];
					for (int num5 = 0; num5 < valueSize; num5++)
					{
						array8[num5] = array4[num5];
					}
					string text3 = ((compound2Position != -1) ? array[compound2Position] : "");
					string text4 = ((comp4Index != -1) ? array[comp4Index] : "");
					if (IsDataBeingMerged)
					{
						myPropertyValueManager.GetPairPropertyValue(myDatabank, array[compound1Position], text3, array[comp3Index], text4, compIdentifier, array2[propertyPosition], true, ref val4);
						if (val4 != null)
						{
							flag2 = true;
							if (flag)
							{
								myPropertyValueManager.UpdatePropertyValue(ref val4, true, (object)array8, "", "", "", (ICitation)null, "");
								text = "Updated {0} property value for compound pair {1} , {2} with {3} , {4}.";
								m_ReaderLog.AddMessageToLog(string.Format(text, val4.Property.Name, val4.Compound.Compound1Name, val4.Compound.Compound2Name, val4.Compound.Compound3Name, val4.Compound.Compound4Name));
							}
							else
							{
								text = "Skipped already existing {0} property value for compound pair {1} , {2} with {3}, {4}.";
								m_ReaderLog.AddMessageToLog(string.Format(text, array2[propertyPosition], array[compound1Position], text3, array[comp3Index], text4));
							}
						}
					}
					if (!IsDataBeingMerged || !flag2)
					{
						myPropertyValueManager.AddPairPropertyValue(myDatabank, array[compound1Position], text3, array[comp3Index], text4, compIdentifier, array2[propertyPosition], (object)array8, "", "", "", (ICitation)null, "", ref val4);
						if (val4 != null)
						{
							text = "Added new {0} pair property value for compound pair {1}, {2} with {3}, {4}.";
							m_ReaderLog.AddMessageToLog(string.Format(text, val4.Property.Name, val4.Compound.Compound1Name, val4.Compound.Compound2Name, val4.Compound.Compound3Name, val4.Compound.Compound4Name));
						}
					}
					valueSize = -1;
					databankPosition = -1;
					compound1Position = -1;
					compound2Position = -1;
					comp3Index = -1;
					comp4Index = -1;
					propertyPosition = -1;
				}
			}
			UpdatePureDatabankInfo(myDatabank);
			UpdateInteractionParameterDatabankInfo(arrayList, myDatabank, "ALL", "ALL");
			dbaseDbankLst.Remove("");
			compLst.Remove("");
			propLst.Remove("");
			FreeLibrary(functionPointers);
		}
		catch (Exception ex)
		{
			throw ex;
		}
	}

	public void DealWithReactions(Hashtable IDatabaseHT, ArrayList dbaseDbankLst, Hashtable dbankTbl, ArrayList compLst, string compIdentifier, Hashtable optionTbl)
	{
		//IL_007d: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
		//IL_0084: Expected O, but got Unknown
		//IL_0086: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
		//IL_0093: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
		//IL_00e2: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
		//IL_00e9: Expected O, but got Unknown
		//IL_018b: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
		//IL_0192: Expected O, but got Unknown
		string text = "";
		ArrayList arrayList = new ArrayList();
		IIdentifier val = default(IIdentifier);
		myCompoundManager.GetIdentifier(compIdentifier, ref val);
		for (int i = 0; i < dbaseDbankLst.Count; i++)
		{
			arrayList.Clear();
			string text2 = (string)dbaseDbankLst[i];
			string[] array = text2.Split('.');
			if (array == null || array.Length != 2)
			{
				continue;
			}
			arrayList.Add(array[1].ToString());
			IDatabase val2 = (IDatabase)IDatabaseHT[i];
			IReactionManager reactionManager = ((Database)val2).ReactionManager;
			ICompoundManager compoundManager = ((Database)val2).CompoundManager;
			ArrayList arrayList2 = new ArrayList();
			if (reactionManager == null)
			{
				continue;
			}
			FindElectrolyicRxns(reactionManager, compLst, compIdentifier, arrayList, out var rxnTbl);
			if (rxnTbl == null || rxnTbl.Count <= 0)
			{
				continue;
			}
			foreach (ReactionObject value2 in rxnTbl.Values)
			{
				ReactionObject val3 = value2;
				foreach (string synonym in val3.Synonyms)
				{
					arrayList2.Add(synonym);
				}
			}
			ArrayList arrayList3 = new ArrayList();
			arrayList3.Add(val2);
			ArrayList arrayList4 = DealWithCompounds(arrayList3, arrayList2, compIdentifier, optionTbl);
			foreach (ReactionObject value3 in rxnTbl.Values)
			{
				ReactionObject val4 = value3;
				bool flag = false;
				foreach (string synonym2 in val4.Synonyms)
				{
					if (arrayList4.Contains(synonym2))
					{
						flag = true;
						text = "Reaction : {0} can not be added because it contains a compound {1} with conflicting identifiers in destination database.";
						m_ReaderLog.AddMessageToLog(string.Format(text, val4.Name, synonym2));
					}
				}
				if (!flag)
				{
					IReaction val5 = null;
					myReactionManager.AddReaction(myDatabank, val4.Name, val4.Type, (ICollection)val4.Synonyms, (ICollection)val4.Coefficients, (ICollection)val4.Exponents, (ICollection)val4.EqConsts, val4.Basis, val4.Phase, val4.EquationType, val4.SymmetricEqConsts, val4.SymmetricEqConstBasis, ref val5);
					if (val5 != null)
					{
						text = "Added a new reaction with name : {0}";
						m_ReaderLog.AddMessageToLog(string.Format(text, val5.Name));
					}
				}
			}
		}
	}

	public void FindElectrolyicRxns(IReactionManager sourceRxnMgr, ArrayList originalCompList, string compIdentifier, ArrayList dbankList, out Hashtable rxnTbl)
	{
		//IL_003e: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
		//IL_0045: Expected O, but got Unknown
		//IL_0074: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
		//IL_007b: Expected O, but got Unknown
		Hashtable hashtable = new Hashtable();
		int num = -1;
		while (originalCompList.Count != num)
		{
			num = originalCompList.Count;
			ICollection collection = null;
			sourceRxnMgr.GetCompoundsReactions((ICollection)originalCompList, compIdentifier, (ICollection)dbankList, ref collection);
			if (collection == null)
			{
				continue;
			}
			foreach (IReaction item in collection)
			{
				IReaction val = item;
				if (!isValidReaction(val, originalCompList, originalCompList))
				{
					continue;
				}
				if (!hashtable.ContainsKey(val.Name))
				{
					hashtable.Add(val.Name, val);
				}
				ReactionObject val2 = (ReactionObject)val;
				for (int i = 0; i < val2.Synonyms.Count; i++)
				{
					if (val2.Type == "DISS")
					{
						if ((double)val2.Coefficients[i] > 0.0 && !originalCompList.Contains(val2.Synonyms[i]))
						{
							originalCompList.Add(val2.Synonyms[i]);
						}
					}
					else if (!originalCompList.Contains(val2.Synonyms[i]))
					{
						originalCompList.Add(val2.Synonyms[i]);
					}
				}
			}
		}
		rxnTbl = hashtable;
	}

	private bool isValidReaction(IReaction rn, ArrayList compoundList, ArrayList originalCompList)
	{
		//IL_0001: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
		//IL_0007: Expected O, but got Unknown
		ReactionObject val = (ReactionObject)rn;
		bool result = true;
		if (rn.Type == "DISS")
		{
			for (int i = 0; i < val.Synonyms.Count; i++)
			{
				if ((double)val.Coefficients[i] < 0.0 && !originalCompList.Contains(val.Synonyms[i]))
				{
					result = false;
					break;
				}
			}
		}
		else
		{
			bool flag = true;
			bool flag2 = true;
			for (int j = 0; j < val.Synonyms.Count; j++)
			{
				if ((double)val.Coefficients[j] < 0.0 && !compoundList.Contains(val.Synonyms[j]))
				{
					flag = false;
				}
				if ((double)val.Coefficients[j] > 0.0 && !compoundList.Contains(val.Synonyms[j]))
				{
					flag2 = false;
				}
			}
			if (!flag && !flag2)
			{
				result = false;
			}
		}
		return result;
	}

	private void UpdatePureDatabankInfo(IDatabank DBank)
	{
		try
		{
			ISundryDataManager sundryDataManager = ((ISupportsSundryData)myDatabase).SundryDataManager;
			string sundryDataTableEntryName = PureDatabankInfoObject.GetSundryDataTableEntryName(DBank.Name);
			IDatabaseObject item = sundryDataManager.GetItem(sundryDataTableEntryName);
			PureDatabankInfoObject pureDatabankInfoObject;
			if (item != null)
			{
				pureDatabankInfoObject = (PureDatabankInfoObject)(object)item;
				if (!myPropertyValueManager.CheckDatabankHasPurePropertyValues(DBank))
				{
					sundryDataManager.DeleteItem(sundryDataTableEntryName);
				}
				return;
			}
			pureDatabankInfoObject = new PureDatabankInfoObject(DBank.Name);
			pureDatabankInfoObject.DatabankType = DBank.Name;
			pureDatabankInfoObject.DisplayNameMM = DBank.Name;
			pureDatabankInfoObject.Password = DBank.Name;
			pureDatabankInfoObject.Product = "";
			if (DBank.Description != null)
			{
				pureDatabankInfoObject.PromptMM = DBank.Description;
			}
			else
			{
				pureDatabankInfoObject.PromptMM = DBank.Name;
			}
			sundryDataManager.UpdateItem((IDatabaseObject)(object)pureDatabankInfoObject);
		}
		catch
		{
		}
	}

	private void UpdateInteractionParameterDatabankInfo(ArrayList ipropLst, IDatabank DBank, string EosModel, string GammaModel)
	{
		//IL_0057: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
		try
		{
			ISundryDataManager sundryDataManager = ((ISupportsSundryData)myDatabase).SundryDataManager;
			string name = DBank.Name;
			IDatabaseObject item = sundryDataManager.GetItem(name);
			InteractionParameterDatabankInformationObject interactionParameterDatabankInformationObject;
			ArrayList parameterInfoList;
			if (item != null)
			{
				interactionParameterDatabankInformationObject = (InteractionParameterDatabankInformationObject)(object)item;
				parameterInfoList = interactionParameterDatabankInformationObject.ParameterInfoList;
			}
			else
			{
				interactionParameterDatabankInformationObject = new InteractionParameterDatabankInformationObject(name, null);
				parameterInfoList = interactionParameterDatabankInformationObject.ParameterInfoList;
			}
			for (int i = 0; i < ipropLst.Count; i++)
			{
				parameterInfoList.Add(new InteractionParameterDatabankInformationObject.GammaModelParameterInformation
				{
					ParameterName = ((IProperty)ipropLst[i]).Name,
					VaporModelName = EosModel,
					GammaModelName = GammaModel
				});
			}
			sundryDataManager.UpdateItem((IDatabaseObject)(object)interactionParameterDatabankInformationObject);
		}
		catch
		{
		}
	}

	public void AddPropertyValue(IDatabank dbank, string compound, string identifierType, string property, object valArr, string uncertainty, string uncertaintySource, string dataSourceType, ICitation citation, string notes, out IPropertyValue propertyValue)
	{
		myPropertyValueManager.AddPropertyValue(dbank, compound, identifierType, property, valArr, uncertainty, uncertaintySource, dataSourceType, citation, notes, ref propertyValue);
	}
}
public class DFMSconsts
{
	public const int NUMPKW = 23;

	public const int COPY_PKW = 0;

	public const int CLEANUP_PKW = 1;

	public const int DELETE_PKW = 2;

	public const int DESCRIPTION_PKW = 3;

	public const int ENDINPUT_PKW = 4;

	public const int FILE_PKW = 5;

	public const int NEWCOMP_PKW = 6;

	public const int NEWPROP_PKW = 7;

	public const int NOECHO_PKW = 8;

	public const int PRINTDATA_PKW = 9;

	public const int PRINTDIR_PKW = 10;

	public const int PROPDATA_PKW = 11;

	public const int TITLE_PKW = 12;

	public const int WRFILE_PKW = 13;

	public const int END_INPUT_PKW = 14;

	public const int _NOECHO_PKW = 15;

	public const int _CLEANUP_PKW = 16;

	public const int _DESC_PKW = 17;

	public const int _NEWCOMP_PKW = 18;

	public const int _NEWPROP_PKW = 19;

	public const int _PRINTDA_PKW = 20;

	public const int _PRINTDI_PKW = 21;

	public const int _PROPDATA_PKW = 22;

	public static readonly string[] pPKWDS = new string[24]
	{
		"COPY", "CLEANUP", "DELETE", "DESCRIPTION", "ENDINPUT", "FILE", "NEW-COMP", "NEW-PROP", "NO-ECHO", "PRINTDATA",
		"PRINTDIR", "PROP-DATA", "TITLE", "WRFILE", "END-INPUT", "NOECHO", "CLEAN-UP", "DESC", "NEWCOMP", "NEWPROP",
		"PRINT-DA", "PRINT-DI", "PROPDATA", null
	};

	public const int NUMPDATA_SKW = 7;

	public const int PROPLIST_PDSKW = 0;

	public const int PVAL_PDSKW = 1;

	public const int BPVAL_PDSKW = 2;

	public const int COMPLIST_PDSKW = 3;

	public const int CVAL_PDSKW = 4;

	public const int BCVAL_PDSKW = 5;

	public const int NEWPROP_PDSKW = 6;

	public static readonly string[] pPDSKWDS = new string[8] { "PROP-LIST", "PVAL", "BPVAL", "COMP-LIST", "CVAL", "BCVAL", "NEW-PROP", null };

	public static readonly string[] PureDatabankTypes = new string[7] { "NONE", "USRPP1", "USRPP2", "INHSPCD", "INHSSOL", "INHSAQUS", null };

	public static readonly string[] BaseDatabankArray = new string[11]
	{
		"", "PURE856", "PURE93", "PURE10", "PURE11", "PURE12", "PURE13", "", "AQU92", "AQUEOUS",
		null
	};
}
public class DFMSreader
{
	private InputFileReader m_IFR;

	private ReaderLog m_ReaderLog;

	private DBwriter m_DBwriter;

	private ArrayList m_PropListList;

	private ArrayList m_CompListList;

	private SortedList propNumElemsList;

	public DFMSreader(ReaderLog readerLog, DBwriter dbWriter)
	{
		m_ReaderLog = readerLog;
		m_DBwriter = dbWriter;
		m_CompListList = new ArrayList();
		m_PropListList = new ArrayList();
		m_CompListList.Clear();
		m_PropListList.Clear();
		propNumElemsList = new SortedList();
	}

	public void OpenDFMSFile(string DFMSFileName)
	{
		m_IFR = new InputFileReader(m_ReaderLog);
		m_IFR.OpenFile(DFMSFileName);
	}

	public void CloseDFMSFile()
	{
		m_IFR.CloseFile();
	}

	private int GetNextValidLine()
	{
		int num = -1;
		do
		{
			num = m_IFR.GetALine();
		}
		while (num != -1 && (m_IFR.CurrentLine.StartsWith(";") || m_IFR.CurrentLine.CompareTo("") == 0));
		return num;
	}

	private int GetPKeyWord()
	{
		Tokens tokens = new Tokens(m_IFR.CurrentLineCopy, Tokens.delimiterNormal);
		IEnumerator enumerator = tokens.GetEnumerator();
		if (!enumerator.MoveNext())
		{
			return -1;
		}
		for (int i = 0; i < 23; i++)
		{
			int length = DFMSconsts.pPKWDS[i].Length;
			if (string.Compare((string)enumerator.Current, 0, DFMSconsts.pPKWDS[i], 0, length, ignoreCase: true) == 0)
			{
				return i;
			}
		}
		return -1;
	}

	private int GetPDSKeyWord()
	{
		Tokens tokens = new Tokens(m_IFR.CurrentLineCopy, Tokens.delimiterNormal);
		IEnumerator enumerator = tokens.GetEnumerator();
		if (!enumerator.MoveNext())
		{
			return -1;
		}
		for (int i = 0; i < 7; i++)
		{
			int length = DFMSconsts.pPDSKWDS[i].Length;
			if (string.Compare((string)enumerator.Current, 0, DFMSconsts.pPDSKWDS[i], 0, length, ignoreCase: true) == 0)
			{
				return i;
			}
		}
		return -1;
	}

	private int CheckConsistentPassword(string pwd)
	{
		if (pwd.Length == 0)
		{
			m_ReaderLog.AddMessageToLog("Error: DFMS input file " + m_IFR.FileName + " did not specify password of WRFILE, line " + m_IFR.CurrentLineNo);
			return -1;
		}
		if (m_DBwriter.Password.Length > 0)
		{
			return 0;
		}
		m_DBwriter.Password = pwd;
		return 0;
	}

	public int Process(ref string errMessage)
	{
		errMessage = null;
		int num = -1;
		int num2 = -1;
		num2 = GetNextValidLine();
		if (num2 == -1)
		{
			m_ReaderLog.AddMessageToLog("Error: End of file at the beginning of " + m_IFR.FileName);
			return -1;
		}
		while (num2 != -1)
		{
			num = -1;
			if (!m_IFR.CurrentLine.StartsWith(" "))
			{
				num = GetPKeyWord();
				if (num == -1)
				{
					m_ReaderLog.AddMessageToLog($"Fatal error has occurred. Specified keyword at line {m_IFR.CurrentLineNo} in file {m_IFR.FileName} is not recognized as correct DFMS keyword. Please make sure your legacy input file has correct DFMS specification");
					throw new GatewayException($"Fatal error has occurred. Specified keyword at line {m_IFR.CurrentLineNo} in file {m_IFR.FileName} is not recognized as correct DFMS keyword. Please make sure your legacy input file has correct DFMS specification");
				}
			}
			switch (num)
			{
			case 0:
				num2 = ProcessCopy();
				if (num2 == -1)
				{
					return num2;
				}
				break;
			case 1:
			case 16:
				num2 = ProcessCleanUp();
				if (num2 == -1)
				{
					return num2;
				}
				break;
			case 2:
				num2 = ProcessDelete();
				if (num2 == -1)
				{
					return num2;
				}
				break;
			case 3:
			case 17:
				num2 = ProcessDescription();
				if (num2 == -1)
				{
					return num2;
				}
				break;
			case 4:
			case 14:
				num2 = ProcessEndInput();
				if (num2 == -1)
				{
					return num2;
				}
				break;
			case 5:
				num2 = ProcessFile(ref errMessage);
				if (num2 == -1)
				{
					return num2;
				}
				break;
			case 6:
			case 18:
				num2 = ProcessNewComp(ref errMessage);
				if (num2 == -1)
				{
					return num2;
				}
				break;
			case 7:
			case 19:
				num2 = ProcessNewProp();
				if (num2 == -1)
				{
					return num2;
				}
				break;
			case 8:
			case 15:
				num2 = ProcessNoEcho();
				if (num2 == -1)
				{
					return num2;
				}
				break;
			case 9:
			case 20:
				num2 = ProcessPrintData();
				if (num2 == -1)
				{
					return num2;
				}
				break;
			case 10:
			case 21:
				num2 = ProcessPrintDir();
				if (num2 == -1)
				{
					return num2;
				}
				break;
			case 11:
			case 22:
				num2 = ProcessPropData();
				if (num2 == -1)
				{
					return num2;
				}
				break;
			case 12:
				num2 = ProcessTitle();
				if (num2 == -1)
				{
					return num2;
				}
				break;
			case 13:
				num2 = ProcessWrfile(ref errMessage);
				if (num2 == -1)
				{
					return num2;
				}
				break;
			default:
				num2 = GetNextValidLine();
				if (num2 == -1)
				{
					return num2;
				}
				break;
			}
		}
		return 0;
	}

	private int ProcessFile(ref string errMessage)
	{
		errMessage = null;
		Tokens tokens = new Tokens(m_IFR.CurrentLineCopy, Tokens.delimiterNormal);
		if (tokens.Length < 3)
		{
			m_ReaderLog.AddMessageToLog("Error: DFMS input file " + m_IFR.FileName + " did not specify FILE type and password, line " + m_IFR.CurrentLineNo);
			return -1;
		}
		IEnumerator enumerator = tokens.GetEnumerator();
		if (!enumerator.MoveNext())
		{
			return -1;
		}
		if (!enumerator.MoveNext())
		{
			return -1;
		}
		m_DBwriter.DatabankType = (string)enumerator.Current;
		if (!enumerator.MoveNext())
		{
			return -1;
		}
		m_DBwriter.Password = (string)enumerator.Current;
		if (m_DBwriter.Password.Length > 9)
		{
			errMessage = "Fatal error: databank name \"" + m_DBwriter.Password + "\" exceeds nine characters in " + m_IFR.FileName + ".";
			m_ReaderLog.AddMessageToLog("Fatal error: databank name \"" + m_DBwriter.Password + "\" exceeds nine characters in " + m_IFR.FileName + ".");
			return -1;
		}
		int num = 0;
		if (tokens.Length > 3)
		{
			if (!enumerator.MoveNext())
			{
				return -1;
			}
			if (enumerator.Current.ToString() == "/")
			{
				num = 4;
			}
			if (string.Compare((string)enumerator.Current, 0, "new", 0, 3, ignoreCase: true) == 0)
			{
				m_DBwriter.CreateNewDBank(m_DBwriter.DatabankType, m_DBwriter.Password);
			}
			else if (m_DBwriter.GetOldDBank(m_DBwriter.Password) == null)
			{
				m_ReaderLog.AddMessageToLog($"Fatal error has occurred. Specified databank {m_DBwriter.Password} doesn't exist in the database. Please make sure your legacy input files are listed in proper order.\nLegacy file with new databank specification must be converted first before converting other files from this same databank.");
				throw new GatewayException($"Fatal error has occurred. Specified databank {m_DBwriter.Password} doesn't exist in the database. Please make sure your legacy input files are listed in proper order.\nLegacy file with new databank specification must be converted first before converting other files from this same databank.\nData Conversion will terminate now.");
			}
		}
		else if (tokens.Length > 2 && m_DBwriter.GetOldDBank(m_DBwriter.Password) == null)
		{
			m_ReaderLog.AddMessageToLog($"Fatal error has occurred. Specified databank {m_DBwriter.Password} doesn't exist in the database. Please make sure your legacy input files are listed in proper order.\nLegacy file with new databank specification must be converted first before converting other files from this same databank.");
			throw new GatewayException($"Fatal error has occurred. Specified databank {m_DBwriter.Password} doesn't exist in the database. Please make sure your legacy input files are listed in proper order.\nLegacy file with new databank specification must be converted first before converting other files from this same databank.\nData Conversion will terminate now.");
		}
		if (num > 0)
		{
			if (-1 == ProcessNextDatabankInFileKeyword(num, tokens))
			{
				return -1;
			}
		}
		else if (tokens.Length > 4 && tokens.GetAt(4) == "/")
		{
			num = 5;
			if (-1 == ProcessNextDatabankInFileKeyword(num, tokens))
			{
				return -1;
			}
		}
		return GetNextValidLine();
	}

	private int ProcessNextDatabankInFileKeyword(int startOfNextDatabankSlash, Tokens tokens)
	{
		return 0;
	}

	private int ProcessNewComp(ref string errMessage)
	{
		int num = 256;
		int num2 = 12;
		int num3 = 1;
		int num4 = 0;
		string text = "";
		string text2 = "";
		string text3 = "";
		string text4 = "";
		bool bNewComp = true;
		while (true)
		{
			string source = m_IFR.CurrentLineCopy;
			Tokens tokens = new Tokens(ref source, Tokens.delimiterSlash, "'", "'", bNewComp);
			m_IFR.CurrentLineCopy = source;
			int length = tokens.Length;
			int num5 = ((num3 == 1) ? 1 : 0);
			while (num5 < length)
			{
				if (text2.Length == 0)
				{
					string text5 = tokens.GetAt(num5);
					string text6 = m_IFR.CurrentLineCopy.Trim();
					int num6 = text6.ToLowerInvariant().IndexOf("new-comp");
					if (num6 != -1)
					{
						text6 = text6.Substring(num6 + 8);
					}
					if (text6.TrimEnd().EndsWith("/"))
					{
						int startIndex = text6.LastIndexOf("/");
						text6 = text6.Remove(startIndex);
					}
					text6 = text6.TrimStart().TrimEnd();
					if (text6.Trim().StartsWith("(") && text6.Trim().EndsWith(")"))
					{
						num4 = 2;
						if (text5.StartsWith("("))
						{
							int num7 = m_IFR.CurrentLineCopy.IndexOf(text5);
							if (num7 == 0 || m_IFR.CurrentLineCopy.Substring(num7 - 1, 1) != "'")
							{
								text5 = text5.Remove(0, 1);
							}
						}
					}
					else
					{
						num4 = 1;
					}
					text5 = UtilityFuncs.StripSingleQuotes(text5);
					text2 = text5;
					if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(text2) && text2.Length > num)
					{
						errMessage = "Fatal error: compound name \"" + text2 + "\" exceeds " + num + " characters in " + m_IFR.FileName + ".";
						m_ReaderLog.AddMessageToLog(errMessage);
						return -1;
					}
					num5++;
				}
				if (num5 >= length)
				{
					continue;
				}
				if (num4 == 2)
				{
					if (text4.Length == 0)
					{
						string text7 = tokens.GetAt(num5);
						if (text7.EndsWith(")"))
						{
							int num8 = m_IFR.CurrentLineCopy.IndexOf(text7) + text7.Length;
							if (num8 == m_IFR.CurrentLineCopy.Length || m_IFR.CurrentLineCopy.Substring(num8, 1) != "'")
							{
								text7 = text7.Remove(text7.Length - 1, 1);
							}
						}
						text7 = UtilityFuncs.StripSingleQuotes(text7);
						text4 = text7;
						if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(text4) && text4.Length > num)
						{
							errMessage = "Fatal error: compound name \"" + text4 + "\" exceeds " + num + " characters in " + m_IFR.FileName + ".";
							m_ReaderLog.AddMessageToLog(errMessage);
							return -1;
						}
						num5++;
					}
					if (num5 >= length)
					{
						continue;
					}
					if (text.Length == 0)
					{
						string text8 = tokens.GetAt(num5);
						if (text8.StartsWith("("))
						{
							int num9 = m_IFR.CurrentLineCopy.IndexOf(text8);
							if (num9 == 0 || m_IFR.CurrentLineCopy.Substring(num9 - 1, 1) != "'")
							{
								text8 = text8.Remove(0, 1);
							}
						}
						text8 = UtilityFuncs.StripSingleQuotes(text8);
						text = text8;
						if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(text) && text.Length > num2)
						{
							errMessage = "Fatal error: compound alias \"" + text + "\" exceeds " + num2 + " characters in " + m_IFR.FileName + ".";
							m_ReaderLog.AddMessageToLog(errMessage);
							return -1;
						}
						num5++;
					}
					if (num5 >= length)
					{
						continue;
					}
					if (text3.Length == 0)
					{
						string text9 = tokens.GetAt(num5);
						if (text9.EndsWith(")"))
						{
							int num10 = m_IFR.CurrentLineCopy.IndexOf(text9) + text9.Length;
							if (num10 == m_IFR.CurrentLineCopy.Length || m_IFR.CurrentLineCopy.Substring(num10, 1) != "'")
							{
								text9 = text9.Remove(text9.Length - 1, 1);
							}
						}
						text9 = UtilityFuncs.StripSingleQuotes(text9);
						text3 = text9;
						if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(text3) && text3.Length > num2)
						{
							errMessage = "Fatal error: compound alias \"" + text3 + "\" exceeds " + num2 + " characters in " + m_IFR.FileName + ".";
							m_ReaderLog.AddMessageToLog(errMessage);
							return -1;
						}
						num5++;
					}
					m_DBwriter.AddPureCompound(text2, text);
					m_DBwriter.AddPureCompound(text4, text3);
					m_DBwriter.AddBinaryComponent(text2, text, text4, text3);
					text2 = "";
					text = "";
					text4 = "";
					text3 = "";
					num4 = 0;
				}
				else
				{
					string at = tokens.GetAt(num5);
					at = UtilityFuncs.StripSingleQuotes(at);
					text = at;
					if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(text) && text.Length > num2)
					{
						errMessage = "Fatal error: compound alias \"" + text + "\" exceeds " + num2 + " characters in " + m_IFR.FileName + ".";
						m_ReaderLog.AddMessageToLog(errMessage);
						return -1;
					}
					num5++;
					m_DBwriter.AddPureCompound(text2, text);
					text2 = "";
					text = "";
					num4 = 0;
				}
			}
			if (-1 == GetNextValidLine())
			{
				return -1;
			}
			if (!m_IFR.CurrentLineCopy.StartsWith(" ") && !m_IFR.CurrentLineCopy.StartsWith("\n") && !(m_IFR.CurrentLineCopy == "") && !m_IFR.CurrentLineCopy.StartsWith("\t"))
			{
				break;
			}
			num3++;
		}
		return 0;
	}

	private int ProcessNewProp()
	{
		int num = 1;
		int num2 = 0;
		int num3 = 1;
		string text = "";
		while (true)
		{
			Tokens tokens = new Tokens(m_IFR.CurrentLineCopy, Tokens.delimiterSlash);
			num2 = tokens.Length;
			int num4 = ((num3 == 1) ? 1 : 0);
			while (num4 < num2)
			{
				if (text == "")
				{
					string at = tokens.GetAt(num4);
					at = UtilityFuncs.StripSingleQuotes(at);
					text = at;
					num4++;
				}
				if (num4 < num2)
				{
					num = Convert.ToInt32(tokens.GetAt(num4));
					num4++;
					propNumElemsList.Add(text, num);
					if (text.ToUpperInvariant().CompareTo("SRKAIJ") != 0 && text.ToUpperInvariant().CompareTo("SRKBIJ") != 0)
					{
						m_DBwriter.AddProperty(text, "Aspen Plus Parameter " + text, propIsGlobal: false, (PropertyDataType)1, (InteractionType)0, (CompoundType)0, num, null, null, null);
					}
					text = "";
				}
			}
			if (-1 == GetNextValidLine())
			{
				return -1;
			}
			if (!m_IFR.CurrentLineCopy.StartsWith(" ") && !m_IFR.CurrentLineCopy.StartsWith("\n") && !(m_IFR.CurrentLineCopy == "") && !m_IFR.CurrentLineCopy.StartsWith("\t"))
			{
				break;
			}
			num3++;
		}
		return 0;
	}

	private int ProcessPropData()
	{
		if (-1 == GetNextValidLine())
		{
			return -1;
		}
		while (true)
		{
			int num = -1;
			switch (GetPDSKeyWord())
			{
			case 0:
			{
				int nextValidLine = ProcessSKWPropList();
				if (nextValidLine == -1)
				{
					return nextValidLine;
				}
				break;
			}
			case 1:
			{
				int nextValidLine = ProcessSKWPval();
				if (nextValidLine == -1)
				{
					return nextValidLine;
				}
				break;
			}
			case 2:
			{
				int nextValidLine = ProcessSKWBpval();
				if (nextValidLine == -1)
				{
					return nextValidLine;
				}
				break;
			}
			case 3:
			{
				int nextValidLine = ProcessSKWComplist();
				if (nextValidLine == -1)
				{
					return nextValidLine;
				}
				break;
			}
			case 4:
			{
				int nextValidLine = ProcessSKWCval();
				if (nextValidLine == -1)
				{
					return nextValidLine;
				}
				break;
			}
			case 5:
			{
				int nextValidLine = ProcessSKWBcval();
				if (nextValidLine == -1)
				{
					return nextValidLine;
				}
				break;
			}
			case 6:
				return 0;
			default:
			{
				int nextValidLine = GetNextValidLine();
				if (nextValidLine == -1)
				{
					return nextValidLine;
				}
				break;
			}
			}
		}
	}

	private int ProcessTitle()
	{
		int num = m_IFR.CurrentLineCopy.IndexOf("'");
		if (num > 0)
		{
			m_IFR.CurrentLineCopy = m_IFR.CurrentLineCopy.Remove(0, num);
			m_IFR.CurrentLineCopy = UtilityFuncs.StripSingleQuotes(m_IFR.CurrentLineCopy);
			m_DBwriter.SetDatabankTitle(m_IFR.CurrentLineCopy);
		}
		return GetNextValidLine();
	}

	private int ProcessWrfile(ref string errMessage)
	{
		errMessage = null;
		Tokens tokens = new Tokens(m_IFR.CurrentLineCopy, Tokens.delimiterNormal);
		int length = tokens.Length;
		if (length < 2)
		{
			errMessage = "Error: DFMS input file " + m_IFR.FileName + " did not specify password of WRFILE, line " + m_IFR.CurrentLineNo;
			m_ReaderLog.AddMessageToLog("Error: DFMS input file " + m_IFR.FileName + " did not specify password of WRFILE, line " + m_IFR.CurrentLineNo);
			return -1;
		}
		if (m_DBwriter.SetDatabankAsDefault(m_DBwriter.GetOldDBank(tokens.GetAt(1))) == -1)
		{
			errMessage = "Error: DFMS input file " + m_IFR.FileName + " did not find databank specified in WRFILE, line " + m_IFR.CurrentLineNo;
			m_ReaderLog.AddMessageToLog("Error: DFMS input file " + m_IFR.FileName + " did not find databank specified in WRFILE, line " + m_IFR.CurrentLineNo);
			return -1;
		}
		return GetNextValidLine();
	}

	private int ProcessSKWPropList()
	{
		string text = "";
		int num = -99;
		int num2 = -99;
		int num3 = 0;
		int num4 = 1;
		int num5 = 0;
		m_PropListList.Clear();
		m_DBwriter.ResetCurrentPropList();
		while (true)
		{
			Tokens tokens = new Tokens(m_IFR.CurrentLineCopy, Tokens.delimiterSlash);
			num3 = tokens.Length;
			int num6 = ((num4 == 1) ? 1 : 0);
			while (num6 < num3)
			{
				if (text == "")
				{
					string at = tokens.GetAt(num6);
					at = UtilityFuncs.StripSingleQuotes(at);
					text = at;
					num6++;
				}
				if (num6 >= num3)
				{
					continue;
				}
				if (num2 == -99)
				{
					num2 = Convert.ToInt32(tokens.GetAt(num6));
					num6++;
				}
				if (num6 < num3)
				{
					string at = tokens.GetAt(num6);
					try
					{
						num = Convert.ToInt32(at);
						num6++;
					}
					catch
					{
						num = -99;
					}
					m_PropListList.Add(text);
					m_DBwriter.AddToTheCurrentPropList(text);
					text = "";
					num2 = -99;
					num = -99;
				}
			}
			if (-1 == GetNextValidLine())
			{
				num5 = -1;
				break;
			}
			if ((m_IFR.CurrentLineCopy.StartsWith(" ") || m_IFR.CurrentLineCopy.StartsWith("\n") || m_IFR.CurrentLineCopy == "" || m_IFR.CurrentLineCopy.StartsWith("\t")) && GetPDSKeyWord() == -1)
			{
				num4++;
				continue;
			}
			num5 = 0;
			break;
		}
		if (text != "")
		{
			m_PropListList.Add(text);
			m_DBwriter.AddToTheCurrentPropList(text);
		}
		return num5;
	}

	private bool IgnoreBecauseOfZero(double dblValue, string CurrentProp)
	{
		if (dblValue == 0.0)
		{
			switch (CurrentProp.ToUpperInvariant())
			{
			case "NOATOM":
			case "ATOMNO":
			case "UFGRP":
			case "UFGRPD":
			case "UFGRPL":
				return true;
			}
		}
		return false;
	}

	private int ProcessSKWPval()
	{
		string text = "";
		int num = 0;
		int num2 = 1;
		int num3 = 0;
		int num4 = 0;
		int count = m_PropListList.Count;
		int highestNonNullElementInVector = 0;
		if (count < 1)
		{
			return 0;
		}
		string idType = "";
		string empty = string.Empty;
		IEnumerator enumerator = m_PropListList.GetEnumerator();
		enumerator.MoveNext();
		string text2 = (string)enumerator.Current;
		long num5 = m_DBwriter.GetNumElemsOfParam(text2);
		if (num5 < 0)
		{
			return -1;
		}
		long num6 = num5;
		ArrayList arrayList = new ArrayList();
		m_DBwriter.AddPureDatabankInfo(m_DBwriter.Password, "", m_DBwriter.Password, m_DBwriter.DatabankType, "", "", UpdateDisplayNameMM: false, UpdatePassword: true, UpdateDatabankType: true, UpdatePromptMM: false, UpdateProduct: false);
		while (num4 < count)
		{
			string source = m_IFR.CurrentLineCopy.Replace("/", " / ");
			Tokens tokens = new Tokens(source, Tokens.delimiterNormal, "'", "'");
			num = tokens.Length;
			int num7;
			if (num2 == 1)
			{
				string at = tokens.GetAt(1);
				num7 = 2;
				if (num < 2)
				{
					return -1;
				}
				at = UtilityFuncs.StripSingleQuotes(at);
				at = at.Replace(" / ", "/");
				text = at;
				if (m_DBwriter.CheckCompAliasExists(text))
				{
					idType = "ALIAS";
				}
				else
				{
					if (!m_DBwriter.CheckCompNameExists(text))
					{
						m_ReaderLog.AddMessageToLog("Fatal error: DFMS input file " + m_IFR.FileName + "  line " + m_IFR.CurrentLineNo);
						m_ReaderLog.AddMessageToLog("          " + text + " is not in the databank either as a NAME or ALIAS");
						throw new GatewayException($"Fatal error: DFMS input file {m_IFR.FileName} at line {m_IFR.CurrentLineNo}. {text} does not exist in the databank either as a NAME or ALIAS.\nData Conversion will terminate now.");
					}
					idType = "NAME";
				}
				m_DBwriter.ReportAnyExtraPropertiesInDBase(text, idType);
			}
			else
			{
				num7 = 0;
			}
			double num8 = 0.0;
			double dblValue = 0.0;
			while (num7 < num)
			{
				string at2 = tokens.GetAt(num7);
				num7++;
				if (at2 != "/")
				{
					num3++;
					num8 = 0.0;
					num8 = ((!(at2 == "*")) ? UtilityFuncs.ConvertToDouble(at2) : 1E+35);
					if (!IgnoreBecauseOfZero(num8, text2))
					{
						if (num6 > 1 && num8 != 1E+35)
						{
							arrayList.Add(num8);
							highestNonNullElementInVector = num3;
						}
						else
						{
							arrayList.Add(null);
						}
					}
					else
					{
						arrayList.Add(null);
					}
				}
				if (num3 >= num6 || (arrayList.Count > 0 && at2 == "/"))
				{
					dblValue = 0.0;
					if (num6 > 1)
					{
						m_DBwriter.AddParamElemValue(text, idType, text2, num3, dblValue, arrayList, highestNonNullElementInVector);
					}
					else if (num6 == 1)
					{
						m_DBwriter.AddParamElemValue(text, idType, text2, num3, num8, null, highestNonNullElementInVector);
					}
					num4++;
					arrayList = new ArrayList();
					num3 = 0;
					highestNonNullElementInVector = 0;
					if (num4 >= count)
					{
						break;
					}
					enumerator.MoveNext();
					text2 = (string)enumerator.Current;
					num5 = m_DBwriter.GetNumElemsOfParam(text2);
					if (num5 < 0)
					{
						return -1;
					}
					num6 = num5;
				}
			}
			if (-1 == GetNextValidLine())
			{
				return -1;
			}
			if (m_IFR.CurrentLineCopy.StartsWith(" ") || m_IFR.CurrentLineCopy.StartsWith("\n") || m_IFR.CurrentLineCopy == "" || m_IFR.CurrentLineCopy.StartsWith("\t"))
			{
				if (GetPDSKeyWord() != -1)
				{
					if (arrayList.Count > 0)
					{
						m_DBwriter.AddParamElemValue(text, idType, text2, num3, dblValue, arrayList, highestNonNullElementInVector);
					}
					return 0;
				}
				num2++;
				continue;
			}
			return 0;
		}
		return 0;
	}

							m_DBwriter.AddBinaryParamVector(text, text2, text3, bIsPairParam: false, arrayList7, null, 0, isDFMS: true);
