	private int ProcessSKWCval()
	{
		string text = "";
		int num = 0;
		int num2 = 1;
		int num3 = 0;
		long num4 = -1L;
		int count = m_CompListList.Count;
		if (count < 1)
		{
			return 0;
		}
		int num5 = 0;
		IEnumerator enumerator = m_CompListList.GetEnumerator();
		while (true)
		{
			Tokens tokens = new Tokens(m_IFR.CurrentLineCopy, Tokens.delimiterSlash, "'", "'");
			num = tokens.Length;
			int num6;
			if (num2 == 1)
			{
				num6 = 4;
				if (num < 4)
				{
					return -1;
				}
				string at = tokens.GetAt(1);
				at = UtilityFuncs.StripSingleQuotes(at);
				text = at;
				num4 = m_DBwriter.GetNumElemsOfParam(text);
			}
			else
			{
				num6 = 0;
			}
			while (num6 < num)
			{
				string at2 = tokens.GetAt(num6);
				if (num3 == 0)
				{
					num3 = Convert.ToInt32(at2);
					num6++;
					m_DBwriter.CheckParamElem(text, num3);
					continue;
				}
				num6++;
				num5++;
				enumerator.MoveNext();
				string text2 = (string)enumerator.Current;
				string text3 = "";
				if (m_DBwriter.CheckCompAliasExists(text2))
				{
					text3 = "ALIAS";
				}
				else
				{
					if (!m_DBwriter.CheckCompNameExists(text2))
					{
						m_ReaderLog.AddMessageToLog("Fatal error: DFMS input file " + m_IFR.FileName + "  line " + m_IFR.CurrentLineNo);
						m_ReaderLog.AddMessageToLog("          " + text2 + " is not in the databank either as a NAME or ALIAS");
						throw new GatewayException($"Fatal error: DFMS input file {m_IFR.FileName} at line {m_IFR.CurrentLineNo}. {text2} does not exist in the databank either as a NAME or ALIAS.\nData Conversion will terminate now.");
					}
					text3 = "NAME";
				}
				if (at2 != "*")
				{
					double num7 = UtilityFuncs.ConvertToDouble(at2);
					if (num7 != 1E+35)
					{
						m_DBwriter.AddParamElemValue(text2, text3, text, num3, num7, null, num3);
					}
				}
				if (num5 >= count)
				{
					num5 = 0;
					enumerator.Reset();
					num3 = 0;
				}
			}
			if (-1 == GetNextValidLine())
			{
				return -1;
			}
			if (!m_IFR.CurrentLineCopy.StartsWith(" ") && !m_IFR.CurrentLineCopy.StartsWith("\n") && !(m_IFR.CurrentLineCopy == "") && !m_IFR.CurrentLineCopy.StartsWith("\t"))
			{
				break;
			}
			if (GetPDSKeyWord() != -1)
			{
				return 0;
			}
			num2++;
		}
		return 0;
	}

	private int ProcessSKWBcval()
	{
		string text = "";
		string text2 = "";
		string text3 = "";
		int num = 0;
		int num2 = 1;
		int num3 = 0;
		long num4 = -1L;
		int count = m_CompListList.Count;
		if (count < 1)
		{
			return 0;
		}
		int num5 = 0;
		IEnumerator enumerator = m_CompListList.GetEnumerator();
		while (true)
		{
			Tokens tokens = new Tokens(m_IFR.CurrentLineCopy, Tokens.delimiterSlash, "'", "'");
			num = tokens.Length;
			int num6;
			if (num2 == 1)
			{
				num6 = 4;
				if (num < 4)
				{
					return -1;
				}
				string at = tokens.GetAt(1);
				at = UtilityFuncs.StripSingleQuotes(at);
				text3 = at;
				num4 = 2 * m_DBwriter.GetNumElemsOfParam(text3);
			}
			else
			{
				num6 = 0;
			}
			while (num6 < num)
			{
				string at2 = tokens.GetAt(num6);
				if (num3 == 0)
				{
					num3 = Convert.ToInt32(at2);
					num6++;
					m_DBwriter.CheckParamElem(text3, num3);
					continue;
				}
				if (text == "")
				{
					string at3 = tokens.GetAt(num6);
					at3 = UtilityFuncs.StripSingleQuotes(at3);
					text = at3;
					m_DBwriter.CheckCompAliasExists(text);
					num6++;
					continue;
				}
				num6++;
				num5++;
				enumerator.MoveNext();
				text2 = (string)enumerator.Current;
				m_DBwriter.CheckBinaryCompExists(text, text2);
				if (at2 != "*")
				{
					double num7 = UtilityFuncs.ConvertToDouble(at2);
					if (num7 != 1E+35)
					{
						m_DBwriter.AddBinaryParamElemValue(text, text2, text3, num3, num7);
					}
				}
				if (num5 >= count)
				{
					num5 = 0;
					enumerator.Reset();
					num3 = 0;
					text = "";
					text2 = "";
				}
			}
			if (-1 == GetNextValidLine())
			{
				return -1;
			}
			if (!m_IFR.CurrentLineCopy.StartsWith(" ") && !m_IFR.CurrentLineCopy.StartsWith("\n") && !(m_IFR.CurrentLineCopy == "") && !m_IFR.CurrentLineCopy.StartsWith("\t"))
			{
				break;
			}
			if (GetPDSKeyWord() != -1)
			{
				return 0;
			}
			num2++;
		}
		return 0;
	}
}
internal class DtsReader
{
	private InputFileReader m_IFR;

	private ReaderLog m_ReaderLog;

	private DBwriter m_DBwriter;

	private DatabaseCollection myDatabaseCollection;

	public DtsReader(ReaderLog readerLog, DBwriter dbWriter)
	{
		//IL_0015: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
		//IL_001f: Expected O, but got Unknown
		m_ReaderLog = readerLog;
		m_DBwriter = dbWriter;
		myDatabaseCollection = new DatabaseCollection();
		myDatabaseCollection.InitializeFromConfigurationFile((Stream)null, "ATVIEWER");
	}

	public void OpenDtsFile(string DtsFileName)
	{
		m_IFR = new InputFileReader(m_ReaderLog);
		m_IFR.OpenFile(DtsFileName);
	}

	public void CloseDtsFile()
	{
		m_IFR.CloseFile();
	}

	private int GetNextValidLine()
	{
		int num = -1;
		do
		{
			num = m_IFR.GetALine();
		}
		while (num != -1 && m_IFR.CurrentLine.StartsWith(";"));
		return num;
	}

	public int Process(IDatabank MergeToThisDbank)
	{
		//IL_069f: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
		//IL_06a6: Expected O, but got Unknown
		//IL_06cc: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
		//IL_06d3: Expected O, but got Unknown
		//IL_039b: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
		//IL_03a2: Expected O, but got Unknown
		//IL_0895: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
		//IL_089c: Expected O, but got Unknown
		string text = "";
		if (myDatabaseCollection.Count == 0)
		{
			m_ReaderLog.AddMessageToLog("Error: No databases were found to be registered in APED Database Manager or config.aem file was not found.");
			return -1;
		}
		if (m_DBwriter.IsSecured)
		{
			text = "Error: Data can not be transferred to a secure database. Database: '{0}' is a secured database.";
			m_ReaderLog.AddMessageToLog(string.Format(text, m_DBwriter.Alias));
			return -1;
		}
		Hashtable hashtable = new Hashtable();
		ArrayList arrayList = new ArrayList();
		Hashtable hashtable2 = new Hashtable();
		ArrayList arrayList2 = new ArrayList();
		ArrayList arrayList3 = new ArrayList();
		string text2 = "";
		string text3 = "";
		int num = -1;
		string text4 = "";
		ArrayList arrayList4 = new ArrayList();
		int num2 = 0;
		bool flag = false;
		num = GetNextValidLine();
		if (num == -1)
		{
			m_ReaderLog.AddMessageToLog("Error: End of file at the beginning of " + m_IFR.FileName);
			return -1;
		}
		IDatabankCollection val2 = default(IDatabankCollection);
		while (num != -1)
		{
			text4 = m_IFR.CurrentLine.Trim().ToLowerInvariant();
			switch (text4)
			{
			case "":
			case "<":
			case "</":
			case ">":
				num = GetNextValidLine();
				continue;
			}
			if (text4.StartsWith("</"))
			{
				if (text4 != "</" + text3 + ">")
				{
					m_ReaderLog.AddMessageToLog("Error: Invalid section end specifications were found. Please make sure sections are specified appropriately.");
					return -1;
				}
				text3 = "";
			}
			else if (text4.StartsWith("<"))
			{
				if (text3 != "")
				{
					m_ReaderLog.AddMessageToLog("Error: Invalid section start specifications were found. Please make sure sections are specified appropriately.");
					return -1;
				}
				text3 = text4;
				text3 = text3.Replace("<", "");
				text3 = text3.Replace(">", "");
				switch (text3)
				{
				default:
					text3 = "";
					break;
				case "options":
				case "database.databanks":
				case "properties":
				case "identifier":
				case "compounds":
				case "reactions":
					break;
				}
			}
			else
			{
				switch (text3)
				{
				case "options":
				{
					string[] array2 = text4.Split('=');
					if (array2 != null && array2.Length == 2)
					{
						if (hashtable.ContainsKey(array2[0].Trim().ToUpperInvariant()))
						{
							text = "Error: Option specifications detected a duplicate. Specified option : '{0}' was found to be specified more than once.";
							m_ReaderLog.AddMessageToLog(string.Format(text, text4));
							return -1;
						}
						if (array2[0].ToLowerInvariant().CompareTo("new-databank-name") == 0 && array2[1].Length > 9)
						{
							text = "Error: Option specifications detected databank name {0} to be longer than allowed 9 characters limit.";
							m_ReaderLog.AddMessageToLog(string.Format(text, array2[1]));
							return -1;
						}
						hashtable.Add(array2[0].ToUpperInvariant(), array2[1].ToUpperInvariant());
						break;
					}
					m_ReaderLog.AddMessageToLog("Error: Option specifications encountered an error. Please make sure options are specified appropriately.");
					return -1;
				}
				case "database.databanks":
				{
					string[] array3 = text4.Split('.');
					if (array3 != null && array3.Length == 2)
					{
						string text5 = array3[0].ToUpperInvariant();
						IDatabase val = myDatabaseCollection[text5];
						if (val != null)
						{
							val.EnumerateDatabanks(ref val2);
							bool flag2 = false;
							if (val2 != null)
							{
								foreach (IDatabank item in (IEnumerable)val2)
								{
									IDatabank val3 = item;
									if (val3.Name.ToLowerInvariant().CompareTo(array3[1].ToLowerInvariant()) == 0)
									{
										flag2 = true;
										break;
									}
								}
							}
							if (!flag2)
							{
								text = "Error: database.databanks specifications encountered an error. {0} database does not contain specified {1} databank.";
								m_ReaderLog.AddMessageToLog(string.Format(text, text5, array3[1].ToUpperInvariant()));
								return -1;
							}
							if (!arrayList.Contains(text4.ToUpperInvariant()))
							{
								arrayList.Add(text4.ToUpperInvariant());
								if (!arrayList4.Contains(val))
								{
									arrayList4.Add(val);
								}
								hashtable2.Add(num2, val);
								num2++;
							}
							else
							{
								text = "Warning: database.databanks specifications detected a duplicate. Specified source database.databank : '{0}' was found to be specified more than once.";
								m_ReaderLog.AddMessageToLog(string.Format(text, text4));
							}
							if (string.Compare(text5, m_DBwriter.Alias) == 0)
							{
								text = "Error: database.databanks specifications encountered an error. '{0}' database was found to be specified as source while being used as a destination database.";
								m_ReaderLog.AddMessageToLog(string.Format(text, text5));
								return -1;
							}
							if (!val.isPublic)
							{
								text = "Error: database.databanks specifications encountered an error. Specified source database : '{0}' was found to be a secured database, data can not be transferred from a secured database.";
								m_ReaderLog.AddMessageToLog(string.Format(text, text5));
								return -1;
							}
							break;
						}
						text = "Error: database.databanks specifications encountered an error. '{0}' database was not found to be registered in the Aped Database Manager.";
						m_ReaderLog.AddMessageToLog(string.Format(text, text5));
						return -1;
					}
					m_ReaderLog.AddMessageToLog("Error: database.databank specifications encountered an error. Please make sure database.databanks are specified in database.databank format.");
					return -1;
				}
				case "properties":
					if (arrayList2.Contains(text4))
					{
						text = "Warning: Property specifications detected a duplicate. Specified property : '{0}' was found to be specified more than once.";
						m_ReaderLog.AddMessageToLog(string.Format(text, text4));
					}
					else if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(text4))
					{
						arrayList2.Add(text4.ToUpperInvariant());
					}
					break;
				case "identifier":
					if (text2 != "")
					{
						text = "Error: Identifier specifications detected a duplicate. You can not specify more than one identifier type in identifier section. Please remove specified identifier : {0} and rerun.";
						m_ReaderLog.AddMessageToLog(string.Format(text, text4));
						return -1;
					}
					text2 = text4.ToUpperInvariant();
					break;
				case "compounds":
					if (arrayList3.Contains(text4))
					{
						text = "Warning: Compound specifications detected a duplicate. Specified compound : '{0}' was found to be specified more than once.";
						m_ReaderLog.AddMessageToLog(string.Format(text, text4));
					}
					else if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(text4))
					{
						arrayList3.Add(text4.ToUpperInvariant());
					}
					break;
				case "reactions":
				{
					string[] array = text4.Split('=');
					if (array != null && array.Length == 2)
					{
						if (array[0].ToLowerInvariant().CompareTo("transfer-reaction-data") != 0)
						{
							text = "Error: Reactions section specifications detected a wrong keyword : {0}. Must be 'transfer-reaction-data'.";
							m_ReaderLog.AddMessageToLog(string.Format(text, array[0]));
							return -1;
						}
						if (array[1].ToLowerInvariant().CompareTo("yes") == 0)
						{
							flag = true;
						}
						break;
					}
					m_ReaderLog.AddMessageToLog("Error: Reactions section specifications encountered an error. Please make sure reaction section is specified in transfer-reaction-data=yes (or no) format.");
					return -1;
				}
				}
			}
			num = GetNextValidLine();
		}
		Hashtable hashtable3 = new Hashtable();
		for (int i = 0; i < hashtable2.Count; i++)
		{
			IDatabankCollection val4 = null;
			IDatabase val5 = (IDatabase)hashtable2[i];
			val5.EnumerateDatabanks(ref val4);
			if (val4 == null)
			{
				continue;
			}
			Hashtable hashtable4 = new Hashtable();
			foreach (IDatabank item2 in (IEnumerable)val4)
			{
				IDatabank val6 = item2;
				if (!hashtable4.ContainsKey(val6.Name))
				{
					hashtable4.Add(val6.Name, val6);
				}
			}
			if (!hashtable3.ContainsKey(val5))
			{
				hashtable3.Add(val5, hashtable4);
			}
		}
		ArrayList arrayList5 = new ArrayList();
		ArrayList arrayList6 = new ArrayList();
		for (int j = 0; j < arrayList3.Count; j++)
		{
			if (!arrayList5.Contains(arrayList3[j]))
			{
				arrayList5.Add(arrayList3[j]);
			}
		}
		for (int k = 0; k < arrayList2.Count; k++)
		{
			if (!arrayList6.Contains(arrayList2[k]))
			{
				arrayList6.Add(arrayList2[k]);
			}
		}
		m_DBwriter.DealWithProperties(arrayList4, arrayList6, hashtable);
		m_DBwriter.DealWithCompounds(arrayList4, arrayList5, text2, hashtable);
		IDatabank val7 = null;
		object obj = hashtable["NEW-DATABANK-NAME"];
		string text6 = ((obj != null) ? obj.ToString() : "");
		obj = hashtable["NEW-DATABANK-DESCRIPTION"];
		string text7 = ((obj != null) ? obj.ToString() : "");
		bool isDataBeingMerged = false;
		if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(text6))
		{
			if (MergeToThisDbank != null)
			{
				m_DBwriter.SetDatabankAsDefault(MergeToThisDbank);
				text = "Warning : NEW-DATABANK-NAME option was found to be specified but it will be ignored. Data will be merged to {0} databank instead.";
				m_ReaderLog.AddMessageToLog(string.Format(text, MergeToThisDbank.Name));
				isDataBeingMerged = true;
			}
			else
			{
				IDatabankCollection databankList = m_DBwriter.GetDatabankList();
				if (databankList != null && ((ICollection)databankList).Count > 0)
				{
					foreach (IDatabank item3 in (IEnumerable)databankList)
					{
						IDatabank val8 = item3;
						if (val8.Name.ToLowerInvariant().CompareTo(text6.ToLowerInvariant()) == 0)
						{
							throw new GatewayException("A databank with same specified name already exists in the destination database.");
						}
					}
				}
				m_DBwriter.CreateNewDBank("", text6);
				val7 = m_DBwriter.GetOldDBank(text6);
				m_DBwriter.SetDatabankAsDefault(val7);
			}
		}
		else
		{
			isDataBeingMerged = true;
			if (MergeToThisDbank == null)
			{
				throw new GatewayException("Destination databank for merging can not be empty.");
			}
			m_DBwriter.SetDatabankAsDefault(MergeToThisDbank);
		}
		m_DBwriter.DealWithPropertyValues(hashtable2, arrayList, hashtable3, arrayList5, arrayList6, text2, hashtable, isDataBeingMerged);
		if (flag)
		{
			m_DBwriter.DealWithReactions(hashtable2, arrayList, hashtable3, arrayList5, text2, hashtable);
		}
		return 0;
	}
}
internal class FormulaReader
{
	private InputFileReader m_IFR;

	private ReaderLog m_ReaderLog;

	private DBwriter m_DBwriter;

	public FormulaReader(ReaderLog readerLog, DBwriter dbWriter)
	{
		m_ReaderLog = readerLog;
		m_DBwriter = dbWriter;
	}

	public void OpenFormulaFile(string FormulaFileName)
	{
		m_IFR = new InputFileReader(m_ReaderLog);
		m_IFR.OpenFile(FormulaFileName);
	}

	public void CloseFormulaFile()
	{
		m_IFR.CloseFile();
	}

	private int GetNextValidLine()
	{
		int num = -1;
		do
		{
			num = m_IFR.GetALine();
		}
		while (num != -1 && m_IFR.CurrentLine.StartsWith(";"));
		return num;
	}

	public int Process()
	{
		int num = -1;
		string text = "";
		string text2 = "";
		string[] array = new string[1];
		IPropertyValue propertyValue = null;
		try
		{
			num = GetNextValidLine();
			if (num == -1)
			{
				m_ReaderLog.AddMessageToLog("Error: End of file at the beginning of " + m_IFR.FileName);
				return -1;
			}
			while (num != -1)
			{
				if (m_IFR.CurrentLine.StartsWith("ALIAS"))
				{
					text = m_IFR.CurrentLine.Split('$')[1].Trim();
				}
				if (m_IFR.CurrentLine.StartsWith("FORMULA"))
				{
					text2 = m_IFR.CurrentLine.Split('$')[1].Trim();
				}
				if (m_IFR.CurrentLine.StartsWith("*"))
				{
					array[0] = text2;
					try
					{
						m_DBwriter.AddPropertyValue(null, text, "ALIAS", "FORMULA", array, "", "", "", null, "", out propertyValue);
					}
					catch (Exception ex)
					{
						m_ReaderLog.AddMessageToLog(ex.Message);
					}
					if (propertyValue != null)
					{
						m_ReaderLog.AddMessageToLog($"Formula added for {text}");
					}
					text = "";
					text2 = "";
					array[0] = "";
				}
				if (m_IFR.CurrentLine.StartsWith("####"))
				{
					string text3 = m_IFR.CurrentLine.Split(',')[1].Trim();
					string text4 = m_IFR.CurrentLine.Split(',')[2].Trim();
					string[] identifierList = new string[1] { text3 };
					if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(text4))
					{
						if (!m_DBwriter.CheckCompAliasExists(text3) && !m_DBwriter.CheckCompNameExists(text3))
						{
							if (m_DBwriter.CheckCompIdentifierExists(text4, "CASRN"))
							{
								try
								{
									m_DBwriter.AddIdentifierValues(text4, "CASRN", "SYNONYM", identifierList);
									m_ReaderLog.AddMessageToLog("Added HYSYS synonym " + text3 + " for compound with CASRN " + text4);
								}
								catch (Exception ex2)
								{
									m_ReaderLog.AddMessageToLog(ex2.Message);
								}
							}
							else
							{
								m_ReaderLog.AddMessageToLog("Compoud with CASRN " + text4 + " does not exist in this database.");
							}
						}
						else
						{
							m_ReaderLog.AddMessageToLog("Compoud with identifier value " + text3 + " already exists in this database.");
						}
					}
				}
				num = GetNextValidLine();
			}
		}
		catch (Exception ex3)
		{
			m_ReaderLog.AddMessageToLog(ex3.Message);
		}
		return 0;
	}
}
internal class GlobalMembers
{
}
public class InputFileReader
{
	private StreamReader m_StreamReader;

	private ReaderLog m_ReaderLog;

	private string m_stringFile;

	private int m_nCurrentLineNo;

	private string m_CurrentLine;

	private string m_CurrentLineCopy;

	public string FileName => m_stringFile;

	public int CurrentLineNo => m_nCurrentLineNo;

	public string CurrentLine => m_CurrentLine;

	public string CurrentLineCopy
	{
		get
		{
			return m_CurrentLineCopy;
		}
		set
		{
			m_CurrentLineCopy = value;
		}
	}

	public InputFileReader(ReaderLog readerLog)
	{
		m_ReaderLog = readerLog;
	}

	public void OpenFile(string strFileName)
	{
		m_stringFile = strFileName;
		try
		{
			m_StreamReader = new StreamReader(File.OpenRead(m_stringFile), Encoding.ASCII);
			if (m_ReaderLog != null)
			{
				m_ReaderLog.AddMessageToLog("Begin processing file: " + m_stringFile);
			}
		}
		catch
		{
			if (m_ReaderLog != null)
			{
				m_ReaderLog.AddMessageToLog("ERROR: Could not open stream to read file " + m_stringFile);
			}
			throw new Exception("Could not open stream to read file " + m_stringFile);
		}
		m_StreamReader.BaseStream.Seek(0L, SeekOrigin.Begin);
	}

	public void ReportFileFailure(string error)
	{
		if (m_StreamReader == null)
		{
			return;
		}
		try
		{
			m_StreamReader.Close();
			m_StreamReader = null;
			if (m_ReaderLog != null)
			{
				error = "Fail to process file: " + m_stringFile + " due to " + error;
				m_ReaderLog.AddMessageToLog(error);
			}
		}
		catch (Exception ex)
		{
			if (m_ReaderLog != null)
			{
				error = "Fail to process file: " + m_stringFile + " and cannot close it due to " + error + " and " + ex.Message;
				m_ReaderLog.AddMessageToLog(error);
			}
		}
	}

	public void CloseFile()
	{
		if (m_StreamReader == null)
		{
			return;
		}
		try
		{
			m_StreamReader.Close();
			m_StreamReader = null;
			if (m_ReaderLog != null)
			{
				m_ReaderLog.AddMessageToLog("End processing file: " + m_stringFile);
			}
		}
		catch
		{
			if (m_ReaderLog != null)
			{
				m_ReaderLog.AddMessageToLog("ERROR: Could not close file: " + m_stringFile);
			}
			throw new Exception("Could not close file " + m_stringFile);
		}
	}

	public int GetALine()
	{
		try
		{
			if (m_StreamReader.Peek() > -1)
			{
				m_CurrentLine = m_StreamReader.ReadLine();
				m_CurrentLineCopy = m_CurrentLine;
				m_nCurrentLineNo++;
				return 0;
			}
		}
		catch
		{
		}
		return -1;
	}
}
public class MMTBSBinaryDatabank
{
	private InputFileReader m_IFR;

	private ReaderLog m_ReaderLog;

	private DBwriter m_DBwriter;

	public MMTBSBinaryDatabank(InputFileReader ifr, ReaderLog readerLog, DBwriter dbWriter)
	{
		m_IFR = ifr;
		m_ReaderLog = readerLog;
		m_DBwriter = dbWriter;
	}

	public int ProcessBinaryDatabank(ref string errMessage)
	{
		errMessage = null;
		m_IFR.GetALine();
		Tokens tokens = new Tokens(m_IFR.CurrentLineCopy, Tokens.delimiterNormal);
		if (tokens.Length < 1 || Convert.ToInt32(tokens.GetAt(0)) != 1)
		{
			errMessage = "Error: Incorrect syntax on line " + m_IFR.CurrentLineNo + " of file " + m_IFR.FileName;
			m_ReaderLog.AddMessageToLog("Error: Incorrect syntax on line " + m_IFR.CurrentLineNo + " of file " + m_IFR.FileName);
			return -1;
		}
		m_IFR.GetALine();
		tokens = new Tokens(m_IFR.CurrentLineCopy, Tokens.delimiterNormal);
		if (tokens.Length < 1)
		{
			errMessage = "Error: Incorrect syntax on line " + m_IFR.CurrentLineNo + " of file " + m_IFR.FileName;
			m_ReaderLog.AddMessageToLog("Error: Incorrect syntax on line " + m_IFR.CurrentLineNo + " of file " + m_IFR.FileName);
			return -1;
		}
		int num = Convert.ToInt32(tokens.GetAt(0));
		for (int i = 0; i < num; i++)
		{
			m_IFR.GetALine();
			tokens = new Tokens(m_IFR.CurrentLineCopy, Tokens.delimiterNormal);
			if (tokens.Length < 3)
			{
				errMessage = "Error: Incorrect syntax on line " + m_IFR.CurrentLineNo + " of file " + m_IFR.FileName;
				m_ReaderLog.AddMessageToLog("Error: Incorrect syntax on line " + m_IFR.CurrentLineNo + " of file " + m_IFR.FileName);
				return -1;
			}
			int num2 = Convert.ToInt32(tokens.GetAt(0));
			int num3 = 1;
			ArrayList arrayList = new ArrayList();
			ArrayList arrayList2 = new ArrayList();
			ArrayList arrayList3 = new ArrayList();
			for (int j = 0; j < num2; j++)
			{
				if (tokens.Length >= num3 + 2)
				{
					arrayList.Add(tokens.GetAt(num3));
					num3++;
					int num4 = 0;
					while (num3 < tokens.Length)
					{
						try
						{
							int num5 = Convert.ToInt32(tokens.GetAt(num3));
							arrayList3.Add(num5);
							num4++;
							num3++;
						}
						catch
						{
							break;
						}
					}
					arrayList2.Add(num4);
					if (j % 10 == 0)
					{
						GC.Collect();
						GC.WaitForPendingFinalizers();
					}
					continue;
				}
				errMessage = "Error: Incorrect number of parameters on line " + m_IFR.CurrentLineNo + " of file " + m_IFR.FileName;
				m_ReaderLog.AddMessageToLog("Error: Incorrect number of parameters on line " + m_IFR.CurrentLineNo + " of file " + m_IFR.FileName);
				return -1;
			}
			m_IFR.GetALine();
			tokens = new Tokens(m_IFR.CurrentLineCopy, Tokens.delimiterNormal);
			if (tokens.Length < 1)
			{
				errMessage = "Error: Incorrect syntax on line " + m_IFR.CurrentLineNo + " of file " + m_IFR.FileName;
				m_ReaderLog.AddMessageToLog("Error: Incorrect syntax on line " + m_IFR.CurrentLineNo + " of file " + m_IFR.FileName);
				return -1;
			}
			int num6 = 0;
			try
			{
				num6 = Convert.ToInt32(tokens.GetAt(0));
			}
			catch
			{
			}
			if (num6 == 0)
			{
				errMessage = "Error: Incorrect number of rows on line " + m_IFR.CurrentLineNo + " of file " + m_IFR.FileName;
				m_ReaderLog.AddMessageToLog("Error: Incorrect number of rows on line " + m_IFR.CurrentLineNo + " of file " + m_IFR.FileName);
				return -1;
			}
			ArrayList arrayList4 = new ArrayList();
			ArrayList arrayList5 = new ArrayList();
			for (int k = 0; k < num6; k++)
			{
				m_IFR.GetALine();
				tokens = new Tokens(m_IFR.CurrentLineCopy, Tokens.delimiterNormal);
				int num7 = 0;
				if (tokens.Length < 1)
				{
					errMessage = "Error: Incorrect syntax on line " + m_IFR.CurrentLineNo + " of file " + m_IFR.FileName;
					m_ReaderLog.AddMessageToLog("Error: Incorrect syntax on line " + m_IFR.CurrentLineNo + " of file " + m_IFR.FileName);
					return -1;
				}
				try
				{
					num7 = Convert.ToInt32(tokens.GetAt(0));
				}
				catch
				{
				}
				if (num7 == 0)
				{
					errMessage = "Error: Incorrect number of rows on line " + m_IFR.CurrentLineNo + " of file " + m_IFR.FileName;
					m_ReaderLog.AddMessageToLog("Error: Incorrect number of rows on line " + m_IFR.CurrentLineNo + " of file " + m_IFR.FileName);
					return -1;
				}
				for (int l = 1; l <= num7; l++)
				{
					arrayList5.Add(tokens.GetAt(l));
				}
				arrayList4.Add(num7);
			}
			m_DBwriter.AddBinaryParameters(arrayList, arrayList2, arrayList3, arrayList5);
			m_IFR.GetALine();
			tokens = new Tokens(m_IFR.CurrentLineCopy, Tokens.delimiterNormal);
			int num8 = 0;
			if (tokens.Length < 1)
			{
				errMessage = "Error: Incorrect syntax on line " + m_IFR.CurrentLineNo + " of file " + m_IFR.FileName;
				m_ReaderLog.AddMessageToLog("Error: Incorrect syntax on line " + m_IFR.CurrentLineNo + " of file " + m_IFR.FileName);
				return -1;
			}
			try
			{
				num8 = Convert.ToInt32(tokens.GetAt(0));
			}
			catch
			{
			}
			if (num8 == 0)
			{
				errMessage = "Error: Incorrect number of rows on line " + m_IFR.CurrentLineNo + " of file " + m_IFR.FileName;
				m_ReaderLog.AddMessageToLog("Error: Incorrect number of rows on line " + m_IFR.CurrentLineNo + " of file " + m_IFR.FileName);
				return -1;
			}
			while (num8 > 0)
			{
				m_IFR.GetALine();
				tokens = new Tokens(m_IFR.CurrentLineCopy, Tokens.delimiterNormal);
				if (tokens.Length < 3)
				{
					errMessage = "Error: Specify EOS model, Gamma model, databank name on line " + m_IFR.CurrentLineNo + " of file " + m_IFR.FileName;
					m_ReaderLog.AddMessageToLog("Error: Specify EOS model, Gamma model, databank name on line " + m_IFR.CurrentLineNo + " of file " + m_IFR.FileName);
					return -1;
				}
				string at = tokens.GetAt(0);
				string at2 = tokens.GetAt(1);
				string at3 = tokens.GetAt(2);
				if (at3.Length > 9)
				{
					errMessage = "Error: databank name \"" + at3 + "\" exceeds nine characters in " + m_IFR.FileName + ".";
					m_ReaderLog.AddMessageToLog("Error: databank name \"" + at3 + "\" exceeds nine characters in " + m_IFR.FileName + ".");
					return -1;
				}
				m_DBwriter.AddBinaryDatabankForParams(arrayList, at3, at, at2);
				m_IFR.GetALine();
				tokens = new Tokens(m_IFR.CurrentLineCopy, Tokens.delimiterNormal);
				if (tokens.Length < 1)
				{
					errMessage = "Error: Invalid format on line " + m_IFR.CurrentLineNo + " of file " + m_IFR.FileName;
					m_ReaderLog.AddMessageToLog("Error: Invalid format on line " + m_IFR.CurrentLineNo + " of file " + m_IFR.FileName);
					return -1;
				}
				int num9 = 0;
				int num10 = 0;
				num10 = Convert.ToInt32(tokens.GetAt(0));
				if (tokens.Length > 1)
				{
					num9 = Convert.ToInt32(tokens.GetAt(1));
				}
				bool bIsPairParam = false;
				for (int m = 0; m < num10; m++)
				{
					string text = "";
					string text2 = "";
					ArrayList arrayList6 = new ArrayList();
					for (int n = 0; n < num6; n++)
					{
						m_IFR.GetALine();
						tokens = new Tokens(m_IFR.CurrentLineCopy, Tokens.delimiterNormal);
						int num11 = Convert.ToInt32(arrayList4[n]);
						if (n == 0)
						{
							num11 += 2;
						}
						if (tokens.Length < num11)
						{
							errMessage = "Error: Invalid format on line " + m_IFR.CurrentLineNo + " of file " + m_IFR.FileName;
							m_ReaderLog.AddMessageToLog("Error: Invalid format on line " + m_IFR.CurrentLineNo + " of file " + m_IFR.FileName);
							return -1;
						}
						int num12 = 0;
						if (n == 0)
						{
							text = tokens.GetAt(0);
							text2 = tokens.GetAt(1);
							num12 = 2;
							if (m == 0 && (text.IndexOf("$") > 0 || text2.IndexOf("$") > 0))
							{
								bIsPairParam = true;
							}
						}
						for (int num13 = num12; num13 < num11; num13++)
						{
							arrayList6.Add(UtilityFuncs.FixExponentsForDouble(tokens.GetAt(num13)));
						}
					}
					ALL_Regparams myRegparams;
					switch (num9)
					{
					case 1:
					{
						VLE_Regparams vLE_Regparams = new VLE_Regparams();
						m_IFR.GetALine();
						tokens = new Tokens(m_IFR.CurrentLineCopy, Tokens.delimiterNormal);
						vLE_Regparams.sigma = UtilityFuncs.ConvertToDouble(tokens.GetAt(0));
						vLE_Regparams.valcon = UtilityFuncs.ConvertToDouble(tokens.GetAt(1));
						m_IFR.GetALine();
						tokens = new Tokens(m_IFR.CurrentLineCopy, Tokens.delimiterNormal);
						vLE_Regparams.temp_l.lower = UtilityFuncs.ConvertToDouble(tokens.GetAt(0));
						vLE_Regparams.temp_l.upper = UtilityFuncs.ConvertToDouble(tokens.GetAt(1));
						vLE_Regparams.pres_l.lower = UtilityFuncs.ConvertToDouble(tokens.GetAt(2));
						vLE_Regparams.pres_l.upper = UtilityFuncs.ConvertToDouble(tokens.GetAt(3));
						m_IFR.GetALine();
						tokens = new Tokens(m_IFR.CurrentLineCopy, Tokens.delimiterNormal);
						vLE_Regparams.x_l.lower = UtilityFuncs.ConvertToDouble(tokens.GetAt(0));
						vLE_Regparams.x_l.upper = UtilityFuncs.ConvertToDouble(tokens.GetAt(1));
						m_IFR.GetALine();
						tokens = new Tokens(m_IFR.CurrentLineCopy, Tokens.delimiterNormal);
						vLE_Regparams.temp_e.npt = UtilityFuncs.ConvertToDouble(tokens.GetAt(0));
						vLE_Regparams.temp_e.aard = UtilityFuncs.ConvertToDouble(tokens.GetAt(1));
						vLE_Regparams.temp_e.aad = UtilityFuncs.ConvertToDouble(tokens.GetAt(2));
						vLE_Regparams.temp_e.maxd = UtilityFuncs.ConvertToDouble(tokens.GetAt(3));
						m_IFR.GetALine();
						tokens = new Tokens(m_IFR.CurrentLineCopy, Tokens.delimiterNormal);
						vLE_Regparams.pres_e.npt = UtilityFuncs.ConvertToDouble(tokens.GetAt(0));
						vLE_Regparams.pres_e.aard = UtilityFuncs.ConvertToDouble(tokens.GetAt(1));
						vLE_Regparams.pres_e.aad = UtilityFuncs.ConvertToDouble(tokens.GetAt(2));
						vLE_Regparams.pres_e.maxd = UtilityFuncs.ConvertToDouble(tokens.GetAt(3));
						m_IFR.GetALine();
						tokens = new Tokens(m_IFR.CurrentLineCopy, Tokens.delimiterNormal);
						vLE_Regparams.x_e.npt = UtilityFuncs.ConvertToDouble(tokens.GetAt(0));
						vLE_Regparams.x_e.aard = UtilityFuncs.ConvertToDouble(tokens.GetAt(1));
						vLE_Regparams.x_e.aad = UtilityFuncs.ConvertToDouble(tokens.GetAt(2));
						vLE_Regparams.x_e.maxd = UtilityFuncs.ConvertToDouble(tokens.GetAt(3));
						myRegparams = vLE_Regparams;
						break;
					}
					case 2:
					{
						LLE_Regparams lLE_Regparams = new LLE_Regparams();
						m_IFR.GetALine();
						tokens = new Tokens(m_IFR.CurrentLineCopy, Tokens.delimiterNormal);
						lLE_Regparams.sigma = UtilityFuncs.ConvertToDouble(tokens.GetAt(0));
						lLE_Regparams.valcon = UtilityFuncs.ConvertToDouble(tokens.GetAt(1));
						m_IFR.GetALine();

						lLE_Regparams.temp_l.lower = UtilityFuncs.ConvertToDouble(tokens.GetAt(0));
						lLE_Regparams.temp_l.upper = UtilityFuncs.ConvertToDouble(tokens.GetAt(1));
						lLE_Regparams.x1_l.lower = UtilityFuncs.ConvertToDouble(tokens.GetAt(2));
						lLE_Regparams.x1_l.upper = UtilityFuncs.ConvertToDouble(tokens.GetAt(3));
						m_IFR.GetALine();
						tokens = new Tokens(m_IFR.CurrentLineCopy, Tokens.delimiterNormal);
						lLE_Regparams.x2_l.lower = UtilityFuncs.ConvertToDouble(tokens.GetAt(0));
						lLE_Regparams.x2_l.upper = UtilityFuncs.ConvertToDouble(tokens.GetAt(1));
						m_IFR.GetALine();
						tokens = new Tokens(m_IFR.CurrentLineCopy, Tokens.delimiterNormal);
						lLE_Regparams.x1_e.npt = UtilityFuncs.ConvertToDouble(tokens.GetAt(0));
						lLE_Regparams.x1_e.aard = UtilityFuncs.ConvertToDouble(tokens.GetAt(1));
						lLE_Regparams.x1_e.aad = UtilityFuncs.ConvertToDouble(tokens.GetAt(2));
						lLE_Regparams.x1_e.maxd = UtilityFuncs.ConvertToDouble(tokens.GetAt(3));
						m_IFR.GetALine();
						tokens = new Tokens(m_IFR.CurrentLineCopy, Tokens.delimiterNormal);
						lLE_Regparams.x2_e.npt = UtilityFuncs.ConvertToDouble(tokens.GetAt(0));
						lLE_Regparams.x2_e.aard = UtilityFuncs.ConvertToDouble(tokens.GetAt(1));
						lLE_Regparams.x2_e.aad = UtilityFuncs.ConvertToDouble(tokens.GetAt(2));
						lLE_Regparams.x2_e.maxd = UtilityFuncs.ConvertToDouble(tokens.GetAt(3));
						myRegparams = lLE_Regparams;
						break;
					}
					case 3:
					{
						HENRY_Regparams hENRY_Regparams = new HENRY_Regparams();
						m_IFR.GetALine();
						tokens = new Tokens(m_IFR.CurrentLineCopy, Tokens.delimiterNormal);
						hENRY_Regparams.sigma = UtilityFuncs.ConvertToDouble(tokens.GetAt(0));
						hENRY_Regparams.valcon = UtilityFuncs.ConvertToDouble(tokens.GetAt(1));
						m_IFR.GetALine();
						tokens = new Tokens(m_IFR.CurrentLineCopy, Tokens.delimiterNormal);
						hENRY_Regparams.temp_l.lower = UtilityFuncs.ConvertToDouble(tokens.GetAt(0));
						hENRY_Regparams.temp_l.upper = UtilityFuncs.ConvertToDouble(tokens.GetAt(1));
						hENRY_Regparams.pres_l.lower = UtilityFuncs.ConvertToDouble(tokens.GetAt(2));
						hENRY_Regparams.pres_l.upper = UtilityFuncs.ConvertToDouble(tokens.GetAt(3));
						m_IFR.GetALine();
						tokens = new Tokens(m_IFR.CurrentLineCopy, Tokens.delimiterNormal);
						hENRY_Regparams.x_l.lower = UtilityFuncs.ConvertToDouble(tokens.GetAt(0));
						hENRY_Regparams.x_l.upper = UtilityFuncs.ConvertToDouble(tokens.GetAt(1));
						hENRY_Regparams.henry_l.lower = UtilityFuncs.ConvertToDouble(tokens.GetAt(2));
						hENRY_Regparams.henry_l.upper = UtilityFuncs.ConvertToDouble(tokens.GetAt(3));
						m_IFR.GetALine();
						tokens = new Tokens(m_IFR.CurrentLineCopy, Tokens.delimiterNormal);

						hENRY_Regparams.henry_e.aard = UtilityFuncs.ConvertToDouble(tokens.GetAt(1));

						hENRY_Regparams.henry_e.maxd = UtilityFuncs.ConvertToDouble(tokens.GetAt(3));
						myRegparams = hENRY_Regparams;
						break;
					}
					default:
						num9 = 0;
						myRegparams = null;
						break;
					}
					int num14 = 0;
					for (int num15 = 0; num15 < num2; num15++)
					{
						string text3 = (string)arrayList[num15];
						int num16 = Convert.ToInt32(arrayList2[num15]);
						int num17 = num14;
						int num18 = num14 + num16;
						ArrayList arrayList7 = new ArrayList();
						for (int num19 = num17; num19 < num18; num19++)
						{
							switch (Convert.ToInt32(arrayList3[num19]))
							{
							case 0:
								arrayList7.Add(arrayList6[num14]);
								num14++;
								arrayList7.Add(arrayList6[num14]);
								num14++;
								break;
							case 1:
								arrayList7.Add(arrayList6[num14]);
								num14++;
								break;
							case 2:
								arrayList7.Add(arrayList6[num14]);
								num14++;
								break;
							case 3:
							{
								double num20 = Convert.ToDouble(arrayList6[num14], CultureInfo.InvariantCulture);
								arrayList7.Add(num20);
								arrayList7.Add(-1.0 * num20);
								num14++;
								break;
							}
							}
						}
						m_DBwriter.AddBinaryParamVector(text, text2, (string)arrayList[num15], bIsPairParam, arrayList7, myRegparams, num9, isDFMS: false);
					}
				}
				num8--;
			}
		}
		return 0;
	}
}
