public class MMTBSParameter
{
	private InputFileReader m_IFR;

	private ReaderLog m_ReaderLog;

	private DBwriter m_DBwriter;

	private static readonly string[] ExcludedParameters = new string[4] { "KLSTD", "SIGSTD", "SLXAS", null };

	public MMTBSParameter(InputFileReader ifr, ReaderLog readerLog, DBwriter dbWriter)
	{
		m_IFR = ifr;
		m_ReaderLog = readerLog;
		m_DBwriter = dbWriter;
	}

	public int ProcessOneParameter()
	{
		Tokens tokens = new Tokens(m_IFR.CurrentLineCopy, Tokens.delimiterNormal);
		if (tokens.Length < 3)
		{
			m_ReaderLog.AddMessageToLog("Error: Incorrect syntax for number of unit types on line " + m_IFR.CurrentLineNo + " of file " + m_IFR.FileName);
			return -1;
		}
		string at = tokens.GetAt(2);
		m_IFR.GetALine();
		string currentLineCopy = m_IFR.CurrentLineCopy;
		currentLineCopy = currentLineCopy.Trim();
		m_IFR.GetALine();
		tokens = new Tokens(m_IFR.CurrentLineCopy, Tokens.delimiterNormal);
		if (tokens.Length < 2)
		{
			m_ReaderLog.AddMessageToLog("Error: Incorrect syntax for load code flags on line " + m_IFR.CurrentLineNo + " of file " + m_IFR.FileName);
			return -1;
		}
		int num = Convert.ToInt32(tokens.GetAt(0));
		int num2 = Convert.ToInt32(tokens.GetAt(1));
		m_IFR.GetALine();
		tokens = new Tokens(m_IFR.CurrentLineCopy, Tokens.delimiterNormal);
		int num3 = Math.Abs(Convert.ToInt32(tokens.GetAt(0)));
		bool flag = false;
		bool flag2 = false;
		bool flag3 = false;
		bool flag4 = false;
		if (tokens.Length > 1)
		{
			try
			{
				if (m_IFR.CurrentLine.Substring(10, 1) == "1")
				{
					flag = true;
				}
				if (m_IFR.CurrentLine.Substring(15, 1) == "1")
				{
					flag2 = true;
				}
				if (m_IFR.CurrentLine.Substring(29, 1) == "1")
				{
					flag3 = true;
				}
				if (m_IFR.CurrentLine.Substring(35, 1) == "1")
				{
					flag4 = true;
				}
			}
			catch
			{
			}
		}
		for (int i = 0; i < num3; i += 4)
		{
			m_IFR.GetALine();
		}
		if (flag)
		{
			for (int i = 0; i < num3; i += 4)
			{
				m_IFR.GetALine();
			}
			for (int i = 0; i < num3; i += 4)
			{
				m_IFR.GetALine();
			}
		}
		if (flag2)
		{
			for (int i = 0; i < num3; i += 4)
			{
				m_IFR.GetALine();
			}
		}
		if (flag3)
		{
			for (int i = 0; i < num3; i += 4)
			{
				m_IFR.GetALine();
			}
		}
		ArrayList arrayList = new ArrayList(num3);
		for (int i = 0; i < num3; i++)
		{
			int num4 = -1;
			if (flag4)
			{
				if (i % 4 == 0)
				{
					m_IFR.GetALine();
					tokens = new Tokens(m_IFR.CurrentLineCopy, Tokens.delimiterNormal);
				}
				if (tokens.Length < i % 4 + 1)
				{
					m_ReaderLog.AddMessageToLog("Error: Incorrect syntax for symmetry values on line " + m_IFR.CurrentLineNo + " of file " + m_IFR.FileName);
					return -1;
				}
				num4 = Convert.ToInt32(tokens.GetAt(i % 4));
			}
			arrayList.Add(num4);
		}
		ArrayList arrayList2 = new ArrayList(num3 * 2);
		if (flag4)
		{
			int num5 = 0;
			for (int j = 0; j < num3; j++)
			{
				int num6 = (int)arrayList[j];
				if (num5 % 4 == 0)
				{
					m_IFR.GetALine();
					tokens = new Tokens(m_IFR.CurrentLineCopy, Tokens.delimiterNormal);
				}
				if (tokens.Length < num5 % 4 + 1)
				{
					m_ReaderLog.AddMessageToLog("Error: Incorrect syntax for element labels on line " + m_IFR.CurrentLineNo + " of file " + m_IFR.FileName);
					break;
				}
				arrayList2.Add(tokens.GetAt(num5 % 4));
				num5++;
				if (num6 != 2)
				{
					if (num5 % 4 == 0)
					{
						m_IFR.GetALine();
						tokens = new Tokens(m_IFR.CurrentLineCopy, Tokens.delimiterNormal);
					}
					if (tokens.Length < num5 % 4 + 1)
					{
						m_ReaderLog.AddMessageToLog("Error: Incorrect syntax for element labels on line " + m_IFR.CurrentLineNo + " of file " + m_IFR.FileName);
						return -1;
					}
					arrayList2.Add(tokens.GetAt(num5 % 4));
					num5++;
				}
			}
		}
		int num7 = 0;
		for (int k = 0; k < num3; k++)
		{
			if (k % 4 == 0)
			{
				m_IFR.GetALine();
				tokens = new Tokens(m_IFR.CurrentLineCopy, Tokens.delimiterNormal);
			}
			if (tokens.Length < k % 4 + 1)
			{
				m_ReaderLog.AddMessageToLog("Error: Incorrect syntax for element UOM on line " + m_IFR.CurrentLineNo + " of file " + m_IFR.FileName);
				return -1;
			}
			int num8 = Convert.ToInt32(tokens.GetAt(k % 4));
			if (num8 < 0)
			{
				num7++;
			}
		}
		m_IFR.GetALine();
		tokens = new Tokens(m_IFR.CurrentLineCopy, Tokens.delimiterNormal);
		int num9 = Convert.ToInt32(tokens.GetAt(0));
		ArrayList arrayList3 = new ArrayList(num9);
		for (int i = 0; i < num9; i++)
		{
			if (i % 4 == 0)
			{
				m_IFR.GetALine();
				tokens = new Tokens(m_IFR.CurrentLineCopy, Tokens.delimiterNormal);
			}
			if (tokens.Length < i % 4 + 1)
			{
				m_ReaderLog.AddMessageToLog("Error: Incorrect syntax for collected conversion codes on line " + m_IFR.CurrentLineNo + " of file " + m_IFR.FileName);
				return -1;
			}
			int num10 = Convert.ToInt32(tokens.GetAt(i % 4));
			if (num10 != 44)
			{
				arrayList3.Add(num10);
			}
		}
		for (int i = 0; i < num7; i++)
		{
			do
			{
				m_IFR.GetALine();
			}
			while (m_IFR.CurrentLine.IndexOf(';') == -1);
		}
		if (num2 == 0 || num == 3 || num == 4 || at.LastIndexOf("PY") == at.Length - 2 || UtilityFuncs.FindStringInList(at, ExcludedParameters, ignoreCase: true, MatchOnlyBeginningOfString: false) != -1)
		{
			return 0;
		}
		try
		{
			m_DBwriter.AddParameter(at, currentLineCopy, num, num3, arrayList, arrayList2, arrayList3);
		}
		catch (Exception)
		{
		}
		return 0;
	}
}
public class MMTBSPureDBank
{
	private InputFileReader m_IFR;

	private ReaderLog m_ReaderLog;

	private DBwriter m_DBwriter;

	public MMTBSPureDBank(ReaderLog readerLog, DBwriter dbWriter)
	{
		m_ReaderLog = readerLog;
		m_DBwriter = dbWriter;
	}

	public void OpenInputFile(string InputFileName)
	{
		m_IFR = new InputFileReader(m_ReaderLog);
		m_IFR.OpenFile(InputFileName);
	}

	public void CloseInputFile()
	{
		m_IFR.CloseFile();
	}

	public int Process()
	{
		int num = 0;
		int num2 = 0;
		num = m_IFR.GetALine();
		if (num == -1)
		{
			m_ReaderLog.AddMessageToLog("Error: End of file at the beginning of " + m_IFR.FileName);
			return -1;
		}
		while (num != -1)
		{
			if (!m_IFR.CurrentLine.StartsWith("/*"))
			{
				if (m_IFR.CurrentLine.StartsWith("DBANK "))
				{
					num = ProcessDatabankInfo();
					if (num == -1)
					{
						return num;
					}
				}
				else
				{
					num = ProcessOneComponent();
					if (num == -1)
					{
						return num;
					}
					num2++;
				}
			}
			num = m_IFR.GetALine();
		}
		return 0;
	}

	private int ProcessDatabankInfo()
	{
		Tokens tokens = new Tokens(m_IFR.CurrentLineCopy, Tokens.delimiterNormal);
		string text = "";
		string text2 = "";
		string text3 = "";
		int length = tokens.Length;
		if (length < 3)
		{
			return -1;
		}
		m_DBwriter.Password = tokens.GetAt(2);
		text = m_DBwriter.Password;
		text2 = text;
		if (m_IFR.GetALine() == -1)
		{
			return -1;
		}
		tokens = new Tokens(m_IFR.CurrentLineCopy, Tokens.delimiterNormal);
		if (tokens.Length < 1)
		{
			return -1;
		}
		text2 = tokens.GetAt(0);
		text3 = "";
		if (tokens.Length > 1)
		{
			text3 = tokens.GetAt(1);
		}
		if (UtilityFuncs.FindStringInList(text2, DFMSconsts.PureDatabankTypes, ignoreCase: true, MatchOnlyBeginningOfString: false) == -1)
		{
			m_ReaderLog.AddMessageToLog("Unknown databank type " + text2 + " on line " + m_IFR.CurrentLineNo);
			return -1;
		}
		if (text2.ToUpperInvariant() == "NONE")
		{
			text2 = m_DBwriter.Password;
		}
		if (text2.ToUpperInvariant().StartsWith("USRPP"))
		{
			if (text3 == "" || string.Compare(text3, "NONE", ignoreCase: true) == 0)
			{
				m_ReaderLog.AddMessageToLog("Password must be specified for USRPP databank, on line " + m_IFR.CurrentLineNo);
				return -1;
			}
			m_DBwriter.Password = text3;
		}
		m_DBwriter.DatabankType = text2;
		m_DBwriter.CreateNewDBank(m_DBwriter.DatabankType, m_DBwriter.Password);
		string at = tokens.GetAt(0);
		if (m_IFR.GetALine() == -1)
		{
			return -1;
		}
		tokens = new Tokens(m_IFR.CurrentLineCopy, Tokens.delimiterNormal);
		string text4 = "";
		bool updateProduct = false;
		if (tokens.Length > 1)
		{
			int num = tokens.Length - 1;
			for (int i = 1; i <= num; i++)
			{
				if (i > 1)
				{
					text4 += " ";
				}
				else
				{
					updateProduct = true;
				}
				text4 += tokens.GetAt(i);
			}
		}
		m_DBwriter.AddPureDatabankInfo(m_DBwriter.Password, text, m_DBwriter.Password, text2, "", text4, UpdateDisplayNameMM: true, UpdatePassword: true, UpdateDatabankType: true, UpdatePromptMM: false, updateProduct);
		return 0;
	}

	private int ProcessOneComponent()
	{
		Tokens tokens = new Tokens(m_IFR.CurrentLineCopy, Tokens.delimiterNormal);
		int length = tokens.Length;
		string text = "";
		string text2 = "";
		IPropertyValue propertyValue = null;
		IPropertyValue propertyValue2 = null;
		if (length < 2)
		{
			return -1;
		}
		text = UtilityFuncs.StripSingleQuotes(tokens.GetAt(0));
		text2 = UtilityFuncs.StripSingleQuotes(tokens.GetAt(1));
		m_DBwriter.AddPureCompound(text2, text);
		if (m_IFR.GetALine() == -1)
		{
			return -1;
		}
		tokens = new Tokens(m_IFR.CurrentLineCopy, Tokens.delimiterNormal);
		if (tokens.Length < 3)
		{
			m_ReaderLog.AddMessageToLog("Error: line " + m_IFR.CurrentLineNo + " does not match documented format till release 12.1");
			return -1;
		}
		if (m_IFR.GetALine() == -1)
		{
			return -1;
		}
		tokens = new Tokens(m_IFR.CurrentLineCopy, Tokens.delimiterNormal);
		if (tokens.Length < 1)
		{
			m_ReaderLog.AddMessageToLog("Error: line " + m_IFR.CurrentLineNo + " does not match documented format till release 12.1");
			return -1;
		}
		string at = tokens.GetAt(0);
		string text3 = "";
		if (tokens.Length > 1)
		{
			text3 = tokens.GetAt(1);
		}
		string text4 = "";
		if (tokens.Length > 2)
		{
			text4 = tokens.GetAt(2);
		}
		string text5 = "";
		if (tokens.Length > 3)
		{
			text5 = tokens.GetAt(3);
		}
		try
		{
			if (at.Trim() != "*" && at.Trim() != "")
			{
				m_DBwriter.AddIdentifierValues(text, "ALIAS", "CASRN", new string[1] { at });
			}
			if (text3.Trim() != "*" && text3.Trim() != "")
			{
				m_DBwriter.AddEnumerationValues(text, "ALIAS", "COMPCLASS", new string[1] { text3 });
			}
			if (text4.Trim() != "*" && !string.IsNullOrEmpty(text4))
			{
				m_DBwriter.AddPropertyValue(null, text, "ALIAS", "HYSIDX", Convert.ToDouble(text4), "", "", "", null, "", out propertyValue);
			}
			if (text5.Trim() != "*" && !string.IsNullOrEmpty(text5))
			{
				m_DBwriter.AddPropertyValue(null, text, "ALIAS", "EDLIDX", Convert.ToDouble(text5), "", "", "", null, "", out propertyValue2);
			}
		}
		catch
		{
			m_ReaderLog.AddMessageToLog("Error: line " + m_IFR.CurrentLineNo + " failed to match alias and add CASRN, Compound Class for " + text);
		}
		return 0;
	}
}
public class MMTBSReactions
{
	private InputFileReader m_IFR;

	private ReaderLog m_ReaderLog;

	private DBwriter m_DBwriter;

	public MMTBSReactions(InputFileReader ifr, ReaderLog readerLog, DBwriter dbWriter, string rxnDbank)
	{
		m_IFR = ifr;
		m_ReaderLog = readerLog;
		m_DBwriter = dbWriter;
		if (m_DBwriter != null)
		{
			m_DBwriter.CreateNewDBank("", rxnDbank);
		}
	}

	public int ProcessOneReaction()
	{
		int num = 0;
		string text = "";
		string reactnBasis = "";
		string text2 = "";
		string symmetricEqConstBasis = "";
		string text3 = "";
		ArrayList arrayList = new ArrayList();
		ArrayList arrayList2 = new ArrayList();
		ArrayList arrayList3 = new ArrayList();
		ArrayList arrayList4 = new ArrayList();
		ArrayList arrayList5 = new ArrayList();
		Tokens tokens = new Tokens(m_IFR.CurrentLineCopy, Tokens.delimiterSlash);
		if (tokens.Length < 3)
		{
			m_ReaderLog.AddMessageToLog("Error: Incorrect syntax for synonyms on line " + m_IFR.CurrentLineNo + " of file " + m_IFR.FileName);
			return -1;
		}
		text = tokens.GetAt(2);
		m_IFR.GetALine();
		tokens = new Tokens(m_IFR.CurrentLineCopy, Tokens.delimiterNormal);
		text2 = tokens.GetAt(0);
		m_IFR.GetALine();
		tokens = new Tokens(m_IFR.CurrentLineCopy, Tokens.delimiterNormal);
		int num2 = 0;
		if (tokens.Length > 0)
		{
			try
			{
				num2 = Convert.ToInt32(tokens.GetAt(0));
			}
			catch
			{
				m_ReaderLog.AddMessageToLog("Error: Could not read number of aliases for " + text + " on line " + m_IFR.CurrentLineNo);
				return -1;
			}
		}
		for (int i = 0; i < num2; i++)
		{
			try
			{
				m_IFR.GetALine();
				tokens = new Tokens(m_IFR.CurrentLineCopy, Tokens.delimiterNormal);
				if (tokens.Length < 2)
				{
					m_ReaderLog.AddMessageToLog("Error: Incorrect syntax for stoichiometric coefficients on line " + m_IFR.CurrentLineNo + " of file " + m_IFR.FileName);
					return -1;
				}
				string at = tokens.GetAt(0);
				string at2 = tokens.GetAt(1);
				arrayList2.Add(at2);
				arrayList.Add(at);
				_ = at != "";
			}
			catch
			{
				m_ReaderLog.AddMessageToLog("Error: Could not add synonym for alias " + text + " on line " + m_IFR.CurrentLineNo + " of file " + m_IFR.FileName);
			}
		}
		m_IFR.GetALine();
		tokens = new Tokens(m_IFR.CurrentLineCopy, Tokens.delimiterNormal);
		if (tokens.Length != 1 || !(tokens.GetAt(0) == "*"))
		{
			for (int j = 0; j < tokens.Length; j++)
			{
				arrayList5.Add(tokens.GetAt(j));
			}
			m_IFR.GetALine();
			tokens = new Tokens(m_IFR.CurrentLineCopy, Tokens.delimiterNormal);
			if (tokens.Length != 2)
			{
				m_ReaderLog.AddMessageToLog("Error: Could not read reaction basis for " + text + " on line " + m_IFR.CurrentLineNo);
				return -1;
			}
			text3 = tokens.GetAt(0);
			if (Convert.ToInt32(tokens.GetAt(1)) == 0)
			{
				reactnBasis = text3;
				for (int k = 0; k < arrayList5.Count; k++)
				{
					arrayList3.Add(arrayList5[k]);
				}
			}
			else
			{
				symmetricEqConstBasis = text3;
				for (int l = 0; l < arrayList5.Count; l++)
				{
					arrayList4.Add(arrayList5[l]);
				}
			}
			m_IFR.GetALine();
			tokens = new Tokens(m_IFR.CurrentLineCopy, Tokens.delimiterNormal);
			if (tokens.Length != 1 || !(tokens.GetAt(0) == "*"))
			{
				arrayList5.Clear();
				for (int m = 0; m < tokens.Length; m++)
				{
					arrayList5.Add(tokens.GetAt(m));
				}
				m_IFR.GetALine();
				tokens = new Tokens(m_IFR.CurrentLineCopy, Tokens.delimiterNormal);
				text3 = tokens.GetAt(0);
				if (Convert.ToInt32(tokens.GetAt(1)) == 0)
				{
					reactnBasis = text3;
					for (int n = 0; n < arrayList5.Count; n++)
					{
						arrayList3.Add(arrayList5[n]);
					}
				}
				else
				{
					symmetricEqConstBasis = text3;
					for (int num3 = 0; num3 < arrayList5.Count; num3++)
					{
						arrayList4.Add(arrayList5[num3]);
					}
				}
			}
		}
		m_DBwriter.AddReaction(text, text2, arrayList, arrayList2, arrayList3, reactnBasis, arrayList4, symmetricEqConstBasis);
		return 0;
	}
}
public class MMTBSswitch
{
	private InputFileReader m_IFR;

	private ReaderLog m_ReaderLog;

	private DBwriter m_DBwriter;

	public MMTBSswitch(ReaderLog readerLog, DBwriter dbWriter)
	{
		m_ReaderLog = readerLog;
		m_DBwriter = dbWriter;
	}

	public void OpenInputFile(string InputFileName)
	{
		m_IFR = new InputFileReader(m_ReaderLog);
		m_IFR.OpenFile(InputFileName);
	}

	public void CloseInputFile()
	{
		m_IFR.CloseFile();
	}

	public int Process(ref string errMessage)
	{
		errMessage = null;
		int num = 0;
		string rxnDbank = "";
		num = m_IFR.GetALine();
		if (num == -1)
		{
			m_ReaderLog.AddMessageToLog("Error: End of file at the beginning of " + m_IFR.FileName);
			return -1;
		}
		while (num != -1)
		{
			string text = m_IFR.CurrentLineCopy.ToUpperInvariant();
			if (text.StartsWith("/*"))
			{
				int num2 = text.IndexOf("RXNDBANK");
				rxnDbank = ((num2 == -1) ? "REACTIONS" : text.Substring(num2 + 8).Trim());
			}
			else if (!text.StartsWith("C$"))
			{
				if (text.StartsWith("DBANK"))
				{
					Tokens tokens = new Tokens(m_IFR.CurrentLineCopy, Tokens.delimiterSlash);
					if (tokens.Length > 2)
					{
						if (string.Compare(tokens.GetAt(2), "GAMKIJ", ignoreCase: true) == 0)
						{
							MMTBSBinaryDatabank mMTBSBinaryDatabank = new MMTBSBinaryDatabank(m_IFR, m_ReaderLog, m_DBwriter);
							num = mMTBSBinaryDatabank.ProcessBinaryDatabank(ref errMessage);
							if (num == -1)
							{
								return num;
							}
						}
						else if (string.Compare(tokens.GetAt(1), "HELP", ignoreCase: true) != 0)
						{
							m_ReaderLog.AddMessageToLog("Error: Pure databanks are processed separately. Parser error. Contach AspenTech");
							return -1;
						}
					}
					if (num == -1)
					{
						return num;
					}
				}
				else if (text.StartsWith("SYNONYMS"))
				{
					Tokens tokens = new Tokens(m_IFR.CurrentLineCopy, Tokens.delimiterNormal);
					if (tokens.Length < 3)
					{
						m_ReaderLog.AddMessageToLog("Error: Incorrect syntax for synonyms on line " + m_IFR.CurrentLineNo + " of file " + m_IFR.FileName);
						return -1;
					}
					string at = tokens.GetAt(2);
					m_IFR.GetALine();
					tokens = new Tokens(m_IFR.CurrentLineCopy, Tokens.delimiterNormal);
					int num3 = 0;
					if (tokens.Length <= 0)
					{
						return 0;
					}
					try
					{
						num3 = Convert.ToInt32(tokens.GetAt(0));
					}
					catch
					{
						m_ReaderLog.AddMessageToLog("Error: Could not read number of aliases for " + at + " on line " + m_IFR.CurrentLineNo);
						return -1;
					}
					MMTBSSynonyms mMTBSSynonyms = new MMTBSSynonyms(m_IFR, m_ReaderLog, m_DBwriter, num3, at);
					num = mMTBSSynonyms.ProcessOneSynonym();
					if (num == -1)
					{
						return num;
					}
				}
				else if (text.StartsWith("REACTNS"))
				{
					MMTBSReactions mMTBSReactions = new MMTBSReactions(m_IFR, m_ReaderLog, m_DBwriter, rxnDbank);
					num = mMTBSReactions.ProcessOneReaction();
					if (num == -1)
					{
						return num;
					}
				}
				else if (text.StartsWith("ADD UNITS"))
				{
					MMTBSUnits mMTBSUnits = new MMTBSUnits(m_IFR, m_ReaderLog, m_DBwriter);
					num = mMTBSUnits.ProcessUnits();
					if (num == -1)
					{
						return num;
					}
				}
				else if (text.StartsWith("PPLCDEFS"))
				{
					MMTBSParameter mMTBSParameter = new MMTBSParameter(m_IFR, m_ReaderLog, m_DBwriter);
					num = mMTBSParameter.ProcessOneParameter();
					if (num == -1)
					{
						return num;
					}
				}
			}
			num = m_IFR.GetALine();
		}
		return 0;
	}
}
public class MMTBSSynonyms
{
	private InputFileReader m_IFR;

	private ReaderLog m_ReaderLog;

	private DBwriter m_DBwriter;

	private int m_NumSynonyms;

	private string m_Alias;

	public MMTBSSynonyms(InputFileReader ifr, ReaderLog readerLog, DBwriter dbWriter, int numSynonyms, string alias)
	{
		m_IFR = ifr;
		m_ReaderLog = readerLog;
		m_DBwriter = dbWriter;
		m_NumSynonyms = numSynonyms;
		m_Alias = alias;
		m_DBwriter.AddIdentifier("SYNONYM", "Alternate Names", allowMultiples: true);
	}

	public int ProcessOneSynonym()
	{
		string[] array = new string[m_NumSynonyms];
		for (int i = 0; i < m_NumSynonyms; i++)
		{
			try
			{
				m_IFR.GetALine();
				array[i] = m_IFR.CurrentLineCopy.Trim();
			}
			catch
			{
				m_ReaderLog.AddMessageToLog("Error: Could not read synonym for alias " + m_Alias + " on line " + m_IFR.CurrentLineNo + " of file " + m_IFR.FileName);
			}
		}
		IDatabankCollection databankList = m_DBwriter.GetDatabankList();
		string text = "";
		if (databankList != null && array != null && array.Length != 0)
		{
			if (m_DBwriter.CheckCompAliasExists(m_Alias))
			{
				try
				{
					string[] array2 = new string[1];
					for (int j = 0; j < array.Length; j++)
					{
						array2[0] = array[j];
						try
						{
							m_DBwriter.AddIdentifierValues(m_Alias, "ALIAS", "SYNONYM", array2);
						}
						catch (Exception ex)
						{
							m_ReaderLog.AddMessageToLog("Failed to add synonyms to database for compound with Alias : " + m_Alias + "\n" + ex.Message);
						}
						array2[0] = "";
					}
				}
				catch (Exception ex2)
				{
					m_ReaderLog.AddMessageToLog("Failed to add synonyms to database for compound with Alias : " + m_Alias + "\n" + ex2.Message);
				}
			}
			_ = text != "";
		}
		return 0;
	}
}
public class MMTBSUnits
{
	private InputFileReader m_IFR;

	private ReaderLog m_ReaderLog;

	private DBwriter m_DBwriter;

	public MMTBSUnits(InputFileReader ifr, ReaderLog readerLog, DBwriter dbWriter)
	{
		m_IFR = ifr;
		m_ReaderLog = readerLog;
		m_DBwriter = dbWriter;
	}

	public int ProcessUnits()
	{
		m_IFR.GetALine();
		Tokens tokens = new Tokens(m_IFR.CurrentLineCopy, Tokens.delimiterNormal);
		if (tokens.Length < 3 || tokens.GetAt(1) != "+")
		{
			m_ReaderLog.AddMessageToLog("Error: Incorrect syntax for number of unit types on line " + m_IFR.CurrentLineNo + " of file " + m_IFR.FileName);
			return -1;
		}
		int num = 0;
		try
		{
			num = Convert.ToInt32(tokens.GetAt(0)) + Convert.ToInt32(tokens.GetAt(2));
		}
		catch
		{
			m_ReaderLog.AddMessageToLog("Error: Incorrect syntax for number of unit types on line " + m_IFR.CurrentLineNo + " of file " + m_IFR.FileName);
			return -1;
		}
		for (int i = 0; i < num; i++)
		{
			m_IFR.GetALine();
			while (m_IFR.CurrentLine.StartsWith("#"))
			{
				if (m_IFR.CurrentLine.ToUpperInvariant().StartsWith("#IFDEF"))
				{
					do
					{
						m_IFR.GetALine();
					}
					while (!m_IFR.CurrentLine.StartsWith("#"));
				}
				m_IFR.GetALine();
			}
			tokens = new Tokens(m_IFR.CurrentLineCopy, Tokens.delimiterNormal);
			if (tokens.Length < 3)
			{
				m_ReaderLog.AddMessageToLog("Error: Incorrect syntax for unit type on line " + m_IFR.CurrentLineNo + " of file " + m_IFR.FileName);
				return -1;
			}
			int num2 = 0;
			int num3 = 0;
			try
			{
				num2 = Convert.ToInt32(tokens.GetAt(0));
				num3 = Convert.ToInt32(tokens.GetAt(1));
			}
			catch
			{
				m_ReaderLog.AddMessageToLog("Error: Could not read unit row on line " + m_IFR.CurrentLineNo);
				return -1;
			}
			if (num3 > 0)
			{
				string at = tokens.GetAt(2);
				m_IFR.GetALine();
				tokens = new Tokens(m_IFR.CurrentLineCopy, Tokens.delimiterNormal);
				if (tokens.Length < 1)
				{
					m_ReaderLog.AddMessageToLog("Error: Incorrect syntax for UOM on line " + m_IFR.CurrentLineNo + " of file " + m_IFR.FileName);
					return -1;
				}
				m_DBwriter.AddUnitRow(num2, at, tokens.GetAt(0));
				while (--num3 > 0)
				{
					m_IFR.GetALine();
				}
			}
		}
		return 0;
	}
}
public class MolReader
{
	private ReaderLog m_ReaderLog;

	private DBwriter m_DBwriter;

	private string m_AccessFileName;

	private OleDbConnection m_Connection;

	private int StructureType = 1;

	private CompressorClass myCompressor;

	public MolReader(ReaderLog readerLog, DBwriter dbWriter)
	{
		//IL_001c: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
		//IL_0026: Expected O, but got Unknown
		m_ReaderLog = readerLog;
		m_DBwriter = dbWriter;
		myCompressor = new CompressorClass();
	}

	public void OpenAccessFile(string AccessFileName)
	{
		m_AccessFileName = AccessFileName;
		try
		{
			string connectionString = "Provider=Microsoft.Jet.OLEDB.4.0;Data Source=" + m_AccessFileName + ";";
			m_Connection = new OleDbConnection(connectionString);
			if (m_Connection.State != ConnectionState.Open)
			{
				m_Connection.Open();
			}
			m_ReaderLog.AddMessageToLog("Begin processing file: " + m_AccessFileName);
		}
		catch
		{
			m_ReaderLog.AddMessageToLog("ERROR: Could not open file " + m_AccessFileName);
			throw new Exception("Could not open file " + m_AccessFileName);
		}
	}

	public void CloseAccessFile()
	{
		if (m_Connection != null && m_Connection.State == ConnectionState.Open)
		{
			m_Connection.Close();
			m_ReaderLog.AddMessageToLog("End processing file: " + m_AccessFileName);
		}
	}

	public int Process()
	{
		if (m_Connection == null || m_Connection.State != ConnectionState.Open)
		{
			return -1;
		}
		IDbCommand dbCommand = null;
		IDataReader dataReader = null;
		IDbConnection connection = m_Connection;
		if (connection.State != ConnectionState.Open)
		{
			connection.Open();
		}
		try
		{
			dbCommand = connection.CreateCommand();
			dbCommand.CommandText = "SELECT Purecomp.CASNumber, compmol.casId, Purecomp.COMPONENT, Purecomp.ALIAS,  Purecomp.CompressedMolfile, compmol.molfile, compmol.synonyms FROM Purecomp LEFT JOIN compmol ON PureComp.id = CompMol.id";
			dbCommand.CommandType = CommandType.Text;
			dataReader = dbCommand.ExecuteReader();
			string text = "";
			string text2 = "";
			string text3 = "";
			string text4 = "";
			string[] array = null;
			while (dataReader.Read())
			{
				text = "";
				text2 = "";
				text3 = "";
				text4 = "";
				array = null;
				if (!dataReader.IsDBNull(0))
				{
					text3 = dataReader.GetString(0).Trim();
					if (text3 != "" && text3.IndexOf("-") == -1)
					{
						int length = text3.Length;
						text3 = text3.Substring(0, length - 3) + "-" + text3.Substring(length - 3, 2) + "-" + text3.Substring(length - 1, 1);
					}
				}
				else if (!dataReader.IsDBNull(1))
				{
					text3 = dataReader.GetString(1).Trim();
				}
				if (!dataReader.IsDBNull(2))
				{
					text = dataReader.GetString(2).Trim();
				}
				if (!dataReader.IsDBNull(3))
				{
					text2 = dataReader.GetString(3).Trim();
				}
				if (!dataReader.IsDBNull(4))
				{
					text4 = dataReader.GetString(4).Trim();
				}
				else if (!dataReader.IsDBNull(5))
				{
					string decompressedString = dataReader.GetString(5).Trim();
					myCompressor.DecompressedString = decompressedString;
					text4 = myCompressor.CompressedString.Trim();
				}
				if (!dataReader.IsDBNull(6))
				{
					string text5 = dataReader.GetString(6).Trim();
					text5 = text5.Replace("&laquo;", "");
					text5 = text5.Replace("&raquo;", "");
					text5 = text5.Replace("&amp;", "&");
					text5 = text5.Replace("&quot;", "''");
					array = text5.Split(';');
				}
				if (text3 != "" && m_DBwriter.CheckCompIdentifierExists(text3, "CASRN"))
				{
					if (array != null)
					{
						for (int i = 0; i < array.Length; i++)
						{
							try
							{
								m_DBwriter.AddIdentifierValues(text3, "CASRN", "SYNONYM", new string[1] { array[i] });
							}
							catch
							{
							}
						}
					}
					if (text4 != null && text4 != "")
					{
						m_DBwriter.AddStructureByName(text3, "CASRN", text4, StructureType);
					}
				}
				else if (text2 != null && text2 != "" && m_DBwriter.CheckCompIdentifierExists(text2, "ALIAS"))
				{
					if (array != null)
					{
						for (int j = 0; j < array.Length; j++)
						{
							try
							{
								m_DBwriter.AddIdentifierValues(text2, "ALIAS", "SYNONYM", new string[1] { array[j] });
							}
							catch
							{
							}
						}
					}
					if (text4 != null && text4 != "")
					{
						m_DBwriter.AddStructureByName(text2, "ALIAS", text4, StructureType);
					}
					if (text3 != "")
					{
						m_DBwriter.AddIdentifierValues(text2, "ALIAS", "CASRN", new string[1] { text3 });
					}
				}
				else
				{
					if (text == null || !(text != "") || !m_DBwriter.CheckCompIdentifierExists(text, "NAME"))
					{
						continue;
					}
					if (array != null)
					{
						for (int k = 0; k < array.Length; k++)
						{
							try
							{
								m_DBwriter.AddIdentifierValues(text, "NAME", "SYNONYM", new string[1] { array[k] });
							}
							catch
							{
							}
						}
					}
					if (text4 != null && text4 != "")
					{
						m_DBwriter.AddStructureByName(text, "NAME", text4, StructureType);
					}
					if (text3 != "")
					{
						m_DBwriter.AddIdentifierValues(text, "NAME", "CASRN", new string[1] { text3 });
					}
				}
			}
			dataReader.Close();
		}
		finally
		{
			if (dataReader != null && !dataReader.IsClosed)
			{
				dataReader.Close();
			}
			dbCommand?.Dispose();
			connection.Close();
		}
		return 0;
	}
}
public class NISTreader
{
	private InputFileReader m_IFR;

	private ReaderLog m_ReaderLog;

	private DBwriter m_DBwriter;

	private ArrayList m_PropListList;

	private ArrayList m_CompListList;

	private SortedList propNumElemsList;

	private string m_error = string.Empty;

	private string m_CurrentFile = string.Empty;

	private IDatabank m_Databank;

	private ICitationManager m_CitationManager;

	private static Dictionary<string, nCompound> m_NISTCompoundList;

	private static Dictionary<string, nCompound> m_NISTOldCompoundList;

	private static Dictionary<string, nFormula> m_NISTFormulaList;

	public NISTreader(ReaderLog readerLog, DBwriter dbWriter)
	{
		m_ReaderLog = readerLog;
		m_DBwriter = dbWriter;
		m_CompListList = new ArrayList();
		m_PropListList = new ArrayList();
		m_CompListList.Clear();
		m_PropListList.Clear();
		propNumElemsList = new SortedList();
	}

	public NISTreader()
	{
	}

	public void OpenNistFile(string InputFileName)
	{
		m_IFR = new InputFileReader(m_ReaderLog);
		m_IFR.OpenFile(InputFileName);
	}

	public void ReportFileFailure(string error)
	{
		m_IFR.ReportFileFailure(error);
	}

	public void CloseNistFile()
	{
		m_IFR.CloseFile();
	}

	[DllImport("managedthermomlData.dll")]
	public static extern void ProcessXML(string InputFileName, DBwriter m_DBwriter, ReaderLog m_ReaderLog);

	public int Process(string InputFileName)
	{
		m_CurrentFile = InputFileName;
		Processdatabank();
		if (InputFileName.Contains("binary"))
		{
			ProcessXML(InputFileName, m_DBwriter, m_ReaderLog);
		}
		else if (InputFileName.Contains("single2.xml") || InputFileName.Contains("water2.xml"))
		{
			ProcessXML(InputFileName, m_DBwriter, m_ReaderLog);
		}
		else
		{
			ProcessXMLFile(InputFileName);
		}
		return 1;
	}

	public int ProcessNewComp(string CompName, string CompAlias, string ComRegN)
	{
		if (CompName != null && CompAlias != null)
		{
			m_DBwriter.AddPureCompound(CompName, CompAlias);
			return 1;
		}
		return 0;
	}

	private void Processdatabank()
	{
		m_DBwriter.DatabankType = "NIST-TRC";
		m_DBwriter.Password = "NIST-TRC";
		m_DBwriter.CreateNewDBank(m_DBwriter.DatabankType, m_DBwriter.Password);
		m_DBwriter.AddPureDatabankInfo(m_DBwriter.Password, "", m_DBwriter.Password, m_DBwriter.DatabankType, "", "", UpdateDisplayNameMM: false, UpdatePassword: true, UpdateDatabankType: true, UpdatePromptMM: false, UpdateProduct: false);
		SetDatabankAndCitationManager();
	}

	public void SetDatabankAndCitationManager()
	{
		//IL_0027: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
		//IL_002d: Expected O, but got Unknown
		IDatabankCollection databankList = m_DBwriter.GetDatabankList();
		if (databankList != null && ((ICollection)databankList).Count > 0)
		{
			foreach (IDatabank item in (IEnumerable)databankList)
			{
				IDatabank val = item;
				if (val.Name.CompareTo("NIST-TRC") == 0)
				{
					m_Databank = val;
					break;
				}
			}
		}
		if (m_Databank != null)
		{
			IDatabase database = m_Databank.Database;
			if (database != null)
			{
				ISupportsCitations val2 = (ISupportsCitations)(object)((database is ISupportsCitations) ? database : null);
				m_CitationManager = val2.CitationManager;
			}
		}
	}

	private void ProcessXMLFile(string inputFile)
	{
		XmlDocument xmlDocument = new XmlDocument();
		xmlDocument.Load(inputFile);
		XmlNodeList xmlNodeList = xmlDocument.SelectNodes("/DataReport/NewCompound");
		if (xmlNodeList != null && xmlNodeList.Count > 0)
		{
			for (int i = 0; i < xmlNodeList.Count; i++)
			{
				XmlNode xmlNewCompoundNode = xmlNodeList[i];
				ProcessEachNewCompound(xmlNewCompoundNode);
			}
			return;
		}
		if (m_NISTCompoundList == null)
		{
			m_NISTCompoundList = new Dictionary<string, nCompound>();
			m_NISTOldCompoundList = new Dictionary<string, nCompound>();
			m_NISTFormulaList = new Dictionary<string, nFormula>();
			LoadNISTOut();
			LoadFornulaOut();
		}
		XmlNodeList xmlNodeList2 = xmlDocument.SelectNodes("/DataReport/Compound");
		if (xmlNodeList2 == null || xmlNodeList2.Count <= 0)
		{
			return;
		}
		foreach (XmlNode item in xmlNodeList2)
		{
			ParseCompound(item);
		}
		if (m_NISTCompoundList.Count > 0)
		{
			ReportNISTCompounds();
			ReportNISTSynonyms();
			ReportNISTLongFormula();
			m_NISTCompoundList.Clear();
		}
	}

	private void ProcessEachNewCompound(XmlNode xmlNewCompoundNode)
	{
		xThNewCompound xThNewCompound2 = new xThNewCompound(xmlNewCompoundNode, m_CurrentFile, m_Databank, m_CitationManager, m_DBwriter, m_ReaderLog);
		xThNewCompound2.ParseCompound();
		xThNewCompound2.ParsePureParameters();
	}

	public void ParseCompound(XmlNode node)
	{
		string text = string.Empty;
		int num = 0;
		string formula = string.Empty;
		string text2 = string.Empty;
		List<string> list = new List<string>();
		try
		{
			foreach (XmlNode childNode in node.ChildNodes)
			{
				switch (childNode.Name)
				{
				case "RegNum":
				{
					XmlNode xmlNode2 = childNode.SelectSingleNode("nCASRNum");
					if (xmlNode2 == null)
					{
						xmlNode2 = childNode.SelectSingleNode("nOrgNum");
					}
					text = GetCASRN(xmlNode2.InnerText);
					break;
				}
				case "sFormulaMolec":
					formula = childNode.InnerText;
					break;
				case "sCommonName":
				{
					num++;
					string text3 = childNode.InnerText.ToUpperInvariant();
					if (num == 1)
					{
						text2 = text3;
					}
					else if (text3.Length < text2.Length)
					{
						text2 = text3;
					}
					list.Add(text3);
					break;
				}
				}
			}
			if (m_NISTCompoundList.ContainsKey(text))
			{
				nCompound nCompound2 = m_NISTCompoundList[text];
				nCompound2.Nodes++;
				m_NISTCompoundList[text] = nCompound2;
			}
			else
			{
				nCompound value = new nCompound(text, text2, formula, 1, list);
				m_NISTCompoundList.Add(text, value);
			}
		}
		catch (Exception ex)
		{
			m_error = ex.Message;
		}
	}

	private string GetCASRN(string line)
	{
		string result = string.Empty;
		string text = string.Empty;
		if (line.Contains("<nCASRNum>") && line.Contains("</nCASRNum>"))
		{
			text = line.Replace("<nCASRNum>", "").Replace("</nCASRNum>", "").Trim();
		}
		else if (line.Contains("<nOrgNum>") && line.Contains("</nOrgNum>"))
		{
			text = line.Replace("<nOrgNum>", "").Replace("</nOrgNum>", "").Trim();
		}
		if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(text))
		{
			int length = text.Length;
			text = text.Insert(length - 1, "-");
			result = text.Insert(length - 3, "-");
		}
		return result;
	}

	private void LoadNISTOut()
	{
		string path = "C:\\dev\\data\\NISTData\\Master\\NIST.out";
		StreamReader streamReader = File.OpenText(path);
		string text;
		while (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(text = streamReader.ReadLine()))
		{
			string[] array = text.Split((string[])null, StringSplitOptions.RemoveEmptyEntries);
			nCompound nCompound2 = new nCompound();
			nCompound2.CASRN = array[2];
			nCompound2.OldAlias = array[1];
			nCompound2.OldName = array[0];
			m_NISTOldCompoundList.Add(array[2], nCompound2);
		}
		if (streamReader != null)
		{
			streamReader.Close();
			streamReader.DiscardBufferedData();
			streamReader.Dispose();
		}
	}

	private void LoadFornulaOut()
	{
		string empty = string.Empty;
		string path = "C:\\dev\\data\\NISTData\\Master\\aliasnum.out";
		StreamReader streamReader = File.OpenText(path);
		while (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(empty = streamReader.ReadLine()))
		{
			string empty2 = string.Empty;
			string[] array = empty.Split((string[])null, StringSplitOptions.RemoveEmptyEntries);
			int num = Convert.ToInt32(array[1]);
			if (array[0].Contains("-"))
			{
				string[] array2 = array[0].Split('-');
				empty2 = array2[0];
				if (m_NISTFormulaList.ContainsKey(empty2))
				{
					nFormula nFormula2 = m_NISTFormulaList[empty2];
					if (array2[1].CompareTo("D") == 0)
					{
						nFormula2.DNum = num;
						m_NISTFormulaList[empty2] = nFormula2;
						continue;
					}
					if (array2[1].CompareTo("A") == 0)
					{
						nFormula2.ANum = num;
						m_NISTFormulaList[empty2] = nFormula2;
						continue;
					}
					int num2 = CanBeConverted(array2[1]);
					if (num2 != -1)
					{
						nFormula2.ONum = num2;
						m_NISTFormulaList[empty2] = nFormula2;
					}
					continue;
				}
				nFormula nFormula3 = new nFormula();
				nFormula3.Formula = empty2;
				if (array2[1].CompareTo("D") == 0)
				{
					nFormula3.DNum = Convert.ToInt32(array[1]);
					m_NISTFormulaList.Add(empty2, nFormula3);
					continue;
				}
				if (array2[1].CompareTo("A") == 0)
				{
					nFormula3.ANum = Convert.ToInt32(array[1]);
					m_NISTFormulaList.Add(empty2, nFormula3);
					continue;
				}
				int num3 = CanBeConverted(array2[1]);
				if (num3 != -1)
				{
					nFormula3.ONum = num3;
					m_NISTFormulaList.Add(empty2, nFormula3);
				}
			}
			else
			{
				empty2 = array[0];
				if (m_NISTFormulaList.ContainsKey(empty2))
				{
					nFormula nFormula4 = m_NISTFormulaList[empty2];
					nFormula4.NNum = num;
					m_NISTFormulaList[empty2] = nFormula4;
				}
				else
				{
					nFormula nFormula5 = new nFormula();
					nFormula5.Formula = empty2;
					nFormula5.NNum = num;
					m_NISTFormulaList.Add(empty2, nFormula5);
				}
			}
		}
		if (streamReader != null)
		{
			streamReader.Close();
			streamReader.DiscardBufferedData();
			streamReader.Dispose();
		}
	}

	private void ReportNISTCompounds()
	{
		string fileName = "C:\\dev\\data\\NISTData\\Pure\\Output\\NISTCompounds.out";
		FileInfo fileInfo = new FileInfo(fileName);
		StreamWriter streamWriter = fileInfo.AppendText();
		string empty = string.Empty;
		foreach (nCompound value in m_NISTCompoundList.Values)
		{
			empty = MakelineR(value.CASRN, 20) + MakelineR(value.Forumula, 20) + MakelineR(value.Nodes.ToString(), 5) + value.Name;
			streamWriter.WriteLine(empty);
		}
		if (streamWriter != null)
		{
			streamWriter.Flush();
			streamWriter.Close();
			streamWriter.Dispose();
		}
	}

	private void UpdateNISTOut()
	{
		foreach (string key in m_NISTCompoundList.Keys)
		{
			if (!m_NISTOldCompoundList.ContainsKey(key))
			{
				continue;
			}
			nCompound nCompound2 = m_NISTCompoundList[key];
			nCompound nCompound3 = m_NISTOldCompoundList[key];
			if (nCompound2.Name.CompareTo(nCompound3.Name) != 0)
			{
				if (nCompound2.Name.Length <= 32)
				{
					nCompound2.NewName = nCompound2.Name;
					continue;
				}
				if (nCompound3.Name.Length <= 32)
				{
					nCompound2.NewName = nCompound3.Name;
					continue;
				}
				_ = nCompound2.Forumula.Length;
				_ = 12;
			}
		}
	}

	private void ReportNISTSynonyms()
	{
		string fileName = "C:\\dev\\data\\NISTData\\Pure\\Output\\NISTSynonyms.out";
		FileInfo fileInfo = new FileInfo(fileName);
		StreamWriter streamWriter = fileInfo.AppendText();
		string empty = string.Empty;
		foreach (nCompound value in m_NISTCompoundList.Values)
		{
			List<string> synonym = value.Synonym;
			if (synonym == null || synonym.Count <= 0)
			{
				continue;
			}
			streamWriter.WriteLine(value.CASRN);
			foreach (string item in synonym)
			{
				empty = "    " + item;
				streamWriter.WriteLine(empty);
			}
		}
		if (streamWriter != null)
		{
			streamWriter.Flush();
			streamWriter.Close();
			streamWriter.Dispose();
		}
	}

	private void ReportNISTLongFormula()
	{
		string fileName = "C:\\dev\\data\\NISTData\\Pure\\Output\\NISTFormula.out";
		FileInfo fileInfo = new FileInfo(fileName);
		StreamWriter streamWriter = fileInfo.AppendText();
		string empty = string.Empty;
		foreach (nCompound value in m_NISTCompoundList.Values)
		{
			if (value.Forumula.Length > 12)
			{
				empty = MakelineR(value.CASRN, 20) + MakelineR(value.Forumula, 20) + MakelineR(value.Nodes.ToString(), 5) + value.Name;
				streamWriter.WriteLine(empty);
			}
		}
		if (streamWriter != null)
		{
			streamWriter.Flush();
			streamWriter.Close();
			streamWriter.Dispose();
		}
	}

	private int CanBeConverted(string d)
	{
		int num = -1;
		try
		{
			num = Convert.ToInt32(d);
		}
		catch (Exception ex)
		{
			num = -1;
			m_error = ex.Message;
		}
		return num;
	}

	private string MakelineR(string line, int length)
	{
		string empty = string.Empty;
		int length2 = line.Length;
		int num = length - length2;
		string text = string.Empty;
		for (int i = 1; i <= num; i++)
		{
			text += " ";
		}
		return line + text;
	}

	private string MakelineL(string line, int length)
	{
		string empty = string.Empty;
		int length2 = line.Length;
		int num = length - length2;
		string text = string.Empty;
		for (int i = 1; i <= num; i++)
		{
			text += " ";
		}
		return text + line;
	}
}
public class Processor
{
	private ReaderLog myReaderLog;

	private DBwriter myDBwriter;

	private IDatabank myMergeToDbank;

	public Processor(string logfile)
	{
		if (logfile == null || logfile == "")
		{
			throw new GatewayException("You must specify log file.");
		}
		myReaderLog = new ReaderLog(logfile, deleteOld: false, ReaderLog.LogType.File);
	}

	public Processor()
	{
		myReaderLog = new ReaderLog("", deleteOld: false, ReaderLog.LogType.NoLog);
	}

	public bool SetErrorLog(ReaderLog.LogType logType, bool ClearOldLog, string name)
	{
		myReaderLog.SetErrorLog(logType, ClearOldLog, name);
		return true;
	}

	public void CloseErrorLog()
	{
		if (myReaderLog != null)
		{
			myReaderLog.CloseLog();
			myReaderLog = null;
		}
	}

	public bool CreateDBwriter(IDatabase database, bool ValidationMode)
	{
		try
		{
			myDBwriter = new DBwriter(database, myReaderLog, ValidationMode);
			return true;
		}
		catch (Exception ex)
		{
			myReaderLog.AddMessageToLog("ERROR: failed to CreateDBwriter" + ex.Message);
			return false;
		}
	}

	public bool CreateDBwriter(string DBalias, string DBname, string initialCatalog, string username, string password, DatabaseProviderType dbpType, bool ValidationMode)
	{
		//IL_0008: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
		try
		{
			myDBwriter = new DBwriter(DBalias, DBname, initialCatalog, username, password, dbpType, myReaderLog, ValidationMode);
			return true;
		}
		catch (Exception ex)
		{
			myReaderLog.AddMessageToLog("ERROR: failed to CreateDBwriter" + ex.Message);
			return false;
		}
	}

	public void SetDatabankToMergeData(IDatabank MergeToThisDbank)
	{
		myMergeToDbank = MergeToThisDbank;
	}

	public bool ProcessFile(string filename, ref string errMessage)
	{
		errMessage = null;
		bool result = false;
		switch (UtilityFuncs.DetermineFileType(filename))
		{
		case UtilityFuncs.FileType.Dfms:
		{
			DFMSreader dFMSreader = new DFMSreader(myReaderLog, myDBwriter);
			dFMSreader.OpenDFMSFile(filename);
			dFMSreader.Process(ref errMessage);
			dFMSreader.CloseDFMSFile();
			GC.Collect();
			GC.WaitForPendingFinalizers();
			break;
		}
		case UtilityFuncs.FileType.Mmtbs:
		{
			MMTBSswitch mMTBSswitch = new MMTBSswitch(myReaderLog, myDBwriter);
			mMTBSswitch.OpenInputFile(filename);
			mMTBSswitch.Process(ref errMessage);
			mMTBSswitch.CloseInputFile();
			GC.Collect();
			GC.WaitForPendingFinalizers();
			break;
		}
		case UtilityFuncs.FileType.MmtbsPureDBank:
		{
			MMTBSPureDBank mMTBSPureDBank = new MMTBSPureDBank(myReaderLog, myDBwriter);
			mMTBSPureDBank.OpenInputFile(filename);
			mMTBSPureDBank.Process();
			mMTBSPureDBank.CloseInputFile();
			GC.Collect();
			GC.WaitForPendingFinalizers();
			break;
		}
		case UtilityFuncs.FileType.MmtbsHxt:
		{
			MMTBShxt mMTBShxt = new MMTBShxt(myReaderLog, myDBwriter);
			mMTBShxt.OpenInputFile(filename);
			mMTBShxt.Process();
			mMTBShxt.CloseInputFile();
			GC.Collect();
			GC.WaitForPendingFinalizers();
			break;
		}
		case UtilityFuncs.FileType.Mol:
		{
			MolReader molReader = new MolReader(myReaderLog, myDBwriter);
			molReader.OpenAccessFile(filename);
			molReader.Process();
			molReader.CloseAccessFile();
			GC.Collect();
			GC.WaitForPendingFinalizers();
			break;
		}
		case UtilityFuncs.FileType.FileList:
			ProcessFileList(filename);
			GC.Collect();
			GC.WaitForPendingFinalizers();
			break;
		case UtilityFuncs.FileType.Nist:
		{
			NISTreader nISTreader = new NISTreader(myReaderLog, myDBwriter);
			try
			{
				nISTreader.OpenNistFile(filename);
				nISTreader.Process(filename);
				nISTreader.CloseNistFile();
			}
			catch (Exception ex)
			{
				nISTreader.ReportFileFailure(ex.Message);
			}
			GC.Collect();
			GC.WaitForPendingFinalizers();
			break;
		}
		case UtilityFuncs.FileType.Dts:
		{
			DtsReader dtsReader = new DtsReader(myReaderLog, myDBwriter);
			dtsReader.OpenDtsFile(filename);
			dtsReader.Process(myMergeToDbank);
			dtsReader.CloseDtsFile();
			GC.Collect();
			GC.WaitForPendingFinalizers();
			break;
		}
		case UtilityFuncs.FileType.Formula:
		{
			FormulaReader formulaReader = new FormulaReader(myReaderLog, myDBwriter);
			formulaReader.OpenFormulaFile(filename);
			formulaReader.Process();
			formulaReader.CloseFormulaFile();
			GC.Collect();
			GC.WaitForPendingFinalizers();
			break;
		}
		default:
			myReaderLog.AddMessageToLog("Invalid file " + filename);
			throw new GatewayException($"Fatal error: Invalid file - {filename}.\nData Conversion will terminate now.");
		}
		return result;
	}

	public void ProcessFileList(string filename)
	{
		string errMessage = null;
		InputFileReader inputFileReader = new InputFileReader(myReaderLog);
		inputFileReader.OpenFile(filename);
		while (inputFileReader.GetALine() != -1)
		{
			if (!inputFileReader.CurrentLineCopy.StartsWith("/*") && !inputFileReader.CurrentLineCopy.StartsWith("//") && !inputFileReader.CurrentLineCopy.StartsWith("  _#") && !inputFileReader.CurrentLineCopy.StartsWith(";"))
			{
				Tokens tokens = new Tokens(inputFileReader.CurrentLineCopy, Tokens.delimiterSlash, "\"", "\"");
				if (tokens.Length >= 2 && string.Compare(tokens.GetAt(0), "include", ignoreCase: true) == 0)
				{
					ProcessFile(tokens.GetAt(1), ref errMessage);
				}
			}
		}
		inputFileReader.CloseFile();
	}
}
public class ReaderLog
{
	public enum LogType
	{
		File,
		Console,
		Buffer,
		NoLog
	}

	private bool bDisplayConsole;

	private StreamWriter sw;

	public ReaderLog(string stringLogFileName, bool deleteOld, LogType logType)
	{
		try
		{
			FileInfo fileInfo = new FileInfo(stringLogFileName);
			sw = fileInfo.AppendText();
			if (logType == LogType.Console || logType == LogType.File)
			{
				bDisplayConsole = true;
			}
		}
		catch
		{
			throw new GatewayException("Error setting Log File. Please make sure specified directory exists and you have write access to this file.");
		}
	}

	public void SetErrorLog(LogType logType, bool ClearOldLog, string name)
	{
		if (logType == LogType.Console || logType == LogType.File)
		{
			bDisplayConsole = true;
		}
	}

	public void AddMessageToLog(string stringMessage)
	{
		try
		{
			if (bDisplayConsole && sw != null)
			{
				sw.WriteLine(stringMessage);
			}
		}
		catch
		{
		}
	}

	public void CloseLog()
	{
		try
		{
			if (sw != null)
			{
				sw.Flush();
				sw.Close();
				sw = null;
			}
		}
		catch
		{
		}
	}
}
public class Tokens : IEnumerable
{
	private class TokenEnumerator : IEnumerator
	{
		private int position = -1;

		private Tokens t;

		public object Current
		{
			get
			{
				if (t.elements == null)
				{
					return null;
				}
				if (position == -1)
				{
					return -1;
				}
				if (position < t.elements.Length)
				{
					return t.elements[position];
				}
				return null;
			}
		}

		public TokenEnumerator(Tokens t)
		{
			this.t = t;
		}

		public bool MoveNext()
		{
			if (t.elements == null)
			{
				return false;
			}
			if (position < t.elements.Length - 1)
			{
				position++;
				return true;
			}
			return false;
		}

		public void Reset()
		{
			position = -1;
		}
	}

	public static readonly char[] delimiterNormal = new char[3] { ' ', '\t', '\n' };

	public static readonly char[] delimiterSlash = new char[4] { ' ', '\t', '\n', '/' };

	public static readonly char[] delimiterDot = new char[1] { '.' };

	private string[] elements;

	public int Length
	{
		get
		{
			if (elements == null)
			{
				return 0;
			}
			return elements.Length;
		}
	}

	public Tokens(string source, char[] delimiters)
	{
		elements = GetElementsFromSource(source, delimiters);
	}

	public Tokens(string source, char[] delimiters, string OpenCharacters, string CloseCharacters)
	{
		if (OpenCharacters == null || CloseCharacters == null || OpenCharacters == "" || CloseCharacters == "")
		{
			elements = GetElementsFromSource(source, delimiters);
			return;
		}
		int num = 0;
		int num2 = 0;
		int num3 = 0;
		int length = OpenCharacters.Length;
		int length2 = CloseCharacters.Length;
		ArrayList arrayList = new ArrayList();
		ArrayList arrayList2 = new ArrayList();
		while (true)
		{
			num = ((num2 != 0) ? source.IndexOf(OpenCharacters, num2 + length2) : source.IndexOf(OpenCharacters, num2));
			if (num == -1)
			{
				break;
			}
			num2 = source.IndexOf(CloseCharacters, num + length);
			if (num2 == -1)
			{
				break;
			}
			arrayList.Add(num);
			arrayList2.Add(num2);
			num3++;
		}
		if (num3 == 0)
		{
			elements = GetElementsFromSource(source, delimiters);
			return;
		}
		ArrayList arrayList3 = new ArrayList();
		num = 0;
		num2 = 0;
		string[] elementsFromSource;
		for (int i = 0; i < num3; i++)
		{
			num = (int)arrayList[i];
			string source2 = source.Substring(num2, num - num2);
			elementsFromSource = GetElementsFromSource(source2, delimiters);
			AddStrArrayToArrayList(elementsFromSource, arrayList3);
			num2 = (int)arrayList2[i];
			int num4 = num + length;
			arrayList3.Add(source.Substring(num4, num2 - num4));
			num2 += length2;
		}
		string source3 = source.Substring(num2);
		elementsFromSource = GetElementsFromSource(source3, delimiters);
		AddStrArrayToArrayList(elementsFromSource, arrayList3);
		elements = ConvertArrayListToStringArray(arrayList3);
	}

	public Tokens(ref string source, char[] delimiters, string OpenCharacters, string CloseCharacters, bool bNewComp)
	{
		if (OpenCharacters == null || CloseCharacters == null || OpenCharacters == "" || CloseCharacters == "")
		{
			if (bNewComp)
			{
				elements = GetElementsFromSourceForCompounds(ref source, delimiters);
			}
			else
			{
				elements = GetElementsFromSource(source, delimiters);
			}
			return;
		}
		int num = 0;
		int num2 = 0;
		int num3 = 0;
		int length = OpenCharacters.Length;
		int length2 = CloseCharacters.Length;
		ArrayList arrayList = new ArrayList();
		ArrayList arrayList2 = new ArrayList();
		while (true)
		{
			num = ((num2 != 0) ? source.IndexOf(OpenCharacters, num2 + length2) : source.IndexOf(OpenCharacters, num2));
			if (num == -1)
			{
				break;
			}
			num2 = source.IndexOf(CloseCharacters, num + length);
			if (num2 == -1)
			{
				break;
			}
			arrayList.Add(num);
			arrayList2.Add(num2);
			num3++;
		}
		if (num3 == 0)
		{
			if (bNewComp)
			{
				elements = GetElementsFromSourceForCompounds(ref source, delimiters);
			}
			else
			{
				elements = GetElementsFromSource(source, delimiters);
			}
			return;
		}
		ArrayList arrayList3 = new ArrayList();
		num = 0;
		num2 = 0;
		for (int i = 0; i < num3; i++)
		{
			num = (int)arrayList[i];
			string source2 = source.Substring(num2, num - num2);
			string[] tempStrArray = ((!bNewComp) ? GetElementsFromSource(source2, delimiters) : GetElementsFromSourceForCompounds(ref source2, delimiters));
			AddStrArrayToArrayList(tempStrArray, arrayList3);
			num2 = (int)arrayList2[i];
			int num4 = num + length;
			arrayList3.Add(source.Substring(num4, num2 - num4));
			num2 += length2;
		}
		string source3 = source.Substring(num2);
		AddStrArrayToArrayList((!bNewComp) ? GetElementsFromSource(source3, delimiters) : GetElementsFromSourceForCompounds(ref source3, delimiters), arrayList3);
		elements = ConvertArrayListToStringArray(arrayList3);
	}

	private static string[] GetElementsFromSource(string source, char[] delimiters)
	{
		if (source != null)
		{
			int num = source.IndexOf(';');
			if (num != -1)
			{
				source = source.Substring(0, num);
			}
		}
		string[] array = source.Split(delimiters);
		int num2 = 0;
		for (int i = 0; i < array.Length; i++)
		{
			if (array[i] != "")
			{
				num2++;
			}
		}
		if (num2 == 0)
		{
			return null;
		}
		ArrayList arrayList = new ArrayList();
		for (int j = 0; j < array.Length; j++)
		{
			if (array[j] != "" && array[j] != "(" && array[j] != ")")
			{
				arrayList.Add(array[j]);
			}
		}
		string[] array2 = new string[arrayList.Count];
		for (int k = 0; k < arrayList.Count; k++)
		{
			array2[k] = arrayList[k].ToString();
		}
		return array2;
	}

	private static string[] GetElementsFromSourceForCompounds(ref string source, char[] delimiters)
	{
		if (source != null)
		{
			if (source.TrimStart().ToUpperInvariant().StartsWith("NEW-COMP"))
			{
				int num = source.IndexOf(';');
				if (num != -1)
				{
					source = source.Substring(0, num);
				}
			}
			else if (source.Contains("/"))
			{
				int num2 = source.IndexOf('/');
				if (num2 != -1)
				{
					source = source.Substring(0, num2 + 1);
				}
			}
			else
			{
				source = source.TrimStart().TrimEnd();
				int num3 = source.LastIndexOf(';');
				int num4 = source.IndexOf(' ');
				if (num4 == -1)
				{
					num4 = source.IndexOf('\t');
				}
				if (num4 > -1 && num3 > num4)
				{
					source = source.Substring(0, num3);
				}
			}
		}
		string[] array = source.Split(delimiters);
		int num5 = 0;
		for (int i = 0; i < array.Length; i++)
		{
			if (array[i] != "")
			{
				num5++;
			}
		}
		if (num5 == 0)
		{
			return null;
		}
		ArrayList arrayList = new ArrayList();
		for (int j = 0; j < array.Length; j++)
		{
			if (array[j] != "" && array[j] != "(" && array[j] != ")")
			{
				arrayList.Add(array[j]);
			}
		}
		string[] array2 = new string[arrayList.Count];
		for (int k = 0; k < arrayList.Count; k++)
		{
			array2[k] = arrayList[k].ToString();
		}
		return array2;
	}

	private string[] ConvertArrayListToStringArray(ArrayList tempElements)
	{
		if (tempElements == null || tempElements.Count == 0)
		{
			return null;
		}
		ArrayList arrayList = new ArrayList();
		for (int i = 0; i < tempElements.Count; i++)
		{
			if (tempElements[i].ToString().CompareTo("(") != 0 && tempElements[i].ToString().CompareTo(")") != 0)
			{
				arrayList.Add((string)tempElements[i]);
			}
		}
		string[] array = new string[arrayList.Count];
		for (int j = 0; j < arrayList.Count; j++)
		{
			array[j] = arrayList[j].ToString();
		}
		return array;
	}

	private void AddStrArrayToArrayList(string[] tempStrArray, ArrayList tempElements)
	{
		if (tempStrArray != null)
		{
			for (int i = 0; i < tempStrArray.Length; i++)
			{
				tempElements.Add(tempStrArray[i]);
			}
		}
	}

	public string GetAt(int index)
	{
		if (index < elements.Length)
		{
			return elements[index];
		}
		return null;
	}

	public IEnumerator GetEnumerator()
	{
		return new TokenEnumerator(this);
	}
}
public class UtilityFuncs
{
	public enum FileType
	{
		Dfms,
		Mmtbs,
		MmtbsPureDBank,
		MmtbsHxt,
		Mol,
		FileList,
		InvalidFile,
		Nist,
		Dts,
		Formula
	}

	public static FileType DetermineFileType(string FileName)
	{
		if (FileName.ToLowerInvariant().EndsWith(".mdb"))
		{
			return FileType.Mol;
		}
		if (FileName.ToLowerInvariant().EndsWith(".xml"))
		{
			return FileType.Nist;
		}
		if (FileName.ToLowerInvariant().EndsWith(".aprdts"))
		{
			return FileType.Dts;
		}
		if (FileName.Contains("pnatom1-formula2.inp") || FileName.Contains("hysys1-syn2.inp"))
		{
			return FileType.Formula;
		}
		InputFileReader inputFileReader;
		try
		{
			inputFileReader = new InputFileReader(null);
			inputFileReader.OpenFile(FileName);
		}
		catch
		{
			return FileType.InvalidFile;
		}
		for (int aLine = inputFileReader.GetALine(); aLine != -1; aLine = inputFileReader.GetALine())
		{
			string text = inputFileReader.CurrentLineCopy.ToUpperInvariant();
			Tokens tokens = new Tokens(text, Tokens.delimiterSlash);
			if (tokens.Length > 0)
			{
				string at = tokens.GetAt(0);
				if (FindStringInList(at, DFMSconsts.pPKWDS, ignoreCase: true, MatchOnlyBeginningOfString: false) != -1 && text.StartsWith(at))
				{
					inputFileReader.CloseFile();
					return FileType.Dfms;
				}
				if (FindStringInList(at, MMTBSconsts.mmtbsKeywords, ignoreCase: true, MatchOnlyBeginningOfString: false) != -1 && text.StartsWith(at))
				{
					switch (at)
					{
					case "DBANK":
						if (tokens.Length > 2 && tokens.GetAt(2) != "GAMKIJ")
						{
							inputFileReader.CloseFile();
							return FileType.MmtbsPureDBank;
						}
						break;
					case ">P_REPLACE":
						inputFileReader.CloseFile();
						return FileType.MmtbsHxt;
					case "ADD":
						if (tokens.Length < 2 || tokens.GetAt(1) != "UNITS")
						{
							inputFileReader.CloseFile();
							return FileType.InvalidFile;
						}
						break;
					}
					inputFileReader.CloseFile();
					return FileType.Mmtbs;
				}
				if (at == "INCLUDE")
				{
					inputFileReader.CloseFile();
					return FileType.FileList;
				}
			}
		}
		inputFileReader.CloseFile();
		return FileType.InvalidFile;
	}

	public static string StripSingleQuotes(string inString)
	{
		inString = inString.Trim();
		if (inString.IndexOf('\'') == 0)
		{
			inString = inString.Substring(1, inString.Length - 2);
		}
		return inString;
	}

	public static double ConvertToDouble(string Value)
	{
		return Convert.ToDouble(FixExponentsForDouble(Value), CultureInfo.InvariantCulture);
	}

	public static string FixExponentsForDouble(string Value)
	{
		return Value.Replace('D', 'e').Replace('d', 'e');
	}

	public static int FindStringInList(string ToFind, string[] list, bool ignoreCase, bool MatchOnlyBeginningOfString)
	{
		if (list == null)
		{
			return -1;
		}
		for (int i = 0; list[i] != null; i++)
		{
			if (MatchOnlyBeginningOfString)
			{
				int length = list[i].Length;
				if (string.Compare(ToFind, 0, list[i], 0, length, ignoreCase) == 0)
				{
					return i;
				}
			}
			else if (string.Compare(ToFind, list[i], ignoreCase) == 0)
			{
				return i;
			}
		}
		return -1;
	}
}

public class xThCompound : IDisposable
{
	private List<xCompound> compound = new List<xCompound>();

	public void Dispose()
	{
		compound.Clear();
	}

	public int GetCompound(ref xCompound c, int i)
	{
		if (i < compound.Count)
		{
			c = compound[i];
		}
		return 0;
	}

	public int SetCompound(xCompound c)
	{
		if (!compound.Contains(c))
		{
			compound.Add(c);
		}
		return 0;
	}

	public int GetCompoundNum(ref int len)
	{
		len = compound.Count;
		return 0;
	}

		"PEA", "PET", "PNA", "THP", "COCL2", "diamine", "dinitrotoluene", "tin oxide", "1-tert-butyl-4-ethylbenzene", "tungsten fluoride",
		"lithium acetate", "dioctyl phthalate", "cyclohexylmethylamine", "1-methylcyclohexylamine", "dioctyl adipate"
	};

	public static string[] TDEBINARYEQNAME = new string[4] { "TDE.NRTL.ActivityCoefficients", "TDE.UNIQUAC.ActivityCoefficients", "TDE.Wilson.ActivityCoefficients", "TDE.Peng-Robinson2.EOS" };

	public static string[] TDEBINARYEOSTYPE = new string[3] { "GAS=Redlich-Kwong", "GAS=Hayden-O'Connell", "GAS=Ideal" };

	public static string[] TDETSeries1 = new string[10] { "A", "B", "C", "D", "E", "k", "c1", "T", "x1", "P" };

		m_abstract = null;

		return 0;

	public xThNewCompound()
	{
	}

	public xThNewCompound(XmlNode xmlNewCompoundNode, string currentFile, IDatabank databank, ICitationManager citationManager, DBwriter dbWriter, ReaderLog readerLog)
	{
		m_NodeNewCompound = xmlNewCompoundNode;
		m_CurrentFile = currentFile;
		m_Databank = databank;
		m_CitationManager = citationManager;
		m_DBwriter = dbWriter;
		m_ReaderLog = readerLog;
		xCompound xCompound2 = new xCompound();
		m_xCompound = xCompound2;
		m_ParameterList = new List<xThData>();
		m_CitationList = new List<xPropCitation>();
		m_UncertaintyList = new List<xPropUncertainty>();
		m_NoteList = new List<xPropNote>();
	}

	private void SetDatabankAndCitationManager()
	{
		//IL_0027: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
		//IL_002d: Expected O, but got Unknown
		IDatabankCollection databankList = m_DBwriter.GetDatabankList();
		if (databankList != null && ((ICollection)databankList).Count > 0)
		{
			foreach (IDatabank item in (IEnumerable)databankList)
			{
				IDatabank val = item;
				if (val.Name.CompareTo("NIST-TRC") == 0)
				{
					m_Databank = val;
					break;
				}
			}
		}
		if (m_Databank != null)
		{
			IDatabase database = m_Databank.Database;
			if (database != null)
			{
				ISupportsCitations val2 = (ISupportsCitations)(object)((database is ISupportsCitations) ? database : null);
				m_CitationManager = val2.CitationManager;
			}
		}
	}

	public void ParseCompound()
	{
		string text = string.Empty;
		string text2 = string.Empty;
		string text3 = string.Empty;
		string text4 = string.Empty;
		string text5 = string.Empty;
		string text6 = string.Empty;
		string text7 = string.Empty;
		string text8 = string.Empty;
		double[] propValues = new double[10] { 1E+35, 1E+35, 1E+35, 1E+35, 1E+35, 1E+35, 1E+35, 1E+35, 1E+35, 1E+35 };
		double[] propValues2 = new double[10] { 1E+35, 1E+35, 1E+35, 1E+35, 1E+35, 1E+35, 1E+35, 1E+35, 1E+35, 1E+35 };
		List<string> list = new List<string>();
		string[] array = null;
		try
		{
			XmlNode xmlNode = m_NodeNewCompound.SelectSingleNode(xThConstants.nodeAspenCompound);
			XmlNodeList childNodes = xmlNode.ChildNodes;
			foreach (XmlNode item in childNodes)
			{
				string name = item.Name;
				string innerText = item.InnerText;
				switch (name)
				{
				case "AspenCASRN":
					text = innerText;
					m_CASRN = innerText;
					break;
				case "AspenName":
					text2 = innerText;
					m_CompoundName = innerText;
					break;
				case "Alias":
					text3 = innerText;
					m_Alias = innerText;
					break;
				case "AspenMW":
					text4 = innerText;
					break;
				case "AspenFormula":
					text5 = innerText;
					break;
				case "ATOMNO":
					propValues = GetElements(innerText);
					break;
				case "NOATOM":
					propValues2 = GetElements(innerText);
					break;
				}
			}
			xmlNode = m_NodeNewCompound.SelectSingleNode("Compound");
			childNodes = xmlNode.ChildNodes;
			foreach (XmlNode item2 in childNodes)
			{
				string name = item2.Name;
				string innerText = item2.InnerText;
				switch (name)
				{
				case "sFormulaMolec":
					text7 = innerText;
					break;
				case "nMolarMass":
					text6 = innerText;
					break;
				case "DipprFamily":
					if (xThConstants.DIPPRFAMILYAB.Contains(innerText))
					{
						int num = xThConstants.DIPPRFAMILYAB.IndexOf(innerText);
						if (num < xThConstants.DIPPRFAMILY.Length)
						{
							text8 = xThConstants.DIPPRFAMILY[num];
						}
					}
					break;
				}
			}
			if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(text7) && text7.Length > 12 && text5.CompareTo(text7) != 0)
			{
				list.Add(text7.ToUpperInvariant());
			}
			m_xCompound.SetCommonName(text2);
			m_xCompound.SetFormula(text3);
			m_DBwriter.AddPureCompound(text2, text3);
			m_xCompound.SetRegNum(text);
			string[] identifierList = new string[1] { text };
			AddIdentifierAndValue("CASRN", "CAS Number", allowMultiples: false, text3, "ALIAS", identifierList);
			if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(text8))
			{
				m_xCompound.SetDipprFamily(text8);
				string[] valueList = new string[1] { text8 };
				m_DBwriter.AddEnumerationValues(text3, "ALIAS", "COMPCLASS", valueList);
			}
			string text9 = string.Empty;
			if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(text7))
			{
				text9 = text7;
			}
			else if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(text5))
			{
				text9 = text5;
			}
			if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(text9))
			{
				m_xCompound.SetChemFormula(text9);
				string[] valArr = new string[1] { text9 };
				IPropertyValue propertyValue = null;
				m_DBwriter.AddPropertyValue(null, text, "CASRN", "FORMULA", valArr, "", "", "", null, "", out propertyValue);
			}
			string value = string.Empty;
			if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(text6))
			{
				value = text6;
			}
			else if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(text4))
			{
				value = text4;
			}
			if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(value))
			{
				m_MolarMass = Convert.ToDouble(value);
				m_xCompound.SetMolarMass(m_MolarMass);
				double[] propValues3 = new double[1] { Convert.ToDouble(value) };
				AddPropertyAndValue(text, "CASRN", "MW", 1, propValues3);
			}
			AddPropertyAndValue(text, "CASRN", "ATOMNO", 10, propValues);
			AddPropertyAndValue(text, "CASRN", "NOATOM", 10, propValues2);
		}
		catch (Exception ex)
		{
			if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(m_Errors))
			{
				m_Errors = ex.Message;
			}
			else
			{
				m_Errors = m_Errors + "; " + ex.Message;
			}
		}
		finally
		{
			if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(m_Errors))
			{
				if (!m_Errors.Contains(m_CurrentFile))
				{
					m_Errors = m_CurrentFile + " for " + text2 + "/" + text3 + "/" + text + ": " + m_Errors;
				}
				m_ReaderLog.AddMessageToLog(m_Errors);
			}
		}
	}

	public void ParsePureParameters()
	{
		XmlNodeList xmlNodeList = m_NodeNewCompound.SelectNodes("PureOrMixtureData");
		if (xmlNodeList.Count > 0)
		{
			foreach (XmlNode item in xmlNodeList)
			{
				m_Errors = string.Empty;
				xThParameterData xThParameterData2 = new xThParameterData(m_CurrentFile, m_xCompound, item);
				try
				{
					xThData thisParameterData = null;
					m_Errors = xThParameterData2.ParseParameterDataNode(ref thisParameterData);
					if (!m_ParameterList.Contains(thisParameterData))
					{
						m_ParameterList.Add(thisParameterData);
					}
				}
				catch (Exception ex)
				{
					m_Errors = ex.Message;
				}
				finally
				{
					if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(m_Errors))
					{
						string propName = xThParameterData2.GetPropName();
						if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(propName))
						{
							m_Errors = m_CurrentFile + " for " + m_CompoundName + "/" + m_Alias + "/" + m_CASRN + ": " + m_Errors;
						}
						else
						{
							m_Errors = m_CurrentFile + " for " + m_CompoundName + "/" + m_Alias + "/" + m_CASRN + ": " + propName + " - " + m_Errors;
						}
						m_ReaderLog.AddMessageToLog(m_Errors);
					}
				}
			}
		}
		XmlNodeList xmlNodeList2 = m_NodeNewCompound.SelectNodes("ReactionData");
		if (xmlNodeList2.Count > 0)
		{
			foreach (XmlNode item2 in xmlNodeList2)
			{
				m_Errors = string.Empty;
				try
				{
					xThParameterData xThParameterData3 = new xThParameterData(m_CurrentFile, m_xCompound, item2);
					xThData thisParameterData2 = null;
					m_Errors = xThParameterData3.ParseParameterDataNode(ref thisParameterData2);
					if (!m_ParameterList.Contains(thisParameterData2))
					{
						m_ParameterList.Add(thisParameterData2);
					}
				}
				catch (Exception ex2)
				{
					m_Errors = ex2.Message;
				}
				finally
				{
					if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(m_Errors))
					{
						m_Errors = m_CurrentFile + " for " + m_CompoundName + "/" + m_Alias + "/" + m_CASRN + ": " + m_Errors;
						m_ReaderLog.AddMessageToLog(m_Errors);
					}
				}
			}
		}
		if (m_ParameterList.Count > 0)
		{
			foreach (xThData parameter in m_ParameterList)
			{
				m_Errors = string.Empty;
				xProperty p = null;
				string n = string.Empty;
				parameter.GetPropertyInfo(ref p, 0);
				p.GetName(ref n);
				double v = 1E+35;
				p.GetValue(ref v, 0);
				try
				{
					PrintParameterList(parameter);
				}
				catch (Exception ex3)
				{
					m_Errors = "For " + n + " with the value " + v + " - " + ex3.Message;
				}
				finally
				{
					if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(m_Errors))
					{
						m_Errors = m_CurrentFile + " for " + m_CompoundName + "/" + m_Alias + "/" + m_CASRN + ": " + m_Errors;
						m_ReaderLog.AddMessageToLog(m_Errors);
					}
				}
			}
		}
		if (m_bthrswt)
		{
			m_Errors = string.Empty;
			try
			{
				AddPropertyAndValue(m_CASRN, m_idType, "THRSWT", 8, m_thrswt);
			}
			catch (Exception ex4)
			{
				m_Errors = "For THRSWT - " + ex4.Message;
			}
			finally
			{
				if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(m_Errors))
				{
					m_Errors = m_CurrentFile + " for " + m_CompoundName + "/" + m_Alias + "/" + m_CASRN + ": " + m_Errors;
					m_ReaderLog.AddMessageToLog(m_Errors);
				}
			}
		}
		if (m_btrnswt)
		{
			m_Errors = string.Empty;
			try
			{
				AddPropertyAndValue(m_CASRN, m_idType, "TRNSWT", 5, m_trnswt);
			}
			catch (Exception ex5)
			{
				m_Errors = "For TRNSWT - " + ex5.Message;
			}
			finally
			{
				if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(m_Errors))
				{
					m_Errors = m_CurrentFile + " for " + m_CompoundName + "/" + m_Alias + "/" + m_CASRN + ": " + m_Errors;
					m_ReaderLog.AddMessageToLog(m_Errors);
				}
			}
		}
		if (m_UncertaintyList.Count > 0)
		{
			foreach (xPropUncertainty uncertainty in m_UncertaintyList)
			{
				m_Errors = string.Empty;
				string propName2 = uncertainty.PropName;
				try
				{
					m_CitationManager.AddDataInfo(m_Databank, m_CASRN, m_idType, uncertainty.PropName, uncertainty.PropUncertainty);
				}
				catch (Exception ex6)
				{
					m_Errors = "For " + propName2 + " - " + ex6.Message;
				}
				finally
				{
					if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(m_Errors))
					{
						m_Errors = m_CurrentFile + " for " + m_CompoundName + "/" + m_Alias + "/" + m_CASRN + ": " + m_Errors;
						m_ReaderLog.AddMessageToLog(m_Errors);
					}
				}
			}
		}
		if (m_CitationList.Count > 0)
		{
			foreach (xPropCitation citation in m_CitationList)
			{
				m_Errors = string.Empty;
				string propName3 = citation.PropName;
				try
				{
					m_CitationManager.AddCitation(m_Databank, m_CASRN, m_idType, citation.PropName, citation.PropRef);
				}
				catch (Exception ex7)
				{
					m_Errors = "For " + propName3 + " - " + ex7.Message;
				}
				finally
				{
					if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(m_Errors))
					{
						m_Errors = m_CurrentFile + " for " + m_CompoundName + "/" + m_Alias + "/" + m_CASRN + ": " + m_Errors;
						m_ReaderLog.AddMessageToLog(m_Errors);
					}
				}
			}
		}
		if (m_NoteList.Count <= 0)
		{
			return;
		}
		foreach (xPropNote note in m_NoteList)
		{
			m_Errors = string.Empty;
			string propName4 = note.PropName;
			try
			{
				m_CitationManager.AddNote(m_Databank, m_CASRN, m_idType, note.PropName, note.PropNote);
			}
			catch (Exception ex8)
			{
				m_Errors = "For " + propName4 + " - " + ex8.Message;
			}
			finally
			{
				if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(m_Errors))
				{
					m_Errors = m_CurrentFile + " for " + m_CompoundName + "/" + m_Alias + "/" + m_CASRN + ": " + m_Errors;
					m_ReaderLog.AddMessageToLog(m_Errors);
				}
			}
		}
	}

	private double[] GetElements(string elementString)
	{
		string[] array = elementString.Split(' ');
		double[] array2 = new double[10];
		for (int i = 0; i < 10; i++)
		{
			if (array[i].CompareTo("0") == 0)
			{
				array2[i] = 1E+35;
			}
			else
			{
				array2[i] = Convert.ToDouble(array[i]);
			}
		}
		return array2;
	}

	private void AddIdentifierAndValue(string identifierName, string identifierDescription, bool allowMultiples, string CompID, string idType, string[] identifierList)
	{
		try
		{
			m_DBwriter.AddIdentifier(identifierName, identifierDescription, allowMultiples);
			m_DBwriter.AddIdentifierValues(CompID, idType, identifierName, identifierList);
		}
		catch (Exception ex)
		{
			if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(m_Errors))
			{
				m_Errors = ex.Message;
			}
			else
			{
				m_Errors = m_Errors + "; " + ex.Message;
			}
		}
	}

	private void AddPropertyAndValue(string CompID, string idType, string propName, int propNumElems, double[] propValues)
	{
		try
		{
			m_DBwriter.AddProperty(propName, "Aspen Plus parameter " + propName, propIsGlobal: false, (PropertyDataType)1, (InteractionType)0, (CompoundType)0, propNumElems, null, null, null);
			m_DBwriter.AddToTheCurrentPropList(propName);
			if (!m_DBwriter.CheckCompIdentifierExists(CompID, idType))
			{
				return;
			}
			if (propNumElems == 1)
			{
				int highestNonNullElementInVector = 0;
				m_DBwriter.AddParamElemValue(CompID, idType, propName, propNumElems, propValues[0], null, highestNonNullElementInVector);
				return;
			}
			double dblValue = 0.0;
			ArrayList arrayList = new ArrayList();
			for (int i = 0; i < propNumElems; i++)
			{
				if (propValues[i] != 1E+35)
				{
					arrayList.Add(propValues[i]);
				}
				else
				{
					arrayList.Add(null);
				}
			}
			m_DBwriter.AddParamElemValue(CompID, idType, propName, propNumElems, dblValue, arrayList, propNumElems);
		}
		catch (Exception ex)
		{
			if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(m_Errors))
			{
				m_Errors = ex.Message;
			}
			else
			{
				m_Errors = m_Errors + "; " + ex.Message;
			}
		}
	}

				{
					flag32 = true;
				}
				if (n3.CompareTo(xThConstants.PHASENAME[30]) == 0)
				{
					flag31 = true;
				}
				if (!flag27 && !flag28 && flag30 && flag29)
				{
					flag33 = true;
				}
				else if (flag27 && flag28 && flag30 && !flag29)
				{
					flag37 = true;
				}
				else if (flag27 && !flag28 && flag30 && !flag29)
				{
					flag34 = true;
				}
				else if (flag27 && !flag28 && flag30 && flag29)
				{
					flag35 = true;
				}
				else if (flag27 && !flag28 && !flag30 && flag29)
				{
					flag36 = true;
				}
				else if (!flag27 && !flag28 && flag31 && flag29)
				{
					flag38 = true;
				}
			}
		}

	private double DoubletoSci(double d, int digits)
	{
		if (d == 0.0)
		{
			return 0.0;
		}
		decimal num = (decimal)Math.Pow(10.0, Math.Floor(Math.Log10(Math.Abs(d))) + 1.0);
		return (double)(num * Math.Round((decimal)d / num, digits));
	}

	private double DoubletoDci(double d, int digits)
	{
		if (d == 0.0)
		{
			return 0.0;
		}
		return Convert.ToDouble(decimal.Round(Convert.ToDecimal(d), digits));
	}

	private bool IsPropertyExisted(string propName)
	{
		foreach (string prop in m_PropList)
		{
			if (propName.CompareTo(prop) == 0)
			{
				return true;
			}
		}
		m_PropList.Add(propName);
		return false;
	}

	private bool IsPropertyExisted1(string propName)
	{
		foreach (string prop in m_PropList)
		{
			if (propName.CompareTo(prop) == 0)
			{
				return true;
			}
		}
		return false;
	}

	private void ParseConstantParameters(string apName, double paramVal, double paramUnval)
	{
		if (!m_PropList.Contains(apName))
		{
			if (paramVal != 1E+35)
			{
				double[] propValues = new double[1] { paramVal };
				AddPropertyAndValue(m_CASRN, "CASRN", apName, 1, propValues);
			}
			if (paramUnval == 1E+35)
			{
				paramUnval = 0.0;
			}
			PrintUncertainty(apName, paramUnval);
			if (curSizePropRefs > 0)
			{
				PrintRefs(apName, curSizePropRefs);
			}
		}
	}

	private void ParseConstantParameters1(string apName, double paramVal, double paramUnval)
	{
		if (!m_PropList.Contains(apName))
		{
			if (paramVal != 1E+35)
			{
				double[] propValues = new double[1] { paramVal };
				AddPropertyAndValue(m_CASRN, "CASRN", apName, 1, propValues);
			}
			if (paramUnval == 1E+35)
			{
				paramUnval = 0.0;
			}
			PrintUncertainty(apName, paramUnval);
		}
	}

	private void ParseTDepParameters(string apName, string eqName, double[] paramVal, int nParam, int nParamMax)
	{
		AddPropertyAndValue(m_CASRN, "CASRN", apName, nParamMax + 2, paramVal);
		double paramUnval = 0.0;
		PrintUncertainty(apName, paramUnval);
		if (curSizePropRefs > 0)
		{
			PrintRefs(apName, curSizePropRefs);
		}
	}

	private void PrintUncertainty(string propName, double paramUnval)
	{
		//IL_0022: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
		//IL_0028: Expected O, but got Unknown
		string text = "+/- " + paramUnval;
		PropertyDataInfoObject propUncertainty = new PropertyDataInfoObject("NIST", text, "", "");
		xPropUncertainty item = new xPropUncertainty
		{
			PropName = propName,
			PropUncertainty = propUncertainty
		};
		if (!m_UncertaintyList.Contains(item))
		{
			m_UncertaintyList.Add(item);
		}
	}

	private void PushPropRef(string propName, string Title, string Pages, string Journal, string Date, string Book, string Volume, string Publisher, string Notes, string Authors, string IdentityToUse)
	{
		//IL_0058: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
		//IL_005e: Expected O, but got Unknown
		//IL_001b: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
		//IL_0021: Expected O, but got Unknown
		if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(Notes))
		{
			PropertyCitationsObject propRef = new PropertyCitationsObject(IdentityToUse, Title, Pages, Journal, Date, Book, Volume, Publisher, Notes, Authors);
			xPropCitation item = new xPropCitation
			{
				PropName = propName,
				PropRef = propRef
			};
			if (!m_CitationList.Contains(item))
			{
				m_CitationList.Add(item);
			}
		}
		else
		{
			NotesObject propNote = new NotesObject(IdentityToUse, Notes);
			xPropNote item2 = new xPropNote
			{
				PropName = propName,

			if (!m_NoteList.Contains(item2))
