	}

	private void PrintRefs(string propName, int numPropRefs)
	{
		int num = -1;
		int num2 = -1;
		for (int i = 0; i < numPropRefs; i++)
		{
			int len = 0;
			string n = string.Empty;
			string n2 = string.Empty;
			string n3 = string.Empty;
			string n4 = string.Empty;
			string empty = string.Empty;
			string v = string.Empty;
			bool flag = false;
			bool flag2 = false;
			curProp.GetPropRef(ref curPropRef, i);
			curPropRef.GetRefID(ref n);
			curPropRef.GetNumAuthor(ref len);
			string text = string.Empty;
			if (len != 0)
			{
				for (int j = 0; j < len; j++)
				{
					curPropRef.GetAuthor(ref v, j);
					text = text + v + ";";
				}
			}
			curPropRef.GetPubName(ref n2);
			curPropRef.GetPubYear(ref n3);
			curPropRef.GetTitle(ref n4);
			flag = !string.IsNullOrEmpty(n2) && len <= 0 && string.IsNullOrEmpty(n4) && string.IsNullOrEmpty(n3);
			flag2 = string.IsNullOrEmpty(n2) && len <= 0 && string.IsNullOrEmpty(n4) && string.IsNullOrEmpty(n3);
			if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(n))
			{
				n = propName + i;
			}
			if (flag)
			{
				num2++;
				if (num2 < 1)
				{
					PushPropRef(propName, "", "", "", "", "", "", "", n2, "", n);
				}
				continue;
			}
			num++;
			if (num < 2 && !flag2)
			{
				if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(n4))
				{
					n4 = "No title";
					PushPropRef(propName, n4, "", "", n3, n2, "", "", "", text, n);
				}
				else
				{
					PushPropRef(propName, n4, "", n2, n3, "", "", "", "", text, n);
				}
			}
		}
	}

	private void GetOmega(ref double omega, ref int nDigits)
	{
		int len = 0;
		int len2 = 0;

			{
				curEqn.GetEqParamNum(ref len2);
				if (len2 > 0)
				{
					curEqn.GetEqParam(ref curEqParam, 0);
					curEqParam.GetValue(ref omega);

					break;
				}
			}
		}
	}

	private bool GetParamX(string propName, string eqName, string[] eqSymbol, string[] eqParamNum, int nIndex, string[] conSymbol, int nConstant, string propNum, string aplusName, int nParamMax, double[] paramVal, int nParam)
	{
		int len = 0;
		int len2 = 0;
		int len3 = 0;
		int len4 = 0;
		int num = 0;
		int num2 = 0;
		int num3 = 0;
		int num4 = 0;
		int num5 = 0;
		string n = string.Empty;
		double v = 1E+35;
		double v2 = 1E+35;
		bool result = false;
		double value = 1E+35;
		double value2 = 1E+35;
		int num6 = 0;
		string n2 = string.Empty;
		string number = string.Empty;
		curResult.GetEqnNum(ref len2);
		for (num = 0; num < len2; num++)
		{
			curResult.GetEqn(ref curEqn, num);
			curEqn.GeteEqName(ref n);
			if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(n))
			{
				curEqn.GetsEqName(ref n);
			}
			if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(n) || n.CompareTo(eqName) != 0)
			{
				continue;
			}
			curEqn.GetEqPropNum(ref len3);
			if (len3 <= 0)
			{
				continue;
			}
			for (num3 = 0; num3 < len3; num3++)
			{
				curEqn.GetEqProp(ref curEqProp, num3);
				curEqProp.GetEqNum(ref n);
				if (n.CompareTo(propNum) != 0)
				{
					continue;
				}
				curEqn.GetEqVarNum(ref len4);
				if (len4 > 0)
				{
					num4 = 0;
					curEqn.GetEqVar(ref curEqVar, num4);
					curEqVar.GetEqRangeMin(ref value);
					curEqVar.GetEqRangeMax(ref value2);
					if (value != 0.0 && value2 != 0.0 && value != 1E+35 && value2 != 1E+35)
					{
						paramVal[nParamMax] = value;
						paramVal[nParamMax + 1] = value2;
					}
				}
				curEqn.GetEqContNum(ref len);
				num5 = 0;
				if (len > 0 && nConstant > 0)
				{
					for (int i = 0; i < nConstant; i++)
					{
						for (num2 = 0; num2 < len; num2++)
						{
							curEqn.GetEqCont(ref curEqCont, num2);
							curEqCont.GetEqSymbol(ref n);
							if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(n) && n.CompareTo(conSymbol[i]) == 0)
							{
								curEqCont.GetValue(ref v2);
								paramVal[nParamMax - nConstant + i] = v2;
								num6++;
							}
						}
					}
				}
				curEqn.GetEqParamNum(ref nParam);
				if (nParam <= 0)
				{
					continue;
				}
				for (int j = 0; j < nIndex; j++)
				{
					if (eqParamNum[j].CompareTo(xThConstants.strNA) == 0)
					{
						for (num4 = 0; num4 < nParam; num4++)
						{
							curEqn.GetEqParam(ref curEqParam, num4);
							curEqParam.GetEqSymbol(ref n2);
							if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(n2) && n2.CompareTo(eqSymbol[j]) == 0)
							{
								curEqParam.GetValue(ref v);
								paramVal[j] = v;
								break;
							}
						}
						continue;
					}
					for (num4 = 0; num4 < nParam; num4++)
					{
						curEqn.GetEqParam(ref curEqParam, num4);
						curEqParam.GetEqNum(ref number);
						curEqParam.GetEqSymbol(ref n2);
						if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(number) && !string.IsNullOrEmpty(n2) && number.CompareTo(eqParamNum[j]) == 0 && n2.CompareTo(eqSymbol[j]) == 0)
						{
							curEqParam.GetValue(ref v);
							paramVal[j] = v;
							break;
						}
					}
				}
				int num7 = nParam + num6;
				nParam = num7;
				result = true;
				break;
			}
		}
		return result;
	}
}

			}

			{
				SetValues(childNode);
			}
			if (name.CompareTo("Equation") == 0)
			{
				SetEquation(childNode);
			}
		}
		thisParameterData = curResult;
		return m_Errors;
	}

	private void SetPropInfo(XmlNode node)
	{
		string text = string.Empty;
		string text2 = string.Empty;
		string propPhase = string.Empty;
		foreach (XmlNode childNode in node.ChildNodes)
		{
			string name = childNode.Name;
			if (name.CompareTo("nPropNumber") == 0)
			{
				text = childNode.InnerText;
			}
			if (name.CompareTo("Property-MethodID") == 0)
			{
				XmlNode xmlNode2 = childNode.SelectSingleNode("PropertyGroup");
				string text3 = string.Empty;
				if (xmlNode2 != null)
				{
					text3 = xmlNode2.FirstChild.Name;
				}
				string xpath = "PropertyGroup/" + text3 + "/ePropName";
				XmlNode xmlNode3 = childNode.SelectSingleNode(xpath);
				if (xmlNode3 != null)
				{
					text2 = (curPropName = xmlNode3.InnerText);
					xProperty xProperty2 = new xProperty();
					xProperty2.SetName(text2);
					xProperty2.SetNum(text);
					curResult.SetPropertyInfo(xProperty2);
					curProp = xProperty2;
				}
				xpath = "PropertyGroup/" + text3 + "/CriticalEvaluation/MultiProp/EvalMultiPropRef";
				XmlNodeList xmlNodeList = childNode.SelectNodes(xpath);
				if (xmlNodeList != null && xmlNodeList.Count > 0)
				{
					for (int i = 0; i < xmlNodeList.Count; i++)
					{
						string empty = string.Empty;
						string text4 = string.Empty;
						string text5 = string.Empty;
						string text6 = string.Empty;
						string text7 = string.Empty;
						string empty2 = string.Empty;
						XmlNode xmlNode4 = xmlNodeList[i];
						xPropRef xPropRef2 = new xPropRef();
						curProp.SetPropRef(xPropRef2);
						curPropRef = xPropRef2;
						XmlNodeList childNodes = xmlNode4.ChildNodes;
						if (childNodes == null || childNodes.Count <= 0)
						{
							continue;
						}
						foreach (XmlNode item in childNodes)
						{
							name = item.Name;
							string empty3 = string.Empty;
							switch (name)
							{
							case "TRCRefID":
								empty = GetTRCRefID(item);
								if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(empty))
								{
									xPropRef2.SetRefID(empty);
								}
								break;
							case "sAuthor":
								text4 = item.InnerText;
								if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(text4))
								{
									xPropRef2.SetAuthor(text4);
								}
								break;
							case "sPubName":
								text5 = item.InnerText;
								if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(text5))
								{
									xPropRef2.SetPubName(text5);
								}
								break;
							case "yrPubYr":
								text6 = item.InnerText;
								if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(text6))
								{
									xPropRef2.SetPubYear(text6);
								}
								break;
							case "sTitle":
								text7 = item.InnerText;
								if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(text7))
								{
									xPropRef2.SetTitle(text7);
								}
								break;
							}
						}
						if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(text7) && !string.IsNullOrEmpty(text4) && !string.IsNullOrEmpty(text5))
						{
							string.IsNullOrEmpty(text6);
						}
					}
				}
			}
			if (name.CompareTo("PropPhaseID") != 0)
			{
				continue;
			}
			foreach (XmlNode childNode2 in childNode.ChildNodes)
			{
				string name2 = childNode2.Name;
				if (name2.CompareTo("ePropPhase") == 0)
				{
					propPhase = childNode2.InnerText;
				}
			}
		}
		if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(text) && !string.IsNullOrEmpty(text2))
		{
			AddProp(text2, text, propPhase);
		}
	}

	private string GetTRCRefID(XmlNode node)
	{
		string text = string.Empty;
		string empty = string.Empty;
		string empty2 = string.Empty;
		foreach (XmlNode childNode in node.ChildNodes)
		{
			empty = childNode.Name;
			empty2 = childNode.InnerText;
			switch (empty)
			{
			case "yrYrPub":
				if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(empty2))
				{
					text += empty2;
				}
				break;
			case "sAuthor1":
				if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(empty2))
				{
					text += empty2;
				}
				break;
			case "sAuthor2":
				if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(empty2))
				{
					text += empty2;
				}
				break;
			case "nAuthorn":
				if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(empty2))
				{
					text += empty2;
				}
				break;
			}
		}
		return text;
	}

	private void SetVariable(XmlNode node)
	{
		string varNum = string.Empty;
		string varName = string.Empty;
		string varPhase = string.Empty;
		XmlNode xmlNode = node.SelectSingleNode("nVarNumber");
		if (xmlNode != null)
		{
			varNum = xmlNode.InnerText;
		}
		XmlNode xmlNode2 = node.SelectSingleNode("VariableID/VariableType");
		if (xmlNode2 != null)
		{

			if (firstChild != null)

				varName = firstChild.InnerText;

		if (xmlNode3 != null)
		{
			varPhase = xmlNode3.InnerText;
		}
		AddVar(varName, varNum, varPhase);
	}

	private void SetValues(XmlNode node)
	{
		string text = string.Empty;
		string value = string.Empty;
		double value2 = 1E+35;
		int propDigits = -1;
		int uncertNum = -1;
		double dblUncertVal = 1E+35;
		XmlNode xmlNode = node.SelectSingleNode("PropertyValue/nPropNumber");

			text = xmlNode.InnerText;
		}
		XmlNode xmlNode2 = node.SelectSingleNode("PropertyValue/nPropValue");
		if (xmlNode2 != null)
		{
			value = xmlNode2.InnerText;
			value2 = Convert.ToDouble(xmlNode2.InnerText);
		}
		XmlNode xmlNode3 = node.SelectSingleNode("PropertyValue/nPropDigits");
		if (xmlNode3 != null)
		{
			propDigits = Convert.ToInt32(xmlNode3.InnerText);
		}
		XmlNode xmlNode4 = node.SelectSingleNode("PropertyValue/CombinedUncertainty/nCombUncertAssessNum");
		if (xmlNode4 != null)

		if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(text) && !string.IsNullOrEmpty(value))
		{
			AddPropValue(text, value2, propDigits, uncertNum, dblUncertVal);
		}
	}

	private void SetEquation(XmlNode node)
	{
		string empty = string.Empty;
		string empty2 = string.Empty;
		string empty3 = string.Empty;
		string propNum = string.Empty;
		string eqSym = string.Empty;
		string empty4 = string.Empty;
		string empty5 = string.Empty;
		string empty6 = string.Empty;
		string empty7 = string.Empty;
		string empty8 = string.Empty;
		string empty9 = string.Empty;
		double eqPropRangeMin = -1E+35;
		double eqPropRangeMax = -1E+35;
		string varNum = string.Empty;
		string eqVarSym = string.Empty;
		string empty10 = string.Empty;
		string empty11 = string.Empty;
		string empty12 = string.Empty;
		string empty13 = string.Empty;
		string empty14 = string.Empty;
		string empty15 = string.Empty;
		double eqVarRangeMin = -1E+35;
		double eqVarRangeMax = -1E+35;
		string eqParNum = string.Empty;
		string eqParSym = string.Empty;
		string eqParInd = string.Empty;
		string empty16 = string.Empty;
		double eqParVal = 1E+35;
		string empty17 = string.Empty;
		int eqParDig = -1;
		string eqContSym = string.Empty;
		string eqContInd = string.Empty;
		string empty18 = string.Empty;
		double eqContVal = 1E+35;
		string empty19 = string.Empty;
		int eqContDig = -1;
		string eqParNum2 = string.Empty;
		string eqParNum3 = string.Empty;
		string empty20 = string.Empty;
		double covVal = 1E+35;
		xThEquation eqn = new xThEquation();
		curResult.SetEqn(eqn);
		curEqn = eqn;
		foreach (XmlNode childNode in node.ChildNodes)
		{
			string name = childNode.Name;
			if (name.CompareTo("sEqName") == 0)
			{
				empty2 = childNode.InnerText;
				curEqn.SetsEqName(empty2);
			}
			if (name.CompareTo("eEqName") == 0)
			{
				empty = childNode.InnerText;
				curEqn.SeteEqName(empty);
			}
			if (name.CompareTo("sComment") == 0)
			{
				empty3 = childNode.InnerText;
				curEqn.SetEqComment(empty3);
			}
			if (name.CompareTo("EqProperty") == 0)
			{
				foreach (XmlNode childNode2 in childNode.ChildNodes)
				{
					string name2 = childNode2.Name;
					if (name2.CompareTo("nPropNumber") == 0)
					{
						propNum = childNode2.InnerText;
					}
					if (name2.CompareTo("sEqSymbol") == 0)
					{
						eqSym = childNode2.InnerText;
					}
				}
				AddEqProp(propNum, eqSym, empty4, empty5, eqPropRangeMin, eqPropRangeMax, empty8, empty9);
			}
			if (name.CompareTo("EqVariable") == 0)
			{
				foreach (XmlNode childNode3 in childNode.ChildNodes)
				{
					string name3 = childNode3.Name;
					if (name3.CompareTo("nVarNumber") == 0)
					{
						varNum = childNode3.InnerText;
					}
					if (name3.CompareTo("sEqSymbol") == 0)
					{
						eqVarSym = childNode3.InnerText;
					}
					if (name3.CompareTo("nEqVarRangeMin") == 0)
					{
						empty12 = childNode3.InnerText;
						if (empty12 != null)
						{
							eqVarRangeMin = Convert.ToDouble(empty12);
						}
					}
					if (name3.CompareTo("nEqVarRangeMax") == 0)
					{
						empty13 = childNode3.InnerText;
						if (empty13 != null)
						{
							eqVarRangeMax = Convert.ToDouble(empty13);
						}
					}
				}
				AddEqVar(varNum, eqVarSym, empty10, empty11, eqVarRangeMin, eqVarRangeMax, empty14, empty15);
			}
			if (name.CompareTo("EqParameter") == 0)
			{
				foreach (XmlNode childNode4 in childNode.ChildNodes)
				{
					string name4 = childNode4.Name;
					if (name4.CompareTo("nEqParNumber") == 0)
					{
						eqParNum = childNode4.InnerText;
					}
					if (name4.CompareTo("sEqParSymbol") == 0)
					{
						eqParSym = childNode4.InnerText;
					}
					if (name4.CompareTo("nEqParIndex") == 0)
					{
						eqParInd = childNode4.InnerText;
					}
					if (name4.CompareTo("nEqParValue") == 0)
					{
						eqParVal = Convert.ToDouble(childNode4.InnerText);
					}
					if (name4.CompareTo("nEqParDigits") == 0)
					{
						eqParDig = Convert.ToInt32(childNode4.InnerText);
					}
				}
				AddEqParam(eqParNum, eqParSym, eqParInd, eqParVal, eqParDig);
			}
			if (name.CompareTo("EqConstant") == 0)
			{
				foreach (XmlNode childNode5 in childNode.ChildNodes)
				{
					string name5 = childNode5.Name;
					if (name5.CompareTo("sEqConstantSymbol") == 0)
					{
						eqContSym = childNode5.InnerText;
					}
					if (name5.CompareTo("nEqConstantIndex") == 0)
					{
						eqContInd = childNode5.InnerText;
					}
					if (name5.CompareTo("nEqConstantValue") == 0)
					{

						if (empty18 != null)
						{
							eqContVal = Convert.ToDouble(empty18);
						}
					}
					if (name5.CompareTo("nEqConstantDigits") == 0)
					{
						empty19 = childNode5.InnerText;
						if (empty19 != null)
						{
							eqContDig = Convert.ToInt32(empty19);
						}
					}
				}
				AddEqCont(eqContSym, eqContInd, eqContVal, eqContDig);
			}
			name.CompareTo("EqConstraint");
			if (name.CompareTo("Convariance") != 0)
			{
				continue;
			}
			foreach (XmlNode childNode6 in childNode.ChildNodes)
			{
				string name6 = childNode6.Name;
				if (name6.CompareTo("nEqParNumber1") == 0)

					{
						covVal = Convert.ToDouble(empty20);
					}
				}
			}
			AddEqCovar(eqParNum2, eqParNum3, covVal);
		}
	}

	private void SetPropertyMethodID(XmlNode node)
	{
		XmlNode xmlNode = node.SelectSingleNode("ePropName");
		foreach (XmlNode item in node)
		{
			switch (item.Name)
			{
			case "nPropNumber":
			{
				string innerText = item.InnerText;
				break;
			}
			case "Property-MethodID":
				SetPropertyMethodID(item);
				break;
			case "PropPhaseID":
			{
				string propPhase = GetPropPhase(item);
				break;
			}
			}
		}
	}

	private string GetPropPhase(XmlNode node)
	{
		string result = string.Empty;
		try
		{
			XmlNode xmlNode = node.SelectSingleNode("ePropPhase");
			if (xmlNode != null && !string.IsNullOrEmpty(xmlNode.InnerText))
			{
				result = xmlNode.InnerText;
			}
		}
		catch (Exception ex)
		{
			if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(m_Errors))
			{
				m_Errors = ex.Message;
			}
			else
			{
				m_Errors = m_Errors + "; " + ex.Message;
			}
		}
		return result;
	}

	private void AddCompound(string num, string name, string form, string iupac, string CASName, double molarMass)
	{
		xCompound xCompound2 = new xCompound();
		xCompound2.SetRegNum(num);
		xCompound2.SetCommonName(name);
		xCompound2.SetFormula(form);
		xCompound2.SetIUPACName(iupac);
		xCompound2.SetCASName(CASName);
		xCompound2.SetMolarMass(molarMass);
		curCompoundObj.SetCompound(xCompound2);
	}

	private void AddCom(string num, string name, string form, string ChemForm, string iupac, string CASName, double molarMass)
	{
		xComponent xComponent2 = new xComponent();
		xComponent2.SetRegNum(num);
		if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(name))
		{
			xComponent2.SetCommonName(name);
		}
		if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(name))
		{
			xComponent2.SetFormula(form);
		}
		if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(name))
		{
			xComponent2.SetChemFormula(ChemForm);
		}
		if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(name))
		{
			xComponent2.SetIUPACName(iupac);
		}
		if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(name))
		{
			xComponent2.SetCASName(CASName);
		}
		xComponent2.SetMolarMass(molarMass);
		curCom = xComponent2;
		curResult.SetCom(xComponent2);
	}

	private void AddVarValue(string varnum, double value)
	{
		string number = string.Empty;
		int len = -1;
		curResult.GetVarNum(ref len);
		for (int i = 0; i < len; i++)
		{
			curResult.GetVar(ref curVar, i);
			curVar.GetNum(ref number);
			if (number.CompareTo(varnum) == 0)
			{
				curVar.SetValue(value);
				break;
			}
		}
	}

	private void AddPropValue(string propnum, double value, int propDigits, int uncertNum, double dblUncertVal)
	{
		string number = string.Empty;
		int len = -1;
		curResult.GetComNum(ref len);
		if (len <= 0)
		{
			return;
		}
		for (int i = 0; i < len; i++)
		{
			curResult.GetPropertyInfo(ref curProp, i);
			curProp.GetNum(ref number);
			if (number.CompareTo(propnum) == 0)
			{
				curProp.SetValue(value);
				curProp.SetUnvalue(dblUncertVal);
				break;
			}
		}
	}
